# Architecture

How the app is put together. Rules and workflow are in [`../AGENTS.md`](../AGENTS.md); deferred ideas in [`ROADMAP.md`](ROADMAP.md).

## Overview

```
data.json (genus data + photo lists) ──┐
forms.json / quality.json / picks.json ├─> app.js (applyCuration + applySlots from core.js, applyForms) ─> pages
curation.json (Pierre's choices) ──────┘                                    │
                                                                            └─> progress in localStorage ─> Apps Script ─> Google Sheet
admin.html ──(GitHub Contents API, Pierre's token)──> curation.json on main ──> GitHub Action fetches approved photos
```

- Static site served by GitHub Pages from `main` via `pages.yml` (Actions source; the workflows and recovery in [PAGES.md](PAGES.md)). No framework, no bundler, no npm.
- A service worker (`sw.js`) caches the app shell for offline use (versioned cache `V` = `reefid-shell-vN`, replaced on each deploy; see "Names, storage keys and caches") and photos as they are viewed, in a separate cache `imr-photos-v1` that deploys never delete (capped at 2,500 entries, only good replies kept). Photos from R2 (see "Photos") go through it too: asked in CORS mode whatever the page asked (an `<img>` asks no-cors, whose opaque reply cannot be checked and costs ~7 MB of quota each in Chrome; the bucket allows the site), looked up under both addresses (R2 and the site's `img/`, so photos cached before the switch stay offline); when R2 fails (asked twice, 800 ms apart, then for 60 s not at all) the worker answers with the failure and the page picks the next source, the photo's online original or the site's copy (`photo()` in `sw.js`; it no longer serves the site's copy by itself, since the photos with an online original leave `img/`: "Photos"). The players' copy of the curation, `curation-play.json`, is part of the shell, so the first offline launch after a deploy still has Pierre's choices (Pierre, 2026-11-09: the deploy writes it from `curation.json` with `tools/curplay.py`, without what the app never reads: the coral auto-sort log `auto`, the fish judging `tags` and `ease`, the fish `auto` log but its undone adds; 1.33 million characters, 293 KB gzipped, down to 0.50 million and 102 KB; `curation.json` itself is served unchanged and the admin reads it whole; the app falls back to `curation.json` where the copy is missing, a local server where the tool was not run; the smokes write it through `tests/calm.js`; `tests/curplay.test.js` checks that the app builds the same games from both and that no player script reads a field the copy drops); the app also keeps the last curation it read in `localStorage` (`reefid-curation-last`) as a fallback. **New versions reach players without a manual reload** (Pierre, 2026-11-02): each deploy changes `sw.js` (`V`), which the browser checks at each launch and the app re-checks when it comes back into view (5 min apart) and every 30 min (`swCheck`); the new worker installs in the background and takes over (`skipWaiting`, `clients.claim`), and the page then reloads itself when it next comes into view or at the next page change (`UPDATED`, `route()`), never in the middle of a page. A page from before 2026-11-03 has none of this (Pierre: RAs who opened one of the very first versions kept it open for days, so their answers never reached the sheet): on activate, the new worker asks each open app page (`index.html`) its version (`reefid-ver?` over a `MessageChannel`, answered by `app.js`); a page that does not answer within 3 s is reloaded by the worker (`client.navigate`) onto the new version, once. Every request to the sheet carries the app's version (`av`, sheet v25), and the sheet logs one without it in Events as kind `old` (with the name or anonymous id it carries), so such devices can be found. **A page left on a replaced version** (Pierre, 2026-11-05: the sheet logged "app before 2026-11-03: photo stats" on 11-03 16:57, 20:59 and 11-05 10:27, Manila, with no device): that message is what any `?stats=1` read without `av` gets (at most once an hour), whoever sends it: the row of 11-05 was the integrator's own `curl` of the stats, and those of 11-03 were most likely tools too (stats saved for `tools/learnsim.js --stats`), not players' devices; the admin page opened without a token (`admin.html?readonly`) read them without `av` too. Every read the repository makes now carries one that is no build number (`admin.js` `av=admin`, `tools/brief.py` `av=brief`, `tools/learnsim.js --stats live` `av=learnsim`; by hand `curl "<SYNC_URL>?stats=1&av=tool"`), checked by `tests/statsav.test.js`. Now: the app's photo stats request carries `av`, its anonymous device id `dev` (the Device of its events, which the Pages tab ties to the player's answers) and `up`, the minutes its page has been open (never from a local copy); the sheet (v50, `Code.gs` `behind_`) keeps the newest build reported (Script Property `BUILD_TOP`, a build far ahead of it refused) and logs in Events, kind `old`, Device the id, App the version, a device on an older build when its page stayed open 30 min (`OLD_OPEN`) on a version replaced an hour (`OLD_H`) before, or when it starts a version replaced a day (`OLD_STUCK`) before; at most once per device every 6 hours. A page that no service worker controls (a browser without one, such as a chat app's built-in browser) hears of no new version until it is reloaded: left as it is, since no player's device was seen in that case (ROADMAP). **Never in the middle of a question**: back in view, the page reloads only when no question waits for its answer (`QOPEN`, set by `quiz.js` `question`, cleared by the answer and by `route()`); Next in a game is a page change (`route()`), so the new version comes between two questions. The install copies a versioned file (`?v=`) or a font that a cache already holds instead of downloading it again (most deploys change a few files; it downloaded the whole shell, about 1 MB, which the takeover waits for), and asks the other shell files (page, `curation.json`, manifest, icon) with `cache: 'no-cache'`: from the browser's HTTP cache (GitHub Pages: 10 min) a new version could hold the page of the deploy before and run its old code (`sw.js` `get`, `fixed`; `tests/swcache.test.js`; the question guard `tests/update.test.js`). The admin page does the same (`admin.js`, every click is already in the draft; not while the draft could not be saved). Both listen for the takeover (`controllerchange`) from their first line: the admin used to add its listener at the end of `start()`, after `data.json` and `cand.json`, and a version that took over meanwhile (a new worker waits for the old one's fetches still in flight, then takes over once the old one is idle) went unseen, so that tab kept the old code. The takeover can come later (Chromium, 2026-11-03, from timestamps of both workers and Chromium's `content/browser/service_worker`): a busy old worker is stopped the moment it is idle, and a request that comes while it stops (the page's next file or photo) starts the old version again, which is then not asked to stop early: the new version waits until the old one has been idle 30 s, or at most 5 min after its `skipWaiting()` (Chromium's "lame duck" timer; with DevTools attached, as in Playwright, only that). It happens when a new version finishes installing while the page still loads (a launch right after a deploy, photos held ~1 s by the R2 retry): the player gets the new version later, never not at all. A page opened in the very instant of that swap can hang (Chromium, 2026-11-05, from Chromium's own worker states in `tests/net.smoke.js` check 9): its navigation goes to the old worker just started again, which Chromium stops a few milliseconds later with the request still in flight, and the page never loads until it is reloaded. Rare for a player (a deploy, a version waiting and a page opened within those milliseconds) and out of the app's reach (no page code runs yet); check 9 opens its pages only once no version is waiting. `tests/net.smoke.js` check 9 publishes each version while its page is quiet. Before, a slow line served the old cached page after 2 s, so it took one or two manual reloads. The install fetches each shell file on its own, up to 3 tries (with `addAll`, one failed request on an unstable line failed the whole install). Versioned files (`?v=` in the URL) and `fonts/` are cache-first (a new version is a new URL), so a launch needs no network once cached. The other app files (page, `curation.json`, manifest) are revalidated with the server, but a file already cached is served after 2 s (`SLOW`) on a slow link while the network refreshes the cache in the background.
- Speed on poor connections and low-end phones (Pierre, 2026-11-01; measured with `node tools/perf.js`, network × CPU profiles, and `node tools/perf.js field`, the field link below; the progress sync's bytes and round trips per answer with `node tools/syncperf.js`, "Lighter saves on slow lines" below):
  - **Load order in tiers** (Pierre, 2026-11-02). A real RA's first visit from the IMR field station (HAR, Chrome) was served from Fastly's Manila POP (latency ~0.4 s, fine) but at ~86 kbit/s: at 0.5 s the page asked for everything at once (fonts, data.json, the 12 scripts, the Lab stylesheet and hub), each file got a sliver of the line and all came together at 20-25 s; then the service worker's install fetched the rest of the shell (Lab prototypes, fonts...) while the first quiz photo (960 px, 62 KB) came at 47 s. Now the files come one tier after the other, so what the player waits for never shares the line with the rest: **1 Home** (`app.css`, `proto/lab.css`, which also holds the layout of Home, My progress and the games' side columns (`.pgame`, `data-page=lab`), `home.css`, the main font, `core.js`, `app.js`, `sync.js`, `home-art.js`, `home.js`, `tour.js`, `data.json`, `curation.json`), **2 the photo quiz** (`model.js`, `tips.js` (the "Did you know?" tips' registry and rules, 3 KB), `quiz.js`, `learn.js`, which the quiz uses for its look-alike pairs and mix-up montages, `progress.js`, 13 KB, which each answer updates (achievements) and which opens My progress at once (Codex review #361: answers given before it lost their achievement bookkeeping, and a sign-in from the tour reached its record before it existed: the sign-in now waits for tier 2), with `forms.json`, `quality.json` and `picks.json`, which the draw and the photo slots use: the data is rebuilt with them, then the achievements are counted and the sheet's photo statistics asked for), **3 the photos of the next quiz questions** (planned at once, see Quiz below), **4 the sheet's reads** (sync, anonymous answers, leaderboard, achievement shares), **5 the rest** (`flag.js`, `proto/lab.js` with the Lab prototypes), then the service worker is registered: its install caches every other shell file (italic and condensed fonts, Lab...) for the next visit, which stays instant. Home's doors come before data.json and the curation (`app.js` `beforeData`, ROADMAP "Load order, further steps" (a), 2026-11-09): once tier 1's scripts have run (`BOOT`), the doors are drawn with the Learn door's band and the Identify door's photo dark (`home.js` `viewHome` without `DATA`; drawn at once, that photo shared the slow line with the data and put the band ~5 s later at 86 kbit/s), for a subject whose doors need none of its own files (`UNITS` `doorsFirst`: the corals; the fish Home waits for its families); once the data is in, the start-up's `route()` draws Home again with its band (`homeAgain`), keeping the scroll, the open ? help and the focus. A page tapped before the data shows the loading bar and asks for its tier at once; the welcome tour (not offered in ? before the data), the cards and the notices wait for the data as before, and `window.ROUTED` is set only by that full drawing. Home's own photos are lazy thumbnails. The tiers are listed in `index.html` (tier 1 as `<link rel=preload as=script data-tier=1>`, later ones as bare `<link data-tier=N href>`, which fetch nothing until the loader turns them into preloads); `loadTier(N)` in the inline loader starts tiers 2..N at once and resolves once their scripts have run, in order; `need(N)` in `app.js` adds each tier's set-up (`SETUP`) and `HAVE` is the last tier in. A page opened before its files arrived (`pageTier`: Home 1; quiz, duels, Learn pages, My progress, leaderboard and achievements 2; Tools and Coral ID Key 5) shows the loading bar and asks for its tier at once, ahead of the queue (`route()`); a link straight to a page asks for its tier at start-up, alongside Home's files. `window.APP_READY` is set once tier 2 is in (Home, the quiz and the Learn pages work); `window.ROUTED` holds the address of the last page `route()` drew (tests wait for it instead of a fixed time); `window.CARDS_SAID` (`progress.js`) reads true once start-up has settled its one-time cards (the notices that waited for the visit's first sync have run, `subjHello` is not waiting for its calm moment, no card waits to try again unless the card it waits behind is on screen), so a test that proves a card does not come waits for it rather than for a fixed time. Field link, phone, first visit (`tools/perf.js field`, one run each, so a few seconds of noise), before → after: Home shown 37.3 → 20.9 s (387 → 213 KB before it), first quiz photo when the player goes straight to the quiz from Home 69 → 38 s from the start, Next → photo for questions 2-5 22/24/7/25 → 10/12/0/0 s, My progress 37 → 28 s from the start (tap on Home), first quiz photo after 30 s on Home 29 → 6 s after the tap. On a desktop, Home also comes at 21 s instead of 37 s, and with Saver taking the 480 px file whatever the box the first quiz photo comes +18.6 s after the tap instead of +37.8 s, Next → photo for questions 2-5 4/5/0/9 s instead of 35/21/22/27 s. A photo-quiz question that changes what comes next (an exam question, a slipped-in duel, an "Again", cards) is answered before anything is planned past it (Codex review #361: an exam planned behind an unanswered exam broke its spacing).
  - Start-up never waits for `curation.json` when a kept copy exists (`CUR_KEPT`); `curRefresh` reads it right after, and a change applies at the next screen.
  - Start-up builds the fish units once, in the first `build()` (`fishLoad` leaves them to it while `DATA` is not built: they were built twice, the first time without the curation and thrown away). The games' units with a photo to show (`app.js` `pool()`, asked several times a question) come from `model.js` `photoAny`, which tells what `photoSet(n).shown.length` would without drawing (a draw on a big fish family costs a millisecond or more; `tests/photoany.test.js`). Performance pass, 2026-11-09.
  - Quiz: the next questions are chosen ahead, up to 3 in the photo quiz (`AHEAD`; one in the Duel tab), and their photos fetched meanwhile, so Next shows one at once (`planNext`, `NEXTQ` holding the next ones in `.next`, each keyed on the answers count it is for and the genera in play; Pierre, 2026-11-02: a fluent RA answers in ~8 s, less than one question's photos take at 86 kbit/s, and 95% of the answers are in the photo quiz). The queue is topped up when a question shows and after each answer. Planning ahead keeps the rules of the draw: a question with cards, an exam question, a duel slipped in or an "Again" ends the queue (nothing is chosen past it before it is played), and a wrong answer drops the queue, putting the planner's own state back (`dropPlans`: genus asked last, duel and "Again" spacing), so the "Again" still comes one question later. After a right answer the questions already chosen stay: their weights lag up to 2 answers, and a new card, exam question or duel can come up to 2 questions later than without planning ahead. The photos come in order of need, two at a time, question after question (cards of the genera met, then the photos shown small then full size, then hint and other views; `pump`, with `fetch()`, which unlike `new Image()` does not hold the page's load event): all at once they shared a slow line and the first photo came later than without preloading. The first questions are planned as soon as tier 2 is in.
  - Start-up on an unstable line: the JSON files are fetched again on failure, up to 3 tries (`getR`); each tier's scripts are preloaded (downloaded in parallel) and run in order by an inline loader in `index.html` (its own CSP hash), each failed one tried again up to 3 times; `window.BOOT` resolves once tier 1 has run, and app.js waits for it before the first page (`domReady`). A failed stylesheet is tried again the same way, and so are the Lab prototypes (`labLoad`). data.json is preloaded too (`as=fetch crossorigin`, same URL as the fetch, so it downloads alongside the scripts). Under "Loading…" a bar moves one step per script run and one for data.json (`bootTick`; it counts tier 1, or the tiers the first page needs: `bootMax`), and after 6 s a line says only the first visit is this slow (beginner RA review, 2026-11-02: 5-8 s of a bare "Loading…" at 400 kbps looked stuck). If data.json still fails, the page offers Try again. A first version reloaded the whole page on a failed file: on a line failing 1 request in 5, every reload failed again. Before, one failed request among ~10 start-up files left the app on Loading for good (`tools/perf.js`, 1 request in 5 failing, no service worker yet).
  - Pages: leaving a page drops its photos still loading (`route()`), so a slow line goes to the new page.
  - Photo sizes (Pierre, 2026-11-01): each photo exists in 1600, 960 (`-m`), 480 (`-s`) and 240 px (`-t`). A big photo (quiz, Look-alikes, sheet cover, lab tools) gets the smallest file that covers its box at the screen's density counted up to 2x (`fit`); the quiz guesses the box from the last question with as many photos (`QBOX`, so the preload fetches the right file), and a box bigger than guessed loads the bigger file (`upsizeImg`, `upsize`, never smaller). Cards and strips use `srcset` with the 480, 960 and 1600 px files (card `sizes` measured per S/M/L setting). Thumbnails (home band, progress tiles, look-alike pairs, tips) use `thumb()`: 240 or 480 px by drawn width and density (a home tile on a phone is ~60 px: 240 px, ~10 KB, instead of 480 px, ~30 KB). The home ReefCloud art has a 760 px version for phones. Leaving a page drops `srcset` with `src`, or the download goes on. The enlarged view loads the 1600 px file over the 480 px one. A photo not downloaded yet (`r: 1`: a candidate just added, and all of Millepora's game photos, `DATA.x.gen`) loads from iNaturalist with two files only (`img()`): `medium` (500 px) for the 480 px size, `large` (1024 px) for 960 and 1600 px; `resized()`, `upsizeImg` and the preload (`pfUrls`) leave it alone. A quiz photo carries its name in `data-f` (`quiz.js` `question`), so the failed-photo report (`ev('photo')`, admin "Photos not loaded") and the tests (`net.smoke.js`, `profiles.smoke.js`, `tools/perf.js`) name an iNaturalist photo by its `f`, not by its URL.
  - Photo quality setting (`reefid-photo-q`): Auto (default), Saver, Full. Saver takes the 480 px file for a big photo whatever its box (shown whole at its own size on a bigger desktop box, LOWRES; field link: at 1x a desktop quiz box still took the 960 px file, two per question), drops the 1600 px file from `srcset`, gives thumbnails their 240 px file and Home's ReefCloud art its 760 px one (field link: Home's photos on a desktop came to ~600 KB), and the enlarged view starts at 960 px (1600 on a click). Auto is Saver when the browser reports the data saver or a 2g/3g connection (Network Information API, Chrome on Android), or, once the app has measured the line itself, when it is under 500 kbit/s (Pierre: 480 px photos on a 1080p or 4K desktop are a real loss; the browser's estimate only counts before a measure; `linkMeasure`, `LINK`: the bytes the page got from the network over the time spent receiving them, Resource Timing, overlapping downloads counted once, cached files not at all, once 40 KB are in; kept in `reefid-link` for a launch served from the cache). Field HAR, 2026-11-02: at ~86 kbit/s Chrome's estimate did not flag the link, so Auto loaded the 960 px quiz photo (62 KB, 19 s); Safari and Firefox have no Network Information API. Saver never moves a shown photo to a bigger file (`upsizeImg`); only the enlarged view does, on a tap. Full always loads 1600 px for big photos.
  - Each game photo shows its 480 px version (`data-lo`, also the blurred backdrop) as the image's background until the bigger one is in.
  - A failing photo is retried twice (after 1 s, then 4 s), then replaced by its 480 px version. If that fails too, the frame shows the "photo missing" tile (`app.js` `noPhoto`, docs/DESIGN.md), the question's answers close, it offers another one, and nothing is counted. The whole chain for a site photo: R2 → R2 once more (800 ms) → its online original at a light size (or the site's `img/` for a photo with none) → 2 retries → the 480 px version (online `medium`, or R2's copy first under the service worker) → the tile. On a slow line (Saver) the files asked are already the 480 px ones (`fit`), so the online fallback is `medium` from the start.
  - The leaderboard and achievement shares are kept on the device (`reefid-board-cache`, one per subject: `reefid-fish-board-cache`; `reefid-achs-cache`): drawn at once, then refreshed. They are fetched with tier 4, after the quiz and its next photos.
  - Fonts are served from `fonts/` (latin subset, SIL OFL), not Google Fonts.
- `privacy.html`: the privacy notice (static, no script, own CSP), linked from the ? help of every page and from About and credits (`#about`); also the privacy policy link of the Google Cloud project behind the sync script's self-update. Keep it true when the data stored changes (new Sheet column, new external host).
- Link preview (Pierre, 2026-11-01): `index.html` carries Open Graph / Twitter meta tags pointing at `og.jpg` (1200 × 630, six of Pierre's own photos, corals and reef fish, + the app name), made by `node tools/og/make_og.js` from `tools/og/og.html` (coral photos from R2, fish photos from their iNaturalist original; `make_og.js b <out.jpg>` renders the other layout, `og.html#b`, while Pierre picks one). Absolute URLs (crawlers need them); WhatsApp caches a preview per URL, so an old share keeps its old card.
- App icon (C1, chosen by Pierre on 2026-11-06: a loupe, dark glass with a white rim and glint, a coral fish and a green brain coral inside, a straight handle): `icon.svg` is the "any" icon, a rounded square (favicon of every page, the header logo, the Lab's placeholder, `og.html`); `icon-192.png` and `icon-512.png` are the same as PNG; `icon-maskable-192.png` and `icon-maskable-512.png` are the full-bleed art (motif inside the 80 % safe circle) for Android's masks; `apple-touch-icon.png` (180 px, full bleed, opaque) is the iOS home-screen icon (`index.html`, `admin.html`). `manifest.webmanifest` lists the SVG and the four PNGs. Only `icon.svg` is in `sw.js` `SHELL` (the PNGs are read by the system when the app is installed: no download for players at each new version); the deploy's `V` hashes `icon.svg` and the manifest, so a new icon reaches installed apps with the next version.
- Everything runs in the browser. The only backend is a Google Apps Script web app that stores RA progress in a Google Sheet.

## Front end

### Pages and routing (`app.js`)
Hash routing in `route()`. Sections and their tabs are declared once in `MODES`.

**Addresses** (URL refonte, Pierre 2026-11-05, Q53-Q56; [study](reviews/2026-11-05-url-scheme-study.md) 3.2): `#<section>/<page>[/<thing>…][?<filters>]`, in the words on screen, lower case, joined by `-` (`#learn/genus/Acropora`, `#learn/look-alikes/A/B`, `#learn/compare/A/B`, `#practice/duels/A/B`, `#identify/key?form=B&box=B1`, `#tools/anatomy`, `#leaderboard/achievements`); Home is the clean URL (the logo's `href="./"`). **A subject other than the corals puts its prefix first** (`core.js` `SUBJECTS` `hash`: `#fish/practice/quiz`, `#fish/learn/families`, `#fish/progress`, `#fish/identify/key`), on the pages of the sections that follow the subject (`ROUTE_SUBJ`: Learn, Practice, Identify, My progress; the leaderboard, the Tools and About carry none); its units' words come from `SUBJECTS` `one` / `many`. Opening a `#fish/…` address brings Fish on screen (`setSubject`), or the corals' page for a player who may not open it (`subjOpen`); an address without a prefix shows the subject chosen (`reefid-sync-subject`) and is rewritten with its prefix in place; switching the subject rewrites the address (`setSubject`). Inside, the code names a page by its **page key** (`genera`, `g-Acropora`, `cmp|A|B`, `cmp-A|B`, `quiz-duel|A|B`, `idkey|form=B;box=B1`, `lab-anatomy|arr|x`: the vocabulary of `MODES`, `SUBJECTS` `pages` and the answer modes, which the sheet parses and which do not change); `core.js` `routeOut` (key → address) and `routeIn` (address → key; the filters split off the raw hash before each segment is decoded, so a separator inside a thing stays in it) translate, and nothing else: `app.js` `hashNow()` (the key on screen), `hr(k)` (a link; the first subject that has the page when the one on screen does not), `hrFull`, `go(k)`; `tests/routes.test.js` checks every key both ways and that no address is typed in the app's code. An old or unknown address opens its section's page when its first segment is a section (`#learn/…` the Genera list, `#practice/…` the Quiz, `#tools/<no tool>` the Tools page), else Home, the address replaced (old links are not translated, rule W3; `ROUTE_FIRST`). The page name logged and used for the photo sizes is the address's section and page (`core.js` `pageName`: `learn.genus`, `practice.duels`, `identify.key`, `tools.anatomy`, `fish.practice.quiz`).

| Section | Address | What |
|---|---|---|
| Home | (clean URL) | Two doors (`home.js`; in the Learn & practise door, under its buttons, a quiet line "New to corals? Start with the basics (5 min)" to `#learn/basics`, only while Coral basics is not done (`app.js` `basicsDone`) and the player has fewer than `CHOICE.ASK` (50) answers, Pierre 2026-11-03, and not for a player who answered the first-launch question "I had the IMR coral course" or "I know corals well", Pierre 2026-11-04; on phones a short link "New to corals? Basics in 5 min ›" in place of the door's "Learning phase" kicker and of the line, so Home still fits one screen at 360 × 640) and one credit line under them (`.hcred`: "By Pierre Pericard · for IMR · Credits", linking `#about`), all in one screen: the doors grow to fill the screen above the phone tab bar, on phones the Learn and Practice buttons (tab bar icon, label, one-line purpose) taking the height left past a capped photo band (at most 28% of the screen), Identify a compact row; Learn is the primary door, Identify the secondary one (DESIGN.md "Home hierarchy"; `home.css`, 2026-11-03). |
| (none) | `#about` | **About and credits** (`home.js` `viewAbout`, tier 1; Pierre, 2026-11-03): who made it, the IMR course content, the photos and their licences (each photo keeps its own credit), References (works cited by page or link: the Coral Finder book is cited there, never credited as a source of the content), privacy (links `privacy.html`) and Send feedback. The page bar names it with a Back button (`ctxBar`). Reached from the Home credit line and from the last line of the ? help on every page (`helpEnd()`: About & credits · Privacy · Send feedback). There is no page footer. |
| (none) | `#about/delete-account` (`/done` after) | **Delete my account** (`sync.js` `viewDeleteAccount`, tier 1; Pierre, 2026-11-05: a player deletes their own account and all their data themselves, from a discreet page): linked from About › Privacy and `privacy.html` only. Signed in: what is deleted in plain words, the account's code (checked on the device first, so a wrong one never reaches the sheet), then a second step ("Yes, delete everything" / Cancel); POST `{selfDelete, dname, dpin}` (sheet v58 `selfDelete_`: "delete everything", the Removed tombstone only, 3 tries per name and 30 in all per 6 hours); then `signOutClear(true)` (the device signed out and cleared, every report this account queued and the name typed for reports too) and `#delete-account|done`. Signed out: how to delete an account. Before sheet v58 (`SELFDEL_SHEET`): a note, no form. |
| Learn | `#learn/basics` | **Coral basics** (`proto/basics.js`, graduated from the Lab into Learn by Pierre on 2026-11-03): step 0 of the Learn path, the first Learn tab ("Basics" on phones); details under "Lab prototypes" below. |
| Learn | `#learn/genera` | Genera grid (lecture-group order by default, slate order toggle), Photos or ID tips view. The first block is step 0 of the Learn path, "0 · Coral basics" (`learn.js` `basicsCard`: in the photo's place, the seven steps as a trail of soft discs with line drawings, `basicsArt`, `BASICS_TRAIL`: one colour per step, `--bt1`…`--bt7`, seen = tinted with a ✓, next = deeper tint, ring and "Next: …" ("Start: Colony" while none is seen), not reached = desaturated on a dotted path, from `reefid-basics` `seen`, all seen once `basicsDone()`; only the next stop's word on a narrow card, a container query; "7 short steps · about 5 min", then "n of 7 steps seen", then "✓ Done"; it always opens step 1, `#learn/basics/colony`, Pierre 2026-11-04; in the slate order the first card; hidden while a search or family filter is on). `#learn/genus/<Genus>` = genus sheet (right under its photo, before the ID tips, the plain sentence, `plainLine()`); once the player has seen the genus, a line at the top gives their level on it and what it still needs (`myProg()`, same text as the My progress tooltip). |
| Leaderboard | `#leaderboard/achievements` | **Achievements**, the second tab of the 🏆 page (`boardTabs()`, as on Steam; Pierre, 2026-11-01). **Per subject** (2026-11-06): each subject shows and checks its own list (`SUBJECTS` `achs()`: the corals' `ACH`, the fish's `FISH_ACH`, 24 since 2026-11-08: families met, levels, Instructor, the circles at Advanced, the global fish score every 10 points, scientific genera at Open Water and Advanced; its ? text `UNITS` `achHelp`), all in the one synced record (`reefid-sync-ach`) under ids of their own (fish ids start with f), so a fish achievement never counts in the corals' and back; the Contributions are one family shown on both (`ACH_CONTRIB`); the record's feats (runs, corallites, hard photos) are kept by the corals' answers only (`SUBJECTS` `achFeats`: a fish answer neither extends nor breaks the run); `achFind(id)` finds one in any list (pop-ups, the admin's Journey). The corals' (Pierre, 2026-11-01: "unlocked as milestones are passed, with an animated pop-up", "well spread out"): 34 in `core.js` `ACH` (family icon, tier 1-3 shown 🥉🥈🥇, test on a snapshot), mostly the genera at each mastery level (`lvStep`: 5/10/20 genera at Open Water or above, 5/10/20 at Advanced, 5/10 at Divemaster; Instructor on 1/5/10/20/all; the ids of the old quarter rungs kept, 2026-11-03, so nothing earned is lost), genera met, overall progress 5-100%, runs of 10/25 right answers, first duel won, right with the corallites alone, right on a photo most players miss (`PSTAT`: ≤ half of 5+ answers right) shown alone (the runs and that photo, Sharp eye, only in the normal photo quiz, its Again, typed questions and slipped-in duels included, never in the custom quiz or the Duel tab: Pierre, 2026-11-05, Q82, "only in the normal Quiz"; an answer there neither extends nor breaks the run, `achAfter(good, ph, cur)`, `achRunIn`), Elo 1100-1400 (the device's global level, from `ELO_MIN` = 200 answers), an IMR group fully learned, a bonus genus unlocked, Coral basics done (every step seen, 2026-11-03: `proto/basics.js` calls `achBasics()`, which sets the feat `_basics`); never time or days. **Two families**: these learning achievements (`kind: 'learn'`) and, apart, the **Contributions** (Pierre, 2026-11-05, Q65: to encourage players to send feedback and show it is taken seriously; `kind: 'contrib'`, 💬, three more in `ACH`): *First feedback*, *Helping hand*, *Co-author* for the 1st, 5th and 10th report counted. A report counts as it is sent (`sync.js` `reportDialog` calls `progress.js` `achReport`, which calls `core.js` `contribAdd`), from anywhere (game, enlarged photo, genus sheet, Send feedback), signed in or not, whatever the subject on screen; one per photo (a report on a photo already in a counted report does not count again; a report on no photo counts once, by its id); an "Other" report only with a note; nothing on a device still signed in with an account the admin deleted (the sheet answered `gone`). The counted reports are the record's `_fb` (each one its photos joined by a space, or `r:<id>`; at most `CONTRIB_MAX` = 50), synced with the rest (`mergeAch` joins two records' lists by the same rule, `contribJoin`: one photo reported on two devices before they synced counts once); the snapshot's `fb` is its length. A report sent from Home or About before `progress.js` (tier 2) has come is counted once it comes (`need(2)`). Every Contributions tier shows at once (the other families: the first and the top tier only, below): the first as the toast, the 5th and 10th as a card that stays until closed (Pierre, 2026-11-05: a toast at the top may go unread; `progress.js` `achCard`, `cardShow` kind `ach` with the achievement's emoji, its button deaf for 600 ms and Enter not pressing it, `cardCalm`; one card at a time, `cardFree`). They never count in the learning: the badge's and `#ach`'s "earned / 34" count only `kind: 'learn'` (`progress.js` `ACH_L`), `#ach` lists the Contributions in a section of their own below (`ACH_C`, with the number of reports counted), and nothing in the progress or the leaderboard reads them. **"Your feedback helped"** (Pierre, 2026-11-05, Q66): when a report is **handled**, the player who sent it is thanked at their next visit. Handled = Pierre's Done in the admin (To do › Reports ✓ Done, `curation.json` `seen` `'rep:<id>'` once published) **or** a PR's: **`reports-handled.json`** at the repo root, `{"<report id>": {"pr": 577, "at": "2026-11-05"}}`, edited in PRs by the integrator when a PR fixed what a report said (agents never write `curation.json`); `core.js` `handledSeen` makes one map of both, read by the app (`progress.js` `handledLoad`: fetched only by a device whose record holds report ids, again at most every 30 minutes) and the admin (a report in the file leaves the to-do list and the Inbox count, and is listed folded under it, "Handled in a PR", each line "handled in #N" linking to the PR: `admin.js` `handledLoad`, `repsPR`). The thanks is a **card** that stays until closed (`cardShow` kind `feedback` with 🙌, `cls: 'helped'`: kicker "Thank you", *Your feedback helped*, "A report you sent was looked at and handled." or "N reports you sent were…"; one OK deaf for 600 ms, Enter not pressing it, `cardCalm`; Pierre: a toast at the top may go unread), shown **at a calm moment** (`progress.js` `achHelped`, `helpedCalm`): never over a question, a card, the tour, a toast or the report dialog (it waits for them, `helpedMo`); with a game on screen, at the break at Next when no other card took it (`helpedArm`, called at the end of `quiz.js` `answer`; closing it goes on to the next question). **Never again and again** (Pierre, 2026-11-05): at the first report handled, then every report handled since the last thanks in one card, at most once per 7 days (`core.js` `helpedDue`, `HELPED_GAP`; `helpedMark` on closing, the record's `_thxAt` the time of the last thanks, the latest of two devices in `mergeAch`); left unseen by leaving the page, it comes back. Checked at start-up (signed in: only once the sheet's load reply and its saved record are merged, `sync.js` `repsSeen`, `repsPulled`, so a thanks given on another device is never repeated), when a new `curation.json` arrives (`app.js` route), when `reports-handled.json` is read and when the sheet lists the player's reports. **Never for an account the admin deleted**: the sheet answers its device `gone` (no `reps`), and a device holding `SYNC.err === 'gone'` thanks nothing and counts no Contributions; `repsOf_` never returns a deleted account's reports, even when its name is taken again by someone else (only reports sent after the name's last removal, Removed tab). The record keeps the ids of the reports the player sent (`_rep`: stored as each is sent, `achReport`; and, signed in, from the sheet's load reply, `reps`, the ids of their own verified reports, the last 50, Code.gs v57 `repsOf_`, used only from `v` >= 57, `sync.js` `repsSeen`) and those already thanked (`_thx`), the last 100 and 200 (`core.js` `LIST_MAX`: more thanked kept than sent, and twice the sheet's 50 sent, so an id the sheet lists again never lost its thanks), synced and joined across devices like `_fb` (`core.js` `repsAdd`). An anonymous player's reports are matched on the device that sent them (the ids it kept). Ids only: nothing Pierre or the PR writes reaches the player. `#ach` adds "· N handled" to the Contributions' line. On 2026-11-01, with the mastery exam, every device recounts its record once (`achMigrate()`): each achievement is tested again on the current rules, those still met keep their date, the others are locked again, feats are kept; records carry `_v` = `ACH_V` and `achMerge` takes no achievement from an older record. Again on 2026-11-08 (`ACH_V` 3, the fish set around the global score, Pierre: "recalculer ... leur succès actuel"), every subject's list on its own progress (`inSubject`, `core.js` `achRecount`), silently (Q264); a subject whose data is not in yet (the fish, lazy-loaded) keeps its achievements aside on the device (`reefid-ach-old`, cleared at sign-out) until its data comes (`achPending`: from `app.js` `fishLoad` and before each check), so nothing untested travels. `achSnap()` builds the snapshot, `achAfter()` runs after each answer (run of right answers, one-off feats; `quiz.js` `answer` passes the game, `cur`), `achCheck()` stores new ones in `reefid-sync-ach` (`{id: time, _run, _best, _cor, _hard, _gone, _basics, _fb, _rep, _thx, _thxAt}`, a `reefid-sync` key: synced; `mergeSaved()` unions two devices' records with `achMerge` / `core.js` `mergeAch`). Achievements are only added. A recalculation that takes a learned mark away (`model.js` `mxRecalc`, docs/LEARNING.md "Learned under the former model") keeps them and tells nothing since 2026-11-09 (Pierre, ~15:40 Manila, Q332: every recalculation silent); until then the learned-based ones (`core.js` `ACH_LEARN`) the progress no longer met were taken back (`progress.js` `achRecheck`, `core.js` `achRevoke`, dated in `_gone` so the union never brings back a copy earned before) and the player told once by a card (`progress.js` `mxLostNotice`, `reefid-mx-lost`, each mark told also in the account's `reefid-sync-told`), code kept for if it comes back. `achCheck()` pops up only the first of each family (icons; 🌱🌿🪸 one family, 🎓👑 one, ⭐🌟 one: `achFam`) and the top tier (Pierre, 2026-11-02: a walkthrough counted 7-9 toasts in 60 answers); several at once make one toast (`achRecap`); the others land quietly in `reefid-ach-new` (this device only, outside the synced record, so a sign-in or another device's sync neither brings nor drops them; cleared at sign-out), with a red dot on 🏆 in the header and on 🏅 in the player's card (`achDot`), and a "New" tag on `#leaderboard/achievements`, which clears them (`achPop`, queued, 4 s, confetti off under reduced motion, never over a card or the tour). Descriptions say what the player can now do ("You told two look-alikes apart"; counts stay counts). A device with progress but no record (the feature arriving) gets what is already met in one recap pop-up. `#leaderboard/achievements` lists them, earned first (newest first) with their date, then the others by how many players hold them (the next within reach first), each with the share of signed-in players holding it (sheet v15 `GET ?achs=1`: `{n, counts}` from each player's saved `reefid-sync-ach`, counts only; `loadAchs()`, 5 minutes in memory). My progress shows the player's **badge** (`badge()`): initial, name, 🏆 rank and Elo (`#leaderboard`), 🏅 earned / 34 with the last three (`#leaderboard/achievements`). Simulated medians (75% / 90% right): 5 genera at 25% after 76 / 57 questions, 10 at 50% 231 / 137, 5 at 75% 363 / 203, first learned ~430 / ~300 (measured before the mastery exam: learned now also takes the exam). |
| Leaderboard | `#leaderboard` | 🏆 in the header on every page (`#boardgo`, Pierre 2026-11-01), first tab of the 🏆 page (Leaderboard / Achievements): signed-in players by progress %, then Elo, shown only from `ELO_MIN` = 200 answers (sheet v15; before, the badge says how many answers are left: `eloLine()`) (`eloScore()` = 1000 + 200 × their global level, the app's `P['~level'].th`: since sheet v63 `board_` reads it from each player's saved progress, with ELO_MIN counted on that record; one level shown everywhere, Pierre 2026-11-06, Q134; before, the sheet's own chain `stats_` `ras`), the player's row highlighted and showing this device's level (`eloLine()`, as My progress), from the sheet's `?board=1` (v14; `loadBoard()`, 1 minute in memory). **The admin's 🌱** (Pierre, 2026-11-09, Q328 corrected: "an icon the others are not allowed to have", added on its own, the account's name unchanged, no step for him): the account the admin page is signed in to when it reads the answer log with a valid admin token (`admin.js` `loadStats`, `aname` / `apin`; `Code.gs` `answers_`) is the admin's account, one at most, kept as its code hash in the script's properties (`ADMIN_ACCT`, `adminAcct_`; written under the script lock with its row read again there, `adminClaim_`, so a read under way never undoes a delete, merge or archive; another account signed in there replaces it; a delete, a merge (its from) and an archive clear it, `adminDrop_`, rule D7). Its rows on every subject's board carry a 4th field `1` (sheet v125), its load reply `admin: 1`; from a reply with `v` >= `MARK_SHEET` (`core.js` `markRow`, rule D4; the board cache keeps the reply's `v`) the app shows a plain-text 🌱 after the name's element, outside its text (`core.js` `markHtml`, `.bwho` > `.bname` + `.amark`, never cut by the name's ellipsis; title and label "Admin"), on the board and on the account line in ⚙ (`sync.js` `markSeen` from a real load reply only, never a light save's own copy; `markMine`, `reefid-amark` under the account's name, cleared at sign-out and by a load that finds no account under the name). **One leaderboard per subject** (Pierre, 2026-11-04: separate per subject, no merged ranking; `SUBJECTS` `board`): the page shows the subject on screen's (`boardSubj()`; the header's subject switch redraws it, the address stays `#leaderboard`), the corals' by coral %, the fish's by the fish global score (My progress's ring: `model.js` `headPct`, `core.js` `treeScore` over the subject's tree, `UNITS` `tree`; sheet v101: the app sends the tree with a save, `summary.boards {fish: {f, s}}`, and `board_` ranks by `treeScore` over it, an older app's list read as before), each then by that subject's Elo (its own `P['~level']`); another subject's comes from `?board=1&s=<id>` (sheet v95), shown only from a reply with `v` ≥ `BOARD_SHEET` (95; `boardOk()`, rule D4: an older script answers with the corals' board, never shown as the fish one), each subject's copy kept apart on the device (`subjDev(id, 'board-cache')`: `reefid-board-cache`, `reefid-fish-board-cache`). My progress shows the player's rank (from the board) and Elo (always the device's own, so it does not change when the board loads) under the ring (`#pbrank`), linking here. Signed-in players appear by default; ⚙ Leaderboard On/Off (`reefid-sync-board` = `{v, t}`, sent with each sync as `summary.board`; `mergeSaved()` takes another device's choice when it is more recent, so an opt-out holds on every device); players already signed in are told once (`boardNotice()`, `reefid-board-told`), on a subject that has the leaderboard (`SUBJECTS` `board`) only; a subject switch takes the note away and it is due again (`progress.js` `toastsOff`; fish beta QA, 2026-11-06). |
| Learn | `#learn/look-alikes` | Look-alikes: deck of ~20 classic pair montages (`classics()`, `montage()`); `#learn/compare/A/B` = pair comparison. The two photos come from `learn.js` `photoPair`, which the quiz's mix-up montage ("Your answer, same view", `quiz.js` `answer`) also uses: same growth form first (searched in every photo of both lists; `x` matches nothing), then nearest the front, then nearest zoom level; with no same-form pair, the best pair among the first 6 of each list. |
| Practice | `#practice/quiz` | **Quiz** (tab "Quiz"; "Photo → genus" until 2026-11-03, Pierre: typed answers and duels slipped in made it a general quiz), adaptive. Its page key and answer modes kept their names (`quiz`, answer modes `quiz\|…` that the sheet parses). |
| Practice | `#practice/custom`, `#practice/custom/play` | **Custom quiz** (Pierre, 2026-11-03; `quiz.js` `quizCustom`, `viewCustom`; tab "Custom quiz" at every width, "Custom" on phones before 2026-11-03): the Quiz on the genera the player picks. `#practice/custom` is the selection screen: every genus in play (the slate, and the bonus genera when on: `pool()`) as a small photo with its name, in three views (a switch, `reefid-custom-view2`, this device; the old key `reefid-custom-view`, which could hold A–Z, is dropped once): IMR groups by default (the lecture's groups, IMR order, All / None per group), My mix-ups (the player's own confusions in clusters, most confused first, with their counts: `model.js` `mixEdges`, `mixClusters`; the declared look-alikes, `lookEdges`, before any mix-up, with a line saying so) and Progress (To review, Learning, Not seen yet, Learned); never A–Z or by growth form, a search box, quick picks that replace the selection (My weakest, Recently missed, Look-alikes of …), Clear all, the count with the genera picked named under it, and Start (at least `CUSTOM.MIN` = 2). The selection is saved at each change in `reefid-sync-coral-custom` = `{g, t}` (a `reefid-sync` key: it travels with the progress, `sync.js` `mergeSaved` takes another device's when newer, sign-out clears it) and preselected next time. `#practice/custom/play` is the quiz (`CUSTOM_H`; without a valid selection it goes back to the selection screen): `quizPhoto(cur)` / `planPhoto(cur)` with the selection, the same question screen, logged `quiz\|s…`. Phone tab labels: "Custom", and "Duels" for Look-alike duels (`.wide` / `.narrow`), so the four Practice tabs fit 360 px. |
| Practice | `#practice/duels` | Look-alike duels. Its answers neither extend nor break the run of right answers and never earn Sharp eye (Pierre, 2026-11-05, Q82: runs and Sharp eye only in the normal Quiz; `progress.js` `achRunIn`). |
| Practice | `#practice/memory` | Memory (colony ↔ corallites), from `proto/pairs.js`. Its genera (`memoryGenera`) come from those the player has met (`introduced`) first, genera not met only to fill the grid (2026-11-03; `tests/pairs.test.js`). |
| Identify | `#identify/key` | Coral ID Key (`proto/idkey.js`): tolerant ranking by clues; state in the hash (`#identify/key?form=…&box=…&not=wall.plo`). Results page: one suggested question (two on tablet/desktop, `nextChecks()`) with big Yes / No answer buttons showing the count and photos of the genera on each side; Yes adds the clue, No adds a `not` clue (genera WITH the feature count one clue off, never hidden; removable chip "No: …"), Can't tell shows the next best question (not saved). |
| My progress | `#progress` | The player's card (`badge()`: overall ring with %, name, then on its own line the units unlocked, "30/40 genera unlocked" (`progress.js` `unlockText`: the subject's units opened on the path, `model.js` `introduced`, out of `slateGenera()`, in `SUBJECTS` `many`; idea of Pierre, 2026-11-05; the header pill has no room for it, one line at 1024 px, so only its tooltip says it), the two highest levels reached, leaderboard place and Elo (the device's own level, `eloLine()`; the board brings the place only), the Elo only once it exists, from `ELO_MIN` answers, with no countdown; achievements) first, above the board on phones/tablets, at the top of the side column on desktop; then the board (Slate by default, `reefid-sync-pview`), level counts, Review first, Often mixed up, and the Slate / Photos / Groups switch at the very bottom (Pierre, 2026-11-02). |
| Tools (Lab) | `#tools`, `#tools/<id>` | Prototypes (`proto/lab.js` hub). Hidden on phones except via the header hammer icon. |

The old addresses (`#g-…`, `#quiz`, `#key`, `#k-…`, `#l-…`, `#key-imr`, `#tips`, `#quiz-course`, `#credits`, `#lab-<graduated tool>`…) are no longer read (Q53-Q56): they open Home.

Key shared helpers (search for them in `app.js`):
- `build()`: `structuredClone(RAW)` then `applyCuration()`, `applyForms()`, `applySlots()` into `DATA` (also for the extra genera `x`).
- `applyCuration()` (in `core.js`): applies `curation.json` on top of `data.json` (cover, featured order, hide, move, add candidates, not-this-genus, exclude, crop, 2-level genera, book switch, `single`); it also drops the coral skeleton photos (`data.json` `skel`, rule P6).
- `applySlots(d, c, {picks, autoq})` (in `core.js`): photos per context into `g.sl[view]` (see "Photos per context" below); first 3 = featured. The admin mirrors it (`autoOrder`).
- `curRefresh()`: a tab left open re-reads `curation.json` when it comes back into view (if the last check is > 5 min old) and every 30 min while visible; a change is applied at the next screen (`route()`), never mid-game. Skipped with `?draft`.
- `pageHelp()`: moves intros into the "?" help box on every render (MutationObserver on `#view`). The help box always ends with `helpEnd()` (About & credits · Privacy · Send feedback), so the ? shows on every page, even one without explanations (`resetHelp()`).
- `shownGroup()`: places starred genera in the lecture group of their look-alikes (display only; `core.js` `lectureGroup`). `starNote()` names a starred genus's references on its card ("★ look-alike of …": `core.js` `starRefs`, the surveyed look-alikes in that group); the opening of genera reads the same references (`starWaits`, `model.js` `weighted`: a ★ genus opens only once they are met; docs/LEARNING.md "Starred genera after their reference").
- `GX(n)`: genus lookup in both IMR genera (`DATA.gen`) and the 27 extra Dauin genera (`DATA.x.gen`: Coral ID Key, their sheets, Millepora and the bonus genera in games).
- **Game genera, defined once** (`core.js`, Pierre 2026-11-03: no genus-specific case in code): `BENTHIC` = `['Millepora']`, `BONUS` (the 5 bonus genera), `slateOf(d)` (the slate) and `gameOf(d)` (the slate + the bonus genera: every genus a game can ask), for the app, the admin (whose `DATA.gen` also holds the `x` genera: a genus of `d.x.gen` is never a slate genus by its key in `d.gen`) and the tools (`tools/genera.py` reads the two lists and `CAP.sheet` from `core.js`: `game_genera`, `key_only`, `cap_sheet`). The other genera of `data.json` `x` are **Key-only**: Identify > Coral ID Key and their sheet, never a game. `tests/genera.test.js` checks the JS and Python sides agree.
- `slateGenera()` (`app.js`, = `slateOf(DATA)`): the genera the games teach and the progress counts, "the slate" in the code: the 39 of the IMR coral slate (`DATA.gen`, data.json order) and `BENTHIC` = `['Millepora']` (Pierre, 2026-11-02: fire coral, a hydrozoan, so not on the IMR coral list, but on the IMR benthic slate, taught in the benthos lecture, and everywhere at Dauin and in ReefCloud photos). Used by the draw (`pool()`), the opening of genera (`introLeft`, the open-all offer), `progPct`, the header pill, My progress, the sync summary (the sheet's "By genus" columns and the leaderboard %), achievements and `slateIdx`. Millepora's data stays in `DATA.x.gen` (Coral Finder ID tips with pages, Research Grade game photos); `isBenthic(n)`. Its first-meeting card and its genus sheet carry `BENTHIC_NOTE` ("Not a hard coral and not on the IMR coral list, but it's on the IMR benthic slate…"); the Slate view of My progress marks it **F** (after the starred genera), the Groups view gives it its own **F** row. The Genera page and the Lab tools still list `DATA.gen` only (Millepora stays in the folded extra-genera line there).
- `MQ`, `isPhone()`, `isStacked()`, `tabH()`: the 3 size classes and phone tab bar height.
- `fitStage()`, `fitTiles()`: size games to one screen. On phone/tablet, with one row of photos, `fitStage` gives the quiz stage all the height the question and answers leave, sized before the photos load (no layout shift); a photo close to the box shape fills it (class `.cov`, object-fit cover), others are shown whole. On desktop it picks the grid of views from the photos' shapes (`data-r` from data.json `ar`, written by `tools/apply_ratios.py`, also run by the approved-photos Action; 4:3 when missing). `flag.js` (admin ⚑) hides its button when its photo leaves the page and follows it on resize. `fitTiles` honours `data-minw` (the phone progress wall: names stay readable, the wall may scroll).
- `IS_ADMIN`: true in Pierre's admin browser (token vault present); gates Pierre-only controls. ⚙ has a View: Admin | User switch.

### Settings (⚙, `index.html` + `app.js`)
no Subject row (the subject is switched on Home and in the header, see "Subjects"); the rows of one subject only carry `data-for` (Bonus genera for corals; Scientific names for fish: every opt-in row is `[data-optin=<k>]` with `data-on` buttons, `showBon`; the fish "SVS video extras" and "Beyond IMR" rows were removed when every circle joined the path, Pierre, 2026-11-06, Q175) ·
Leaderboard On/Off (`reefid-sync-board`, synced with the progress, default On: name, progress and Elo on `#leaderboard`; Off is sent with the next sync) · Bonus genera On/Off (`#bonset`, `Q.bon` in `reefid-sync-coral-quiz`, see Bonus genera; off until the player accepts the offer) · Theme Light | Dark segmented switch (`reefid-sync-theme`, light default) · Colour-blind On/Off (`reefid-sync-cvd` → `:root[data-cvd]`, blue/orange instead of green/red; the right answer is also filled and the wrong one dashed, since dark blue is close to the accent) · Colours: Lagoon / Ocean / Seagrass / Twilight / Depth, swatches named under each (`reefid-sync-palette` → `:root[data-palette]`, **Depth default**; the old `reef` value falls back to Depth) · View Admin | User (only in the browser holding the admin token) · Install the app (`#instrow`, hidden in the installed app; see "Install the app" below). A small inline script in `index.html` applies theme, palette and cvd before first paint. The Coral ID Key has its own switch for the 27 extra Dauin genera (`reefid-idkey-x`, on by default). Photo size S/M/L sits on each page. The "Photos from Dauin / PH / All" setting was removed in PR #230 (don't bring it back).

### Learning model (`model.js`, split out of `app.js` 2026-11-02)
- Per-genus state in `localStorage` `reefid-sync-coral-progress` (`P[genus]`: `ok`, `ko`, `h` recent results string (last 10), `pg` progress (moving average, `core.js` `PROG`; `L`, BKT knowledge, in progress saved before 2026-11-02), `th` ability, `ps` photos seen `{f: [seen, right]}`, `ro` recent colonies, `cor`/`du` milestones, and for the comeback after a break `at` last answer (ms; `P['~level'].at` the player's last answer, and `P['~level'].h` the recent results over every game, which `model.js` `openRight` reads: an opening only after a right answer), `dd` days answered right, `dl` the last of them (local day number); `core.js` `mergeProgress` keeps the latest `at`/`dl` and the larger `dd`).
- **Comeback after a break** (2026-11-02, replaced the gentle restart `WARM`; docs/LEARNING.md): `model.js` `rustNow` (run when a question is planned or answered) starts a new visit after `SESSION.GAP` = 30 min without an answer (the next `COMEBACK.FLOOR` = 10 answers open no genus: `noOpen()` blocks cards in `planPhoto`, the open-all and bonus genera offers in `quizPhoto`; bonus genera, once on, open by cards like slate genera), and from the FSRS recall per genus (`recallAt`, `stability`) adds the gap's rust (`rustOf`); a comeback runs while it is above 0 (`comebackOf`: genera answered right, the surest half first, then the most rusted; the usual choices (`trapsFor`), photos at ability + ln R via `photoSet(n, skip, shift)`, no card/duel/exam; `q.warm`, `q.cb` = the genus's recall) and ends by a CUSUM on its answers (`comebackStep`). State in `reefid-comeback` {r, t, ok, f} (device only, cleared with the progress). Its answers are logged `|c` with the recall-adjusted `e` and count in the opening hold.
- **First-meeting cards, Open all genera and back to one at a time** (`model.js` `INTRO`, `introAdd`, `introduced`, `introReady`, `introAll`, `backDue`, `introBack`, `introMerge`; `quiz.js` `introShow`, `backShow`; docs/LEARNING.md "First meeting with a genus"): `reefid-sync-coral-intro` maps a genus to [answers when shown, 1 when ready at once, time, kind: `s` first cards, `c` a card of its own, `a` Open all, `b` closed again]; `reefid-sync-coral-intro-offer` is {at, open, via, t, n, h, bk, bat, bn, bt}: Open all (from the first cards or My progress; `via` 'offer' and `open` 0 only in records from before 2026-11-12, when a card offered it: Pierre, Q337), then `h`, the opening hold's CUSUM since Open all (`holdStep`), and the back card's choice. The back card (2026-11-03, Pierre) is due once after Open all when `h` reaches 2 × `INTRO.HOLD`, as a prompt (`promptOk`, `noOpen`); "One at a time" marks every `a` genus `b` (out of the draw, the choices, duels and "Again" until its card opens it again; answers and progress kept), "Keep them all" changes nothing. Both keys are `reefid-sync*` (synced; `introMerge`: per genus the later of a `b` mark and an opening wins, the record by its latest decision `t`/`bt`); the admin's player journey shows the choice (`admin-journey.js`).
- **Learning model**: research and choices are in [LEARNING.md](LEARNING.md) (2026-11-02: three player paths without coded tracks; progressive opening of genera `OPEN`/`genusDiff`/`shaky`, one rule since 2026-11-03 (`openChance`: a card with the chance 1 − shaky / `OPEN.K` when the path allows one, a unit still shaky after `OPEN.STUCK` 10 of its own answers taking no place once the player's answers so far, `answersSoFar`, are `OPEN.STUCK_FROM` 200 or more, since 2026-11-12, Q338; the genus by `openPick`), its pace since 2026-11-04 (`openPace`: no card while a genus carded in the last `OPEN.WIN` 15 answers waits for its question, at most `OPEN.MAX` 3 carded in any 15, a carded genus asked as soon as it is ready; it replaced the hotfix #421's cap of the chance at 0.5 and its `OPEN.UNASKED`), an opening only after a right answer (`openRight`, 2026-11-03: no card while the last graded answer in any game was a miss; a card planned before the miss is planned again), easier questions while the opening hold is on (`holdEase`: the photos and the format aim `h − INTRO.HOLD` logits lower; every question but an exam, the Duel tab's too since 2026-11-04, `quiz.js` `planDuel`), the player's own mix-ups in the expectation (`confTerm`, `core.js` `confGap`, questions with 3 or more plausible choices), look-alikes from the data (`core.js` `dataLook`, `model.js` `dataLooks`: `CONF.N` 20 offers and a pooled score of `CONF.TRAP`; duels, Again, genus sheets, admin Confusions), common first (`commonFactor` in `core.js`, `data.json` `cm`, 2026-11-03), volatility in `eloStep` `ELO.vr`/`vg`, pooled traps for experts `pooledConf`, one contrast queue for the Again and the duels slipped into the quiz (`quiz.js` `CONTRAST`, 2026-11-03; up to `CONTRAST.Q` 3 pairs since 2026-11-04, `rematchAdd`, oldest first, an Again never asking a genus not met), met genera first as easy wrong choices; the progress bar as a moving average weighed by what each answer proves, `PROG`, `pgWeight`, replacing BKT; simulated by `tests/profiles.smoke.js`, learning outcomes over many seeds by `tools/learnsim.js`). The paragraphs below describe the code.
- **Learned** = the **silent mastery exam** passed (`s.mx`; Pierre, 2026-11-01: "an ultimate test from 95% to 100%"; the former two milestones, right once with the corallites in view and once in a duel, were dropped as easy and arbitrary). The progress `s.pg` (a moving average of the answers weighed by what each proves: `core.js` `PROG`, `pgStep`, `pgOf`; `model.js` `pgWeight`, `knownP(n)`; scored by `core.js` `scoreAnswer`, the replay's own scorer, docs/LEARNING.md "One scorer"; BKT until 2026-11-02) fills a genus up to 95%; once `knownP` ≥ `PROG.M` = 0.8 (`examOn`), `EXAM.n` = 5 questions on it are slipped unannounced into the photo quiz (`quizPhoto` → `examSet`), at least `EXAM.gap` = 8 answers apart (`examReady`, counted in answers, never time; `weight()` raises a genus ready for its next exam question to 3 and holds it back in between): one photo alone, never answered right before (else the least seen), the hardest of its view, the views taking turns (`s.ex = {k, v, at, ts}`, `ts` the time it began, which places it among the logged answers for the full replay), 3 look-alike traps. All right: `s.mx = 1` and a pop-up; mastery is kept while the genus stays known (two misses before it is back up, for the genus shown or the one answered, take it away and bring the exam back). Exam photos are never-shown ones first. The exam's count is shown nowhere (Pierre: only the genus's %, which creeps up from 95% and falls back on a miss, keeps the mystery). Any miss on the genus (exam or not) and any time it is given as the answer for another genus drops `s.ex`; it starts again when `pg` is back at the entry. `mergeProgress` takes `mx` and `ex` from the device that played, like `pg`. A mark passed under BKT (a record without `pg`) is dropped by `core.js` `memRecalc` and the genus takes the exam again; the player is told once by a card and the learned-based achievements no longer met are taken back (Pierre, 2026-11-03; docs/LEARNING.md "Learned under the former model"). `tests/draw.smoke.js` plays an exam through the real quiz. A right answer weighs (1 − 1/k) / (3/4) with k plausible choices, halved when none of its look-alikes is among the choices; a miss weighs the Elo chance of a right answer over the ~80% the photos aim for, at most 1 (`pgMissW`: in full at the player's level, less on a harder photo); a learned genus survives one miss (`mxKept`) and loses its mastery on a second one before it is back up. Progress saved before `pg` is recomputed from its last 10 results (`pgOf`, the same in the sheet's `pctOf_`, v22). **Review** = shaky: the player's expected success on an average photo of the genus under `OPEN.P` 0.6 (since 2026-11-03, the quantity the genus draw weighs, `drawW`; was 2+ misses in the last 5 results). **No time between sessions anywhere** (rule L1; the seconds an answer took weigh only a right answer's ability step, "Answer time and enlargement").
- A wrong answer is a failed observation for the genus shown **and** the genus given (rule).
- **Typed questions** (free-text answers, Pierre 2026-11-03; `model.js` `TYPED`, `typedP`, `examTyped`; `quiz.js` `typeForm`, `typeBind`, `typeRead`, `typeNames`, `typeMatch`, `TYPEDQ`; `core.js` `TYPE`, `typeDist`, `typeNorm`, `typeNamesOf`, `typeMatchIn`, `typedShare`, `TYPED_RE`): in the photo quiz (`planPhoto`), a question on genus g is asked typed (a field, Check, I don't know; no suggestions, autocorrect and capitals off, no autofocus on touch screens, `enterkeyhint`) with the chance `typedShare(genScore(g), TYPED)`: none below `TYPED.from`, then a straight line between the points `TYPED.at` ([progress, share]), flat after the last; never a comeback question, an Again or a duel. **Every tunable number is in `TYPED`** (`from`, `at`, `exam`, and `k` = Infinity, no guess rate); its values are placeholders from the learnsim study (2026-11-03) and may be retuned. The answer is matched with light spelling tolerance (1 edit up to 6 letters, 2 beyond; a swap of two letters is one) against every genus the app knows and their old names ("formerly X" in their own lines); a word that is no genus, or as close to two, is no answer (the player is asked to check the spelling). **On a genus question below the subject's rank** (a fish scientific genus; Pierre, 2026-11-08, Q237: the spelling message misled a player who typed a common or family name), a text that reads as another rank's name or a common name is told so instead (`core.js` `typeElse`, `quiz.js` `typeKind`, `typeKindMsg`, `app.js` `UNITS` fish `typeOther`: the scientific genus names of fish.json, never one, and the genera's, IMR tags' and species' common names): "“Anemonefish” is a common name: this question needs a genus's scientific name." / "“Damselfishes” is a family: this question needs a genus's scientific name.", the same whatever the photo's genus, never a scientific name (`tests/typedrej.test.js`, `tests/fish.smoke.js`). **Fish names** (Pierre, 2026-11-09, Q319; FISH_PATH "Typed family names"): every name also in the other number (`core.js` `typeForms`), the names of the name index (`fishNameIndex`, passed by `app.js` `UNITS` fish `names`; "The name index") read as the family they lift to, a name turned off as `{known}` (no unit, a line saying so; at a genus question the common-name nudge), a name several units share typed exactly as `{shared}` (`quiz.js` `typeUnit`: right on any of them, else no unit given, so it is scored as a miss on the photo's unit only, its text logged in `ty`; `typeRead` says what else it names); the closest names decide, so a name typed exactly is never a slip of another. **Every text refused** (no unit, two as close, the nudge) goes with the answer finally given, in order (`typeBind` `rj`, `TYPEDQ_RJ` = 10, each cut to 40 characters; logged `rj`, sheet v100 Rejected tries; Pierre, 2026-11-08, Q237: "c'est essentiel. Et c'est vrai pour tout le jeu, partout"). **The spelling step** (a player's idea, Pierre 2026-11-05: "Good idea!"; `core.js` `typeVerdict`, `typeDiff`; `quiz.js` `typeBind` with `o.t`, `typeSpell`, `typeStep`): a name of the genus asked typed within the tolerance but not exactly (`'close'`) is still a right answer, graded as before, but is no answer yet: a warm nudge under the buttons, "✏️ So close! Right genus: just fix the spelling and try once more" (a small card in the warm `--warn` kind with its emoji medallion, `.tyform.retry .tymsg`, `.tymed`), the field keeps the text selected and the focus (warm `--warn` edge), I don't know becomes Show me; one retype, whatever it reads as. The verdict stays one line (`fbBody` `step`): exact, the plain "✓ Yes, it's X"; still wrong or skipped, "✓ Yes, but not quite: it's spelled X" (`h3.tystep`: the words in normal weight, the name in bold), X the same link to the genus sheet with the letters the last try got wrong marked (`.tysp b`: the wrong colour and its underline; the field `.close`). The seconds logged are read when the retype is asked (the recognition's), and the hint button hides meanwhile. A close typo of another genus is a wrong answer as before (`'other'`); the mastery exam's typed questions and Tools > Type the genus take the same step. Logged with the answer: `ty` (the raw text first typed, every typed answer) and `tr` (the step's outcome), Answers columns Typed and Retyped right (sheet v59). Scoring (`answer`): k infinite, so the Elo expectation has no 1/k floor (ability, global level, logged `e`, opening hold), a right answer counts in full in the progress (`pgWeight`: every genus, look-alikes included, was a possible answer), a miss weighs what it proves (`pgMissW`); a genus typed by mistake counts against both genera as a wrong pick and as a mix-up (`s.conf`), "I don't know" is a miss for the genus shown only; no pair counts as offered. Logged `quiz|t` (`quiz|t|x` in the exam). The **mastery exam** needs `TYPED.exam` typed right answers among its `EXAM.n` (`s.ex.t`; `examTyped`: typed while the typed answers still needed fill the questions left, else with the genus's usual share, so the format gives nothing away); an exam begun before this needs its typed answers first (its count held at `EXAM.n`). The Lab prototype `proto/type.js` (Tools > Type the genus) uses the same code. Admin player journey: a "Typed (recall)" row under Games.
- **Similar names after a mix-up** (the name study's question b, [docs/reviews/2026-11-05-name-confusion-study.md](reviews/2026-11-05-name-confusion-study.md); Pierre, 2026-11-14, Q124; `quiz.js` `namesAlike`, `nameDice`, `namesDiff`, `namesLine`, in `fbBody`; `app.js` `unameIn`): after any mix-up of two units (any format: 4 choices, a duel, an Again, a typed name read as another unit; corals and fish alike, on the names as shown), when the two names share their first letter or have a bigram Dice of at least 0.4 (the study's measure: lower case, `#` at each end, bigrams with repeats), the feedback's first line under the tips reads "Similar names: X / Y", the photo's unit first, then the answer, each set as `uname` sets it, with the letters between their common beginning and common ending in bold (letters only, never a space or a sign; `.fb .ndiff b` in the text colour). Unit tests `tests/namesdiff.test.js`; smoke part `names` (`tests/pages.smoke.js` `namesCheck`).
- **Custom quiz** (Pierre, 2026-11-03; `model.js` `CUSTOM`, `customOf`, `customDraw`, `customChoices`; `quiz.js` `CUSTOM_H`, `photoQuiz`, `modeBase`, `cbIn`; docs/LEARNING.md "Custom quiz"): `planPhoto('quiz-custom|play')` draws the genus among the selection with the usual `weight()` and no opening path (no first cards, no opening chance (`openChance`), no hold, no visit floor; a genus not met yet is asked with its card, `introShow(L, true, 'c', false)`: ready at once, no Open all box), and the wrong choices from the selection first (`distractors` on it), topped up from the other genera in play only under 4 genera. Again (its selected genus), duels slipped in (pairs of the selection), typed questions, calibration and the comeback (only with 2+ selected genera answered right, else left to the photo quiz: `cbIn`) work as in the photo quiz; the offers about genera in play (open all, back card, bonus genera) are not made there. No mastery exam question there (rule L9: the selection may hold no look-alike; Codex review #411), and a question is scored with the look-alikes its choices really hold (fewer than 2: `plausibleK`, as the photo quiz scores easy choices). Planned questions are keyed on the selection too (`planPool`). Every answer is a photo-quiz answer (progress, Elo, hold, achievements but the runs and Sharp eye, which only the normal Quiz earns, the sheet), logged `quiz|s` first (`quiz|s|d|k2`, `quiz|s|k3`, `quiz|s|t`), which sheet v27 already stores and rates as a photo-quiz answer (`guess_` reads `|kN` at the end, `|t` anywhere; mode split `quiz`). Admin: Activity's Recent sessions count it as **custom**, the player journey has a "Custom quiz (genera picked)" row under Games and the sessions with it in "When they played".
- **Progress scale**: `genScore` (`core.js` `scoreOf`) = 0.95 × min(1, `pg` / `PROG.M`), + 1% per exam question passed, 1 once mastered; `lvStep` = 6 steps, the **mastery levels** (`model.js` `MASTERY`, `lvIcon`; Pierre, 2026-11-03: "0/39 learned" for weeks was discouraging; a diver's ladder every RA knows, one icon each): 0 not seen, 1 Snorkeler (under 50%), 2 Open Water (from 50%), 3 Advanced (from 80%), 4 Divemaster (from 95%, the exam's entry), 5 Instructor (exam passed, `s.mx`); the thresholds `LV_AT` only name points on the %, the game never reads them; coloured by the palette's learning-level ramp; the legend shows each level's icon and count, the player's card the two highest levels reached (`lvSummary`: "2 Instructor · 3 Advanced"), the genus sheet its level and icon; tiles on My progress (Photos / Slate / Groups views) show a mini ring + %; review = a small amber dot on a surface ring (`--review`; the red border and "!" until 2026-11-03, beginner review: they read as an alarm). `progPct` drives the header ring.
- **Which genus next**: `weight()` favours unseen (10), review and unsure genera, learned 0.25, and holds back the last 4 genera asked. Bonus genera weigh like any other since 2026-11-02 (their `BONUS_W` cap and `BONUS_SHARE` limit went with the opt-in: see Bonus genera).
- **Continuous difficulty** (Elo as in Pelánek 2016; PRs #197, #200): per-genus ability `P[n].th` on the photo-difficulty scale, updated in `core.js` `scoreAnswer` (`eloStep` with `ELO`; `quiz.js` `answer` and the sheet's full replay both score through it since 2026-11-06, docs/LEARNING.md "One scorer") with P(right) = g + (1-g)·sigmoid(ability − difficulty), g = 1/choices, K shrinking with answers but ≥ 0.25 (`ELO`). A wrong answer moves the Elo of the genus shown only: the genus wrongly given counts in its progress, recent results and runs (rule L2) but gets no Elo step (2026-11-03; it used to take a full miss at difficulty 0, which under-rated strong players; docs/LEARNING.md "The genus wrongly given in the Elo", `tools/abilityeval.js` replays the real answer log under such rules). A genus already answered also moves with the global level since its own last step (`core.js` `levelMove`: the level's move since `s.lt`, the level then, saved and merged with `th`, over 1 + its answers / `ELO.n`; both ways; the level's own step has no `kmin` floor), 2026-11-03, docs/LEARNING.md "Global level and genus". The success the photos aim for follows the global level (`aimP`, `core.js` `aimOf` and `AIM`: 85% up to 0.5, 75% from 1.5), and so does the shaky bar of the opening (OPEN.P + aimed − `PICKD.P`), 2026-11-03, docs/LEARNING.md "Beginners and experts". **Global level** (Pierre, 2026-11-01: strong beta testers must not wait ~100 questions for traps): the same Elo step runs over every answer into the pseudo-genus `P['~level']` (`LEVEL` in `core.js`; ok, ko, th like a genus, so it syncs and merges like one, and appears in the sheet's per-genus abilities under that key; since 2026-11-04 also `rt`, the player's answer times, "Answer time and enlargement" below). A genus never answered starts from `playerStart(P, n)` (`core.js`): 0.8 × global ability × M/(M+10) (M = its answers), clamped to [−0.5, 1.5] (`START`); progress saved before falls back on the answer-weighted mean of the genera's abilities, and the record is made at the next answer from that history (`levelSeed()`: all answers so far, that mean), so an existing player's earlier answers keep counting. Taken once: the first answer saves the genus's own `th`. Simulated (real `photoSet`/`distractors`, all right): traps over the first 25 questions 1.9 on average instead of 0.1, 2.8 over questions 26-50 instead of 0.5; a player at 50% stays at 0 traps, starting new genera at −0.5. Progress (`pg`) is not carried over: each genus is still proven by its own answers. `photoSet()` draws photos weighted around the target `ability − aimOff(k)` (the question right with the chance `PICKD.P` = 0.8 in its format: 1.01 below with 4 plausible choices, 0.41 with 2, 1.39 typed; one difficulty controller, 2026-11-03; from sheet v68 less the format's offset, `aimOff(k, df)`, which the ability steps' expectation takes too, `scoreAnswer` `df`: `model.js` `PFMT`, read through `pfmt()`, which gives a subject the sheet does not rate (fish) no offset, every format 0, never the corals' fitted ones, 2026-11-09; docs/LEARNING.md "Per-format offsets"), with a bonus for never-seen photos (IMR and Pierre's first), photos answered right twice down-weighted, the last colonies held back and ≥ 3 colonies in rotation (`VARIETY`). **Answer choices** (Pierre, 2026-11-01) follow the same ability: `trapsFor(t)` = how many of the 3 wrong choices are look-alikes of the target (`look` both ways), 0 on a genus's first question (ability 0), then +1 per 0.18 (`FORMAT`, `formatAt`), randomly rounded in between, all 3 from 0.54: one look-alike after the first right answer, all three after about 5, because they are how genera are learned (Pierre, 2026-11-01). While traps are 0-1 the other wrong choices are genera sharing no IMR growth form with the target (`imrFormsOf`), other lecture groups first and each in a different growth form (so the answer is not the odd one out); from 2 traps the last choice is family first, then any genus (the old rule). With 0-1 trap, Elo (and the progress weight) use g = 1/k with k = target + plausible wrong choices (`plausibleK()`: look-alikes or a shared growth form, min 2), and the answer log records the mode as `quiz|kN` so the sheet's `stats_` (Code.gs v9) and `eloOf()` (`core.js`) rate the photo with the same guess rate; an older sheet script treats it as 4 choices. **Confusion scores** (Pierre, 2026-11-01: real mix-ups are more relevant than the declared look-alikes alone, which only initialise them): the look-alike traps are the genera with the highest `confOf(t, n)` = `confScore()` (`core.js`): mixed up / offered together for the pair, pooled over every RA (`?stats=1` `conf`, Code.gs v10), starting from a prior worth `CONF.W` = 8 offers (declared look-alike 0.3, same family 0.12, shared IMR growth form 0.08, other 0.03). A pair is a trap (`confusable`) from `CONF.TRAP` = 0.2, so a declared pair nobody confuses fades and an undeclared one players do confuse becomes a trap; scores are shuffled ±20% so close pairs all get tried. `plausibleK()` counts confusable choices. Every answer logs the choices offered (`ch`, Code.gs v10 `Choices` column); without v10 deployed `conf` is empty and the priors alone apply (the old behaviour). **Personal path** (Pierre, 2026-11-01): that pooled score is then the prior for the player's own record on the pair, `pairConf(P, a, b)` (`core.js`) = [offered together, mixed up] in both directions from `P[g].off` (each wrong choice shown beside `g`, counted in `answer()` since 2026-11-01) and `P[g].conf` (genus answered instead), again worth `CONF.W` offers, so the player's weight is offers / (offers + 8). A pair a player keeps mixing up becomes their trap even if others don't confuse it; one they now tell apart sinks back. Duels weigh pairs ×3 when the player mixed them up and still confuses them, ×2 when `confusable` or a classic, else ×1. My progress "Often mixed up" lists the player's mixed-up pairs ranked by that score (top 3, chips open the comparison with "Drill this pair"). `mergeProgress()` adds `off` up across devices like `conf`.
- **Online calibration** (`CAL`, `calBonus` in `model.js`, Pierre 2026-11-02): a photo with few answers from all the players (`?stats=1` plain count) is drawn more often, and 20% of quiz questions are calibration questions (one view alone, the colony and view chosen on the difficulty targeting and that bonus only), so photos nobody had seen get rated; calibration questions only on a genus the player holds (met, not shaky), and the targeting is wider for a photo with few answers (`CAL.U`: its prior is only its genus's offset; a subject may set its own, `SUBJECTS` `calib` `u`: fish 0.25, whose photos all carry a judged ease, and share the draw's floor over a pool of observations, `calib` `pool`: fish 30; Q186, docs/LEARNING.md "The fish draw in big pools"). Details and simulation: `docs/LEARNING.md`.
- **One colony per question** (PR #207, rule P4): photos are grouped by observation (`p.o`); a question shows the views of one colony only, never topped up from another.
