the fish auto-sort counts its target with it too); `fishMine` stays the keep on its first species, so a Not this on an auto add counts as a correction in the agreement, and the auto-sort never proposes the photo again. **Missing phases first**: a candidate whose phase (`fishPhase`, as `tools/fetch_fish.py` `photo_phase`: Pierre's phase and tags when set, else the pass's phase tag, its juvenile flag, else the source's life stage or sex) none of its species' photos in play shows comes first in its species, tagged "new"; the copy of the pass's tags and phase that Add and the fish auto-sort write beside an add carries the source's phase when the pass gives none (`autosort.js` `fishCopy`: the `j` flag for a juvenile, else the phase word), so a juvenile by its source still counts as one once in play (Codex review #602).
- **Pre-sort** (`tools/judging_fish.py`, run on request): **one fish judging vocabulary** (Pierre, 2026-11-04), the rubric of `judging/FISH.md` ("Candidates"), **visual criteria only**: the species is the iNaturalist identification by human experts, taken as given, never judged by the pass. Per candidate the rubric's code (view, whole, light, colour loss 0-3, clarity, size, count, the juvenile / camouflage / out-of-water flags, ease 1-5), `keep` (best / alt / dup within its observation), an optional IMR sex or colour `phase` and a proposed crop; the import applies the rubric's caps and phase flag (`tools/fish_judge.py` `cap`, `juv_early`) and writes `suggest.json` `fish` {`jt`, `e`, `k`, `ph`, `c`, `u`, `n`}, the same `jt` and `e` as the game photos in `fish-photos.json`, so a photo keeps its tags when it moves from the candidates to the game. **Sure decisions** (`autosort.js` `fishSure`, the fish `autoSure`; no `u`): best or alt → add with its tags, ease, phase and crop, whatever the ease; dup, the subject not obvious (count `x`), other species one could take for the subject (count `k`, Q323) or out of the water (flag `a`) → reject; the legacy count `m` (other species, not sorted yet) → Pierre's, never added. They go through the coral auto-sort's path (the draft, the `auto` log, 1-in-20 Checks with `autoCheck`, agreement per field; admin plan PR 4), and since 2026-11-06 (Pierre, Q180 and Q199) the sure adds are **in play by themselves**, hosted on R2 like his own adds: "Curated game photos", the photos in play by themselves (above). `fetch_fish.py` `sure_out` mirrors the rejects for its room count (`tests/fishphotos.test.js` checks they agree). The pass goes family by family, the starter families first (ROADMAP "Species photos: agent pre-proposals"); `python3 tools/judging_fish.py todo` lists the species whose candidates are not judged yet (the commits and PRs say which families each pass covered).

### Fish photos (the sources and the area rule)

Where a fish photo comes from, the rules every source applies, and the files on the way to the game (details in "Fish
content" and "Fish candidates" above; the rubric in `judging/FISH.md`).
- **Sources**: iNaturalist observations (`tools/fetch_fish.py`, candidates), FishBase's underwater photos
  (`tools/fetch_fishbase.py`, candidates), and the photos reference sources use to represent a taxon (`tools/fish_refs.py`:
  iNaturalist's taxon cover and taxon-page photos, FishBase's main picture, into `tools/fish_refs.json`).
- **Rules**: from iNaturalist, Research Grade at the species level (Pierre's own observations named to the species
  count as that species even when Needs ID: rule S2), CC0, CC BY or CC BY-NC, never share-alike or ND, never captive,
  never annotated Dead; from FishBase, underwater photos under CC BY-NC as FishBase states it. Every source keeps the
  area rule below.
- **The area**: abroad means the Indo-West Pacific only (`fetch_fish.py` `IWP`, a 30 S - 30 N, 90 E - 170 E box). A
  species confirmed near Dauin (`fish.json` `present.near`) takes its photos from anywhere in the Philippines and the
  box; any other species from the Philippines first, and from abroad only when it is confirmed in the Philippines
  (`present.ph`, rule C8; the iNaturalist search only once the Philippines are exhausted). Pierre's own observations
  come first, in the Philippines or the box.
- **On the way to the game**: candidates wait in `fish-cand.json` (admin only) and the reference photos in
  `tools/fish_refs.json`; once put in play (by Pierre in the admin, or by a sure judgement) they are downloaded with his
  crop, uploaded to R2 and listed in `fish-game.json` (`tools/fish_game.py`, the Candidate photos workflow). Until an
  entry has 5 very easy curated photos it also shows the stopgap remote photos of `fish-photos.json` ("Photos, stopgap"
  above).
- **The tiles** (Pierre, 2026-11-09, Q321 D4; "Find the X" and Matrix): a photo in play whose tags pass the tile rule and
  that has the fish's box (the judging pass's `bb`: `tools/fish_judge.json` `boxes`, a candidate's `suggest.json` `bb`)
  gets a fifth file on R2, `img/<f>-x.webp`, a 360 px square cut around the fish (`tools/fish_game.py` `tile_crop`,
  `tile_write`), kept when the fish fills 70% of it and its box is 320 px wide in the original; `fish-game.json` carries
  `bb` and `xc` (the square) or `xn` (why none). `tools/photos.py --r2-tiles` lists them and `tools/r2_sync.sh` checks the
  bucket has each. Rubric and thresholds: `judging/FISH.md` "The fish's box".

### The instance's centre (`tools/instance.json`, 2026-11-14)
Pierre, 2026-11-13 (Q339): "Il faudra rendre générique la localisation centrale de l'instance, si jamais on propose d'autres instances à d'autres organisations plus tard." `tools/instance.json` `centre` `{name, lat, lon, radiusKm}` (Dauin, 9.19, 123.26, 40 km) is the one place the instance's centre is set: `tools/presence.py` builds its "near" query from it (`CENTRE`, `NEAR_AREA`, the same string as before, so the reply caches still match), `tools/build_fish.py` reads it there (its `DAUIN`) and writes it into `fish.json` `centre` in every mode (`render`). The default covers read the presence near it under generic names (`nearU`, `nearN`, falling back on `dauinU`, `dauin`: "Fish content"). **Not yet generic** (a full rename, its own PR): the presence fields `fish.json` writes (`dauin`, `dauinNI`, `dauinU`, and `present.near` / `near_dauin`, which already mean "near the centre"), which `core.js` `rankWeights` and `fishGen` (`cm`), `app.js` `fishGenera` (`cm`), `tools/fish_photos.py`, `tools/fetch_fish.py` and the tests read by name: write `nearU`, `nearN`, `nearNI` beside them in `tools/build_fish.py` `presence()` and `--near` (a rebuild from the reply cache), switch every reader to the generic names (`core.js` is a sheet-script file: rule D3), then drop the old ones; the tools that still hard-wire Dauin's area (`tools/build_extra.py`, `cand_gaps.py`, `commonness.py`, `fetch_local.py`, `fetch_fish.py`, `fish_commonness.py`, `fish_photos.py`, `fish_refs.py`, `tag_origin.py`) read `tools/instance.json` instead; the texts players read that name Dauin (`app.js`, `learn.js`, the credit's "Dauin" tag of Pierre's own photos) read `fish.json` `centre` `name`; the coral presence (`presence.json`, `tools/coral_presence.py`) keeps its `loc: 'dauin'` tag until then.

### Presence of coral species (`presence.json`, 2026-11-04)
The presence rule (rule C8, `tools/presence.py`) applied to every coral species named on a photo (game, sheet and Key photos, candidates): `python3 tools/coral_presence.py [--offline | --refresh]` writes `presence.json` = `{v, date, sp: {species (iNaturalist's name, as on the photo's t): {ok, by, rg, chk}}}` (one species per line; replies cached in `tools/.cache/presence.json`, git-ignored). Per genus (its iNaturalist taxon from `tools/taxonomy.json`), two batched species counts (the Philippines, 40 km around Dauin) and an observers query only for a species neither Corals of the World (`chk`, `tools/presence_checklists.json`: the species lists of the four COTW ecoregions that cover the Philippines, confirmed and strongly predicted; COTW's pre-2014 names mapped to WoRMS) nor near Dauin settles. The admin loads it (`side('presence.json', 'sp')`) and `presence.js` `outOfRule(p, sp)` flags a photo taken abroad (`loc` world) of a species the rule does not confirm: an "out of rule" tag next to its source on every admin page (`srcTag`), and a part of To do (Reports) listing the game and sheet photos so flagged. Nothing is removed automatically: Pierre decides (2026-11-04). He keeps the rule strict and sorts them himself (2026-11-05: a photo out of the rule "must not be there"), from that part of To do: **Remove** per photo (the × of every photo, `ACT.exclude`: excluded for good, from every genus, game and candidate list, and imports skip it; no confirmation, Undo works, Genera › Excluded restores it), **Keep** per photo (an exception: curation `seen` `rule:f`, `admin.js` `ruleKept`; the photo stays on the site and moves down to a **Kept** subsection at the bottom of the section, folded with its count (`ruleKeptList`; `presence.js` `ruleQueue`'s `kept` leaves it out of the photos to handle), out of the section's count and of Remove all; there Remove still excludes it and Keep, pressed, un-keeps it (removes the mark, undoable); on other pages its tag reads "out of rule · kept" in a neutral style), **Remove all** above the section (asks first, then excludes each photo still to handle, each its own Undo step; the kept ones stay). All go into his draft and Publish, never straight to `curation.json`. Rerun the tool after a candidate refill brings new species; a species it does not know is never flagged. `tools/fetch_local.py` asks the same rule (`confirmed_species`) before its last tier abroad. First run: see the PR of 2026-11-04 (the photos labelled world, among them the five Anacropora photos from Sabah once labelled ph, relabelled by `tools/apply_loc_fix.py`).

### Share-alike coral photos (2026-11-05)
Pierre, 2026-11-05 (aligned with the fish rules): no share-alike licence for corals any more. The candidate search takes CC0, CC BY and CC BY-NC only (`tools/fetch_local.py` `LIC`, imported by `tools/cand_gaps.py` and `tools/build_extra.py`), checked photo by photo (`photo_ok`: one observation may mix licences); every refill drops the waiting share-alike candidates from `cand.json` (`drop_share_alike`, never a candidate `curation.json` names, e.g. one Pierre added; `--plan` shows them as `drop`; `tools/apply_no_share_alike.py` did it once, 7 candidates: 4 waiting, 3 he had rejected). The share-alike photos already on the site are never removed automatically: `presence.js` `licence(p)` (the recorded `lic`, else the credit line, as `fetch_local.py` `licence()`; `tests/coralcand.test.js` checks both agree on every photo) and `shareAlike(p)` give a **share-alike** tag next to the source on every admin page (`admin.js` `saTag`, in `srcTag`), and To do (Reports) has a **Share-alike** part (`saQueue`: every photo a genus page lists, not removed, not kept) with exactly the actions of "Out of the presence rule" above: Remove (the ×), Keep (curation `seen` `sa:f`, `saKept`; down to the folded Kept subsection, tag "share-alike · kept"), un-keep, Remove all (asks first, only the photos still to handle). Both parts are drawn by one function, `decSec(key)` / `bindDecide(key)` with `DECIDE[key]` (the attributes carry the key: `data-rule…`, `data-sa…`). On 2026-11-05 the site had 14 share-alike coral photos (8 CC BY-SA, 6 CC BY-NC-SA: 6 in `data.json` views, 8 candidates Pierre had added; the list by genus is in the PR).

### Photo rights tie-break (2026-11-08)
Pierre, 2026-11-08 (Q270): "à qualité égale", photos under CC0 or CC BY, and his own, before CC BY-NC, so the stock a later commercial version could use grows; the CC BY-NC photos stay fully usable. `core.js` `rightsClass(p)` gives `imr` (IMR lecture), `book` (Coral Finder), `own` (Pierre's own, whatever its licence), `open` (CC0, CC BY) or `nc` (the rest, no licence read included), from `licOf(p)` (the recorded `lic`, else the credit line; the admin's `presence.js` `licence` is it); `rightsRank(p)` is 0 for own or open, 1 otherwise, own and open alike so his photos are never boosted over another open one (rule P1, fish). It is the **last key before the fixed order** (hash, file name, votes rank) wherever otherwise-equal photos are ordered: `easyCover` (`easyRank` with the rights: the fish card photo of a family, genus or species, and the admin's `fishOrder` and `fishCoverOf` when Pierre has not picked), `applySlots` `byQr` (the corals' featured, sheet and Coral ID Key photos at equal quality, inside the IMR / own / others source order), `autosort.js` `fishRun` (which sure candidates fill a species' room) and `fishQueue` (the swiper's order within a species), `tools/fetch_local.py` `tie_key` (between coral observations of the same grade and as many iNaturalist votes). `tools/fetch_fish.py` `select` too, after the distance and the observer and life-stage variety (Pierre, 2026-11-08, Q284: "je ne veux pas que ça soit au coût de la variété, surtout dans un premier temps"; from 2026-11-03 the licence was a bucket before both: `lic_class`, now on `tools/rights.py`). Nothing else moves: what enters play (`tools/fish_game.py` `in_play`, the judging rules), and the draw: **the lists the draw reads keep no licence** (the fish `views.wide`, `easyRank(L, d, false)`; the corals' `sl.game`, `byQ0`, its featured lead included), since `model.js` `photoSet` breaks ties between equal scores by the list's order when it gives the first observations its variety floor (Codex review #742), so its weights, difficulty and memory are those of before. `tools/rights.py` is the Python copy; `tests/rights.test.js` checks the app, the admin and Python read every photo record alike. **The rights facet in the admin** (ROADMAP "Rights tagged for a later split", the licence study of 2026-11-08; read-only, nothing moves): every photo tile carries its class as a small outlined badge (`admin.js` `rightsTag`: own, open, NC, IMR, book; the licence in its title), on the genus pages and every page that shows a coral photo's source (`srcTag`), the fish family, review and candidate tiles (`fuTile`, `fishSrcTag`); **Inbox › Coverage › Rights** (`renderRights`, every subject's page whatever its `SUBJECTS` `admin` `pages` say: `admin.js` `EVERY`) counts them per genus or species for the corals (every photo a genus page lists now, not hidden, IMR lecture photos included; a species row per species named on the photos, then the genus's photos named to the genus only) and per family, genus or species for the fish (the photos in play, `fuData`), with **Ready** = commercial-ready (own + open), least ready first or the list's order, a class no photo has without a column; Coverage › Games carries the subject's total in one line (`rightsLine`). The counts are `presence.js` `rightsTally` (a photo once, by its `f`), `rightsBy` and `speciesOf` (`tests/rights.test.js`). On 2026-11-08's files (the admin with the published curation): the corals' genus pages 425 of 1882 photos commercial-ready, 23% (246 own, 179 open, 1339 NC, 118 IMR lecture), the fish photos in play 8954 of 13898, 64% (689 own, 8265 open, 4944 NC); no book photo. On 2026-11-08's data (default ease order, no answers yet): the card photo of 3 of 50 fish families (all three have a cover Pierre picked in the admin, which the game's card does not read: `core.js` `fishGen`), 3 of 151 genera and 8 of 438 species, each from a CC BY-NC photo to an open one; the coral featured photos of 6 genus views (8 photos; no coral card photo) and the sheet's next ones of 24; no list the draw reads; the first candidate of 114 of 648 fish species in the swiper; no auto-sort add (every species at its target). The fish fetch with the licence last (`--plan` on a reply cache of 2026-11-04, so an estimate): 168 of 415 observations picked change, in 55 of 179 species with room; the picks hold 414 distinct observers per species instead of 404, and 36 open-licence observations instead of 201 (the open ones mostly come from observers already in the lists).

## Photos per context (`applySlots`, `CAP`)

Per genus and view, from field photos + IMR lecture photos (`data.json` `course`), never multi-coral ones, never a coral skeleton (`skel`, rule P6: `applyCuration` has dropped it from every list before):
- **Order**: Pierre's pins (`feat`), then Claude's suggestions (`picks.json`), then IMR photos, Pierre's own, other observers, each by quality, at equal quality an open licence before CC BY-NC for the featured, sheet and Key photos (`rightsRank`, "Photo rights tie-break" above; the game list keeps the quality order alone); Poor last (but an IMR photo is never auto-rated Poor).
- **Sheet**: the 3 featured + the next ones, 6 in all.
- **Games** (`game`, one list per view): every field + IMR lecture photo kept, never `use: 'ref'`; the featured game photos first, then IMR / own before other observers, each by quality, Poor last. No cap since 2026-11-02 (Pierre: newly validated candidates must come into the games; Medium and Hard used to stop at 4, other observers' photos only filling a short set). The players' answers rate every photo (`photoDiff`, online calibration `CAL`); until 2026-11-03 the list was split into Easy (the featured) / Medium (other good ones) / Hard (Poor) sets that gave a new photo its starting difficulty, removed by Pierre once the replay of the real answers showed them worse than no prior. `gamePics()` reads it.
- **Coral ID Key** (`idkey`): featured + up to 5 other-observer photos (`core.js` `CAP.inat`; 2, plus up to 3 approved Coral Finder book crops, until Pierre's 2026-11-08 decision to drop the crops) (variety on purpose). The book path's box tiles show a drawing of each box's criterion, never photos (`proto/idkey.js` `BOXART`; DESIGN.md "Lab drawings"); a box with no drawing whose genera are in no other box (the non-hard-coral group, the elongate free-living discs) shows those genera's covers. Once a growth form is picked (Coral Finder box or IMR form), each genus card in Mix leads with a photo tagged with that form (`formCover()` in `proto/idkey.js`: `formPhotos`/`imrFormPhotos`, IMR lecture first, then Pierre's own); the cover stays when it already shows the form, and a genus with no photo of that form keeps its cover (first-time RA review, 2026-11-01).

## Photos

- **Served from Cloudflare R2** (Photos out of the repo, step 2, 2026-11-03): every URL of a site photo is `PHOTO_BASE` + `img/<f>…webp`, `PHOTO_BASE` in `core.js` (`https://pub-934250f83b884a4f890497f2027ce251.r2.dev/`, the bucket `tools/r2_sync.sh` mirrors `img/` to, same paths, `Cache-Control: public, max-age=604800`). It is the one switch: `''` sends the app, the admin and the Coral ID Key to the site's `img/` for the photos with no online original and to their online original for the others, crops drawn (`AWAY` true from the start in `app.js` and `admin.js`: since step 2 the site has no copy of those). Built by `app.js` `photoUrl()` / `loc()` (also `srcset`, `thumb`, Home's ReefCloud art) and `admin.js` `img()`; `ownPhoto()` tells a site photo (on R2 or the site) from an iNaturalist one, `resized()` keeps the base. **Fallback** (Photos out of the repo, step 1 of Pierre's rule of 2026-11-05): an `<img>` whose R2 file fails is asked from R2 once more 800 ms later (`core.js` `photoRetry`, a passing error; under the service worker the worker does that retry), then moves to the photo's **online original** when it has one (`src` in `data.json` / `cand.json` / `fish-photos.json` / `fish-game.json`: iNaturalist, Pierre's own iNaturalist photos, FishBase), at a light size, never the original file: iNaturalist `small` (240 px) for our 240 px file, `medium` (500 px) for 480 px, `large` (1024 px) for 960 and 1600 px, FishBase's one file as it is (`core.js` `photoOnline`); a photo with none (IMR lecture photos, page art) moves to the same file on the site's `img/` (`photoFallback` rewrites `src`, `srcset`, `data-lo` and the game backdrop's inline style through `photoAway`, which finds the photo's record by the file name in the address, `photoFileOf`, in an index of every photo record, `photoIndex`; `photoFail` in `app.js`, a capture listener on `document` before every other; the admin has the same). Without a service worker, `R2` then turns false and `AWAY` true for the rest of the visit, so the next photos go there directly (`loc()`, `resized()`; the quiz preloads, which are no page elements, do the same in `pump()`). Under the service worker both stay as they are: the worker keeps photos offline under their R2 address and fails at once while R2 is out, each photo falls back on its own, and its 480 px step (`data-lo`) and backdrop stay on R2, where an offline copy may be. The usual retries (below) then apply to the new address. **Crops**: the site's files have Pierre's crop baked in (`cr`, `tools/fetch_approved.py`), the original has not: once a photo falls back, every `<img>` added or given a new `src` is watched (`cropWatch`, a `MutationObserver`, only from the first fallback on) and a cropped photo shown from its original gets the crop drawn with `object-view-box` (`core.js` `photoCropOf`, as the admin's crop preview); a browser without `object-view-box` (Safari, Firefox in 2026) shows the whole photo. **Rotations**: the files have Pierre's rotation baked in too (`ro`, "Photo rotation" in the admin), the original has not. The app makes the file's picture itself (Codex review #793): from the first fallback on, the same watch finds every element using a turned photo's original (`app.js` `turnSet`: an `<img>`'s `src`, `srcset` and `data-lo`, an inline style's `url()`, so the game's backdrop `--qbg` and the lightbox's placeholder) and gives it the original cut and turned in a canvas as the workflow does (`turnPlan`, `turnJob`: a `blob:` address, one per address for the visit, the crop baked in, so no `object-view-box`); every page then shows it at its file's shape with no layout of its own (the quiz's fit, the lightbox at actual size and its labels, the sheet tiles). Meanwhile the element shows none of the original. A picture that cannot be made (FishBase sends no CORS header, so its canvas is tainted; a failed download) is given up at once: a game photo or a card's becomes the "photo missing" tile through the usual error listeners, another photo is hidden; never a photo the wrong way up. The admin's fallback draws it: its error handler gives the photo its published rotation (`admin.js` `photoRoOf`, the record found as `photoCropOf` finds its crop) as `data-rot`, which `rotFit` turns once the original has loaded. The CSP of `index.html` allows the originals' hosts (`tests/csp.test.js` checks every host in the data).
- **Where the files are** (Photos out of the repo, step 2 of Pierre's rule of 2026-11-05): `img/` in git keeps only the photos with no other backup: 120 IMR lecture photos, the page art and 3 old candidate files that only `curation.json` names (124 photos, 494 files on 2026-11-08; the 554 Coral Finder book crops, 2216 files, were removed that day: Pierre, copyrighted). Every downloaded photo with an online original (`src` and no `r`: `tools/photos.py` `online()`) lives **on R2 only**, its fallback being that original: 1387 photos, 5548 files, ~502 MB on 2026-11-05 (1004 candidates Pierre added, 304 site photos of slate genera, 79 of Millepora and the bonus genera; 241 of them Pierre's own), removed from `img/` in one commit once each file was found on R2 byte for byte (`tools/r2_move.py`; R2's ETag is the file's MD5). What keeps it so: `tools/fetch_approved.py` writes the photos it downloads to `r2-upload/` (gitignored, never `img/`) and sets their `ar`, and `approved-photos.yml` uploads that folder (`tools/r2_sync.sh r2-upload`) before it commits `data.json`, so `data.json` never marks downloaded a photo R2 lacks; `tools/r2_sync.sh` copies and never deletes from the bucket (`rclone copy`: a sync from `img/` would wipe the R2-only photos), then checks the bucket holds the four files of every R2-only photo (`photos.py --r2-list`); `tools/photos.py --check` (CI) fails on a photo with an online original back in `img/` and on a missing file of a photo with none; a tool that reads a photo's pixels opens `photos.path(f, size)` (the file in `img/`, else a copy from R2 kept in `tools/.cache/r2/`: `photo_quality.py`, `photo_metrics.py`, `find_dups.py`, `apply_multi.py`; `judging_manifest.py` gives the R2 address); the smokes answer R2 and iNaturalist from the local files, an R2-only photo with a stand-in of the same size (`tests/photostub.js`). The old copies stay in `.git` until step 3, the history rewrite (ROADMAP, HANDOVER "Photos out of the repo, step 3").
- WebP, four sizes on the long side: 1600 px `img/<f>.webp`, 960 px `img/<f>-m.webp`, 480 px `img/<f>-s.webp`, 240 px `img/<f>-t.webp` (`tools/photos.py`: `save()` for every tool that writes photos, `python3 tools/photos.py` fills missing smaller files, `--check` in CI). In `img/` 2710 files, ~110 MB, and on R2 only the 5548 files of the photos with an online original, ~502 MB (2026-11-05, above; on 2026-11-01 the 960 px files added 82 MB, mean 69 KB, the 240 px ones 12 MB, mean 10 KB; 1600 px mean 141 KB, 480 px 31 KB). No Git LFS.
- Sources: IMR Lecture 6 deck (internal use at IMR only; cut from the full-resolution PowerPoint media, `2026_L6_CoralID/ppt/media` on Pierre's Drive (HANDOVER "External resources"), never from the Google Slides copy, compressed on purpose. Each file keeps the framing the slide shows; 36 photos first cut from the Slides copy, as small as 281 px, were re-cut from their originals on 2026-11-05 (slide → media through `ppt/slides/_rels/slideN.xml.rels`, then matched by content), the others already had the original's resolution. Many originals are browser screenshots; three pictures are stretched on their slide, about 15 % wider than the original (`imrl-p8_i6`, `imrl-p9_i7`, `imrl-p45_i3`): their files take the slide's region at the original's true proportions, without the stretch (Pierre, 2026-11-05, Q63: the stretch was only for the slides)), Pierre's iNaturalist observations, other iNaturalist observers (CC0, CC BY or CC BY-NC, never share-alike since 2026-11-05; Research Grade, a genus-level community ID, or, in the Philippines only, a current identification by the trusted expert `thamnasteroid` at the genus or below that no other current identification disagrees with: `tools/fetch_local.py` `EXPERTS`, `expert_ok`, Pierre 2026-11-05: by far the top identifier of stony corals in the Philippines, trust his identification; an identification on the same line of descent agrees, so an observer's coarse "Stony corals" refined by him to the genus is accepted (iNaturalist then has no genus-level community taxon), while another genus or a taxon off that line refuses; withdrawn identifications do not count, an expert identification above the genus is refused; abroad Research Grade only and the presence rule). No Coral Finder 2022 book photo (copyright R. Kelley/BYOGUIDES): its 554 crops were removed on 2026-11-08 (Pierre).
- New candidates are not stored: they are shown from iNaturalist until Pierre adds them. `.github/workflows/approved-photos.yml` runs on every `curation.json` push: `tools/fetch_approved.py` downloads added photos (applying Pierre's crop, then his rotation) into `r2-upload/`, uploaded to R2 before the commit (they have an online original, so they never go to `img/`: "Where the files are" above), `tools/fetch_local.py` refills candidate lists (caps per genus, counted in observations waiting: 15 near Dauin, 6 elsewhere in the Philippines while a genus has fewer than 15 Dauin photos; a **Key-only genus** (Pierre, 2026-11-03) keeps at most as many observations waiting, over all sources, as the colonies it still lacks (`key_need`: `CAP.sheet` = 6 per Colony and Close-up view, Close-up = macro for a 2-level genus, minus the distinct observations of its curated photos there: counted as the admin's `gamePool` counts them, one rule mirrored in `colonies()`: data.json views with curation `move`, `hide`, `add` and `out`, views as `viewOf` (medium joins Colony only for a genus switched to 2 levels by curation), no sheet-only (`use: 'ref'`), extra, multi-coral, book or excluded photo, and in Colony no photo whose growth form is not visible unless the genus has one form; `tests/pages.smoke.js` checks the admin and the script agree on the real data, genus by genus; Needs ID colonies count, as in the admin's `gamePool` for a genus that is not played; a waiting candidate counts only when its guessed view is one of those two and still lacks colonies, as the admin's swiper shows it, so a full view's candidates never hold the room another view needs), and one that lacks none is not refilled (no iNaturalist request); `--plan` prints offline, as JSON, what a run would refill (`tests/genera.test.js`); each observation brings all its photos (up to 12, a guard only) since 2026-11-01: Pierre takes at least the colony, a closer view and a macro, a game question shows the views of one colony, and the order of an observation's photos says nothing about their zoom, so sorting by view, quality and near-duplicates is the judging pass's job; before, 2 per observation, its first and last), then commits `data.json` + `cand.json` (+ `img/`, only for a missing smaller size of a photo git keeps).
- `tools/excluded.py` makes every import skip excluded photos (by file name and iNat photo id); `drop_excluded` (run by `tools/fetch_local.py` after each publish) takes them out of every photo list, except a candidate the auto-sort excluded (`exclude[f] = 'auto'`), whose entry stays in `cand.json` so that its Undo can bring it back (Codex review #400); excluded, it is neither shown nor counted as waiting. Each record Pierre's exclusion removes is kept in **`excluded.json`** (`{photos: {f: {in, g, k, v, i, p}}}`: which list, genus, views/more, view, position and the record; one photo per line, `datajson.dump`; seeded from git history by `tools/apply_excluded_archive.py` for the photos removed before 2026-11-06), so that his **Restore** (admin Genera › Excluded) brings the photo back by itself at the next run, at its place (`restore_unexcluded`; Pierre, 2026-11-06). A candidate comes back marked `restored: 1`, which the share-alike drop spares (a share-alike one he restores stays a candidate he can add); a photo with no online original (an IMR lecture photo, whose file left `img/` with its exclusion) is held in `excluded.json` until its file is back: the workflow (whole history, `fetch-depth: 0`, `filter: blob:none`) runs `python tools/apply_excluded_archive.py --files` before `tools/photos.py`, which takes the file back from git history, so it returns with its files; one whose file is nowhere in the history stays held, said in the run's log. Both run in `fetch_local.py` `tidy()`, which ends every refill and runs alone, offline, as `python3 tools/fetch_local.py --tidy`: restored photos back, excluded ones out (into `excluded.json`), then the waiting share-alike candidates dropped, and `dump_all` re-splits `data.json` / `cand.json`; `datajson.curated()` (the candidates `curation.json` uses) leaves out any excluded id. The workflow checks out the latest `main` (a run queued behind another starts from its commit: on 2026-11-06 two Publishes in a row left the second one's exclusions out of the data, its run's commit lost in a conflict) and runs `--tidy` last before its commit (`tests/coralcand.test.js`).

## Admin back-office (`admin.html`)

For Pierre only (not linked except from ⚙ for token holders). **Four header modes, each with its tabs** (Pierre, 2026-11-03, on the mockup "Admin tabs: 17 → 9": nine tabs in three groups then; before, 2026-11-01, four header tabs over 17 pages; Analyses since 2026-11-04, tabs added since): `GROUPS`, `TABS` and `navs()` in `admin.js`, `#modes` in `admin.html`. The header shows the modes **Inbox**, **Photos**, **Players** and **Analyses** (see "Analyses" below; the To do count and the over-the-cap ⚠ on Inbox); the row under it (`#subnav`) the tabs of the current group on the left and, for a tab with several views, one segmented switch of its views on the right (links with `aria-pressed`, the counts on the views: reports, hidden, excluded, ⚠ over the cap). **Addresses** (URL refonte, Pierre 2026-11-05, Q53-Q56; [study](reviews/2026-11-05-url-scheme-study.md)): `#<group>/<tab>/<view>`, each segment the word on screen in lower case joined by `-` (a tab drops its group's word: Photo review → `review`), computed from `GROUPS` (`TABS`, `ADM_PAGES`); a tab with one view has no view segment (`#players/activity`), unless that view is named for the views to come (`#fish/photos/tree/key`: Key, WoRMS later); a thing a page is about follows as a last segment (`#photos/genera/live/<Genus>`, `#players/list/journey/<name>`, `#players/list/anon/<id>`, `#inbox/todo/points/<id>`; `ADM_PARAM`), a filter after `?` (`#inbox/coverage/games?look=all`: Where to look open in full; `hashQuery`, split off before decoding). `admRoute()` decides what an address shows: a shorter one (a group, a tab) opens its first view, an old or unknown one its group's first view, else the landing page, and the address is replaced in place (`readHash`); old addresses (`#~…`, `#<Genus>`) are not translated (rule W3; no `ALIAS`). Links come from `GROUPS` (`navs()`) and `genusHref()`. A header group reopens the tab and view used last in it (`reefid-admin-nav-group`, group → address), a tab its view used last (`reefid-admin-nav-tab`, tab → address), the subject switch the same page on the other side (`admin.js` `sameOn`: the view on screen when that side has it, else the view that side opens for the same tab or group; Pierre, 2026-11-09), each checked against today's addresses before use, all in the admin store (`demo:`-prefixed in the demo); the app's ⚙ View keeps `reefid-admin-view` to itself. Phones (< 600 px): the header menu (`#modesel`) lists the 9 tabs grouped, the row keeps only the view switch, full width; between 600 and about 1280 px the header groups give way to the same menu when they don't fit (`fitModes()`).

**Names** (Pierre, 2026-11-04): no tab holds a view of its own name and no group a tab of its own name: the group To do became **Inbox**, the tab Players **List**, the views of Activity **Recent sessions** and **Mix-ups**.

**One subject switch** (admin plan PR 2, 2026-11-04; Pierre: one Corals | Fish switch for everything, To do included): a mini switch next to the logo, in the header line at every width (`#subj`, `navs()`; Pierre, 2026-11-06: as the app's header chip), each side the subject's icon (`core.js` `SUBJECTS` `icon`, the app's set), its name on screens 600 px and wider (its label on phones, where each side shows its icon over its count and the logo steps aside while the switch shows, so the page menu keeps its room) and its to-do count (`subjTodo`: the corals' To do items; for a subject with its own part of `curation.json`, the Checks its auto-sort left for Pierre). Each subject describes its admin part in `core.js` `SUBJECTS` `admin`: `hash`, the prefix of its addresses (`''` for the corals; `fish/` for fish, so `#fish/inbox/todo/reports` is the fish side's Reports), `cur`, its part of `curation.json` (`''` = the top level; `fish`), `pages`, the admin pages it has (`null` = all; a page covers the addresses under it; fish for now: Reports, Errors, Players and the journeys, the pages not about one subject, Coverage › Games, Photos › Tree and Photo review › Ease and Phase & framing, Look-alikes). The Players pages are every subject's whatever `pages` says (`hasPage`; Pierre, 2026-11-06): Activity and a Journey follow the switch, the list is shared; every subject's progress is replayed in the admin from its answers (`unitsOf`: data.json's genera for the replayed subject, the fish entries' circles for another, read once by `fishAdmin`; `progOf(u, full, S)`, `statsFor`: the sheet's photo numbers for the replayed subject only), with the saved copy's cards and mastery exams (`savedFor`: each subject's keys) and ↻ saved where it differs; a Journey lists the player's sessions as Activity does (`recentSessions(R, {own})`, `bindSess`); the Elo and the genus sheets opened are the replayed subject's only. A tab for one unit rank only (`GROUPS` option `rank`: Tree, `rank: 'family'`) is shown only to a subject of that rank (`SUBJECTS` `rank`; `hasPage`), never to the corals. The header shows only the groups, tabs and views of the pages the subject has; an address of a page it does not have yet says "Not for fish yet" (the fish pages join as the admin plan builds them). Each subject remembers its own last tab, view and page (`subjK`: the corals keep their store keys). A link without a prefix (a player's journey from Activity) keeps the subject on screen when it has that page; the switch's own links say which subject, and so does a link marked `data-own` (the list's journey links, a journey's own toggle); on a player's page the switch keeps the player (`playerPage`). Tested in `tests/admin.smoke.js` (both widths).

| Group | Tab | Views and addresses |
|---|---|---|
| Inbox | **To do** | All `#inbox/todo/all` (new: everything by urgency: Health's red line only when something is wrong (`healthLine()`: photos everyone misses, genera players are stuck on), reports, flags, the devices' errors of the last 30 days (`evSummary()`), Featured photos rated Poor, the judging agent's suggestions last) · Reports `#inbox/todo/reports` (was Triage) · Errors `#inbox/todo/errors` (was Errors & feedback) · Health `#inbox/todo/health` (what needs action: photos everyone misses, genera that don't take off) · Points `#inbox/todo/points` (the points to handle, one page each `#inbox/todo/points/<id>`: "Points to handle" below) |
| Inbox | **Coverage** | Games `#inbox/coverage/games` (was Game coverage; **Where to look** is a banner above its grid: the 5 cells most worth a candidate search, `lookBanner()`, "All N" opens the full list there, `#inbox/coverage/games?look=all`, was the Where to look page) · Sheets & ID Key `#inbox/coverage/sheets-id-key` (was Sheet & ID Key coverage) · Rights `#inbox/coverage/rights` (the rights facet, every subject's: "Photo rights tie-break" below; on the fish side `#fish/inbox/coverage/rights`) | On the fish side, `#fish/inbox/coverage/games` (`renderFishCov`, admin plan PR 8): per family, genus or species (a switch), the species at their target, photos in play, easy (ease 4-5) and reference photos, the phases shown (an IMR phase still missing marked ?), candidates waiting and what is missing (`autosort.js` `fishCov`); All \| IMR and Thinnest \| Book; **To do** (species under target with candidates waiting, species with no photo in play, families validated but incomplete, families not ready); **Where to look** (`fishLook`: species under target whose waiting candidates cannot fill the gap, the IMR course's first; `?look=all` lists every one).
| Photos | **Genera** | Live = the genus pages `#photos/genera/live/<Genus>` (their sticky bar has a view filter, All · Colony · Close-up · Corallites, Pierre 2026-11-04: one view's section alone, Candidates and Not this genus hidden and counted in one line; kept across genera in `reefid-admin-gview`, All on a genus without the saved view) · Hidden `#photos/genera/hidden` (new: every photo removed from the site, Put back; `renderHidden()`) · Excluded `#photos/genera/excluded` (was the Excluded tab; Restore, Undo) |
| Photos | **Tree** (fish only for now; was Families until 2026-11-13: Pierre, Q340, "ok"; the corals join it in a later step) | **Key** `#fish/photos/tree/key/<node id>` (`#fish/photos/tree/key/f%3ADamselfishes`, `…/g%3AChromis`, `…/s091`; none or an unknown id: the first family; the old `#fish/photos/families/…` is not kept, rule A4) and **WoRMS** `#fish/photos/tree/worms/<node id>` (2026-11-14; Q340: views Key \| WoRMS, one parent per classification), in the tab's view switch (the switch keeps a species, the one node of both views). **WoRMS** (`fuWormsOf`): the reference taxonomy as a tree, from `fish.json` `ref` (WoRMS AphiaIDs, each `{name, rank, parent}`, `tools/build_fish.py`) above the Key's own leaves (the species in the game, in the Key's order) with `taxtree.js` `ttFromRef`: any depth and WoRMS's ranks in lower case (class, order, suborder, family, subfamily, tribe, genus; up to 8 levels today), its problems (a reference or a parent missing, a cycle) counted in the bar (`.adm-ttprob`, none today); none or an unknown id: its first family. **Node ids** (one parent per classification, so one id space per view above the leaves): a species keeps its id (`s091`) and is **one node in both views** (its page, its cover `curation.json fish genera[s091]`, its choices are the same); every taxon above it is `w:<AphiaID>` (`w:125553` Pomacentridae, `w:126045` Chromis), never a Key id: the key's families and genera are not WoRMS's (64 game species sit in a WoRMS family other than their key family's scientific name: Labridae holds the Wrasses and the Parrotfishes, Holocentridae the Soldierfishes and the Squirrelfishes; 10 in another genus: Pycnochromis holds four of the key's Chromis), and `f:`/`g:` are the app's sheet ids. A species' WoRMS page is its Key page (every choice through the Key's tree: its unit, its photos' paths) with the WoRMS path, tree and selects around it; a **WoRMS taxon's page is read-only** (no data shape for a `w:` node without Pierre's decision): its cover in force, always the default by the rule (`nodeCover` on its species' photos, as a Key node's; never a ★), and its children's cards, each with its own cover in force; the bar's select lists WoRMS's families; All \| IMR shows the leaves under an IMR tag of the Key (`imr`), so the same species in both views; the unfolded nodes are kept per view (`reefid-admin-tree-open-worms-<subject>`). **Key**: the identification key's classification as a **collapsible tree** at the left (`fuTreeOf`, `fuTreeMount`, `ttMount`): each key group (`k:<its number>`, `k:none` for a family with none), its families, their genera and species (the species in the game, as the family's page shows them), built with the generic tree of `taxtree.js` (below), each row with its rank as a small grey word, its species and photos in play (a family's row as the list had it: its cover, circle, what it needs, ✓ Validated, the draft's dot; a species its draft's dot: `fuRow`); the key groups unfold by default, the ▸ / ▾ unfolds a node (remembered per subject in this browser, `reefid-admin-tree-open-<subject>`), a new address unfolds the way to its node; the WAI-ARIA tree pattern (`role=tree`, a flat list of `treeitem` with `aria-level`, `aria-posinset`, `aria-setsize`, `aria-expanded`, one row in the Tab order: ↑ ↓ move, → unfolds then goes to the first child, ← folds then goes to the parent, Home / End, `*` unfolds the siblings, Enter or Space opens the node's page); **every node has a page of its own** (`renderFishUnits`, 2026-11-14; docs/ROADMAP.md "The Tree tab"; the ★ Family | Genus | Species switch and the genus select of #858 went with them): the sticky bar shows the path from the root above it (`.adm-crumbs`, each ancestor a link), the families' select (none chosen above the families), All \| IMR, and the select of the node's children (`[data-ttkid]`, the drill-down: on phones, where the tree is hidden, the way down to a genus or a species; at every width). A **key group** (any node above the unit rank) gets a light page: its children's cards (`.adm-tcard`, each its tree row: cover, names, counts, what it needs, ✓, the draft's dot), each opening its page. A **family, genus or species** (the unit, a node under it: `taxtree.js` `ttNearest` finds its unit) shows its photos in play, one section per species under it inside one block per genus between (any depth: `block`), each heading with its own cover in force (ringed when his) and its name opening its page; the bar its cover in force and why (`admin-covers.js` `nodeCover`, `nodeCoverWhy`: "Your ★ cover", else "Default: Most seen near <centre>: N observers" for a node with species under it, the rule of Q339 (b) as `autosort.js` `fishCoverOf`; for a species "Default: its first photo (featured, then the easiest)", `autosort.js` `fishLeafCover`, as the app's sheets; his ★ on a juvenile or out of play said), with a link to Cover proposals while the rule's other pick of a node he covered is open; its species and photos in play and the candidates still waiting (`autosort.js` `fishWaiting`, as Coverage's), also on each species heading. **★ Cover** on a photo sets the cover of the page's node (`fishTile` `o.lv`, its `data-cvk`: curation.json `fish.genera[<node id>].cover`, one Undo step, Publish names it rank by rank); each photo marks the covers it is (Family · Genus · Species cover). A node's photos (`fuNodePhotos`: the family's `fuFam` list, else its species' in the tree's order) are the ones Cover proposals reads too. Only the family's page has the wink card, ✓ Validated and what it needs; Feature, Remove, Not this and the rows Not this species and Other species, out of play keep their family's id on every page (those rows list the photos of the page's species). The page itself: the book's families (English name, the scientific names below, an **IMR** tag on the families and genera in the IMR course, its IMR name in the tooltip: Pierre, 2026-11-06 13:38; **All \| IMR** filters the list, `reefid-admin-fu-imr`); a family's page has one block per genus and one section per species with its photos in play, featured first, then the game's order (the easiest, then the most reference-like: `core.js` `easyRank`), never by who took them (`autosort.js` `fishOrder`), Feature / ★ Cover / Remove / Not this / ×, ✓ Validated with what is missing (`fishNeeds`), a wink's card; the family's choices in `curation.json` `fish` `genera` `{family id: {cover, feat: {species id: [f]}, ok}}`; the families read through `autosort.js` `fishUnits` from `fish.json` `families` / `genera` (`renderFishUnits`) |
| Photos | **Candidates** | `#photos/candidates` (its own source switch) |
| Photos | **Photo review** | Quality `#photos/review/quality` · Growth form `#photos/review/growth-form` (were two pages; same grid and tools) · By difficulty `#photos/review/by-difficulty` (every photo with answers, hardest or easiest first, with its quality to re-rate: the former Photo stats › Photo list, moved here on 2026-11-03 since it is a review tool, not stats) | On the fish side (admin plan PR 9, `renderFishReview`; views for the family rank only, `GROUPS` view option `rank`): Ease `#fish/photos/review/ease` and Phase & framing `#fish/photos/review/phase-framing`, one family's photos in play at a time (`reefid-admin-fishrev`), each in the row of its value now; one click sets Pierre's ease, juvenile flag, phase word (a family with IMR phase lines only) or view (`autosort.js` `fishSet`, `fishTag`, as the fish Candidates); the agreement line, the draft's values against the judging pass's (`fishAgree`). **Light proposals** `#fish/photos/review/light-proposals` (Pierre, 2026-11-14, "ok pour (a)"; `admin-light.js`, tests `tests/fishlight.test.js` and the admin smoke's photos part): the light re-judge's proposals (`judging/fish-light-q308.json` `curation`, rubric 6, Q308), read at run time with the token through the GitHub contents API (`ghFile`; `judging/` is not on the site; the demo answers it from the local copy), one tile each: the photo, its species, what would change in words (each tag and flag that differs, his value struck through, the re-judged one in bold, the codes in the tooltip; then the ease), **Accept** (the re-judged tags and ease into the draft, `fishSet`: one Undo step), **Keep mine** (`curation.json` `fish.kept`), **Accept all N** (one step, confirmed: bulk); rows by the light change. A proposal is open (`lightOpen`) while the photo is his add in play, its draft tags and ease both still the ones it was made against, and not kept; so an accepted one, a kept one or one whose tags or ease he changed since is done. A proposal that would move a tag to a retired code (count `m`, before 2026-11-09) is never offered (`lightRetired`; the test checks the file has none). The view is in the switch only while one is open (`GROUPS` view options `live` and `draw`, read by `hasPage` and `render`, whatever `SUBJECTS` admin `pages` lists); once on screen it stays drawn ("No light proposal left."). Publish's summary counts the proposals accepted and kept (`lightSummary`). **Cover proposals** `#fish/photos/review/cover-proposals` (Pierre, 2026-11-14, after Q339 (b) and #864: "mon choix était le meilleur au moment avec ce que j'avais sous les yeux. Si tu as mieux fait moi des propositions"; `admin-covers.js`, tests `tests/fishcoverprop.test.js` and the admin smoke's photos part): his ★ Cover of a family or a genus (`fish.genera[node].cover`) is never replaced by the default cover rule; where it is in force (in play, not a juvenile: `fishCoverOf`) and the rule now picks another photo (`autosort.js` `fishCoverRule`, the default part of `fishCoverOf`, on the photos the node's Tree page chooses from: `fuNodePhotos` over `fuFamM`; compared by `fishPid`), one card per node, families then genera: the node's name, his photo and the rule's, each with the species it shows, the rule's why in a line ("Most seen near <centre>: N observers", the centre's name from `fish.json` `centre`; "no reference photo" when its species competed without one; "your ★ species cover" when the photo is his cover of that species), **Accept** (the rule's photo as his cover in the draft, `fishPick`: one Undo step), **Keep mine** (`fish.kept[node]` = the pair, `coverKey`), **Accept all N** (one step, confirmed: bulk). Proposals are live, from the draft (`coverProposal`, `coverOpen`): one is done once answered (Accept makes the rule agree), once his cover changes (a new cover of his that still differs is a new pair, listed again), or once the rule agrees; a Keep mine holds for its pair only, so a later pick of the rule may come back. A node with no cover of his, or with his cover out of play or on a juvenile, has none (the default already applies there); species covers are not nodes and get none. In the switch while one is open (`coverLive`: the family pages' data read once, `fishLoad(true)`), the list cached per draft on `fuDataM`'s record; Publish's summary counts the Keep mine answers (`coverSummary`; an Accept is counted with the covers, rank by rank).
| Photos | **Look-alikes** | `#photos/look-alikes`. On the fish side (`#fish/photos/look-alikes`, `renderFishMont`, admin plan PR 10): one row per pair of families the IMR course compares (`autosort.js` `fishPairs`, from `fish.json` `families` `look`, IMR's lines shown as their source), a phase switch (Adult = every photo not of a juvenile, Juvenile, All: `fishMontPick`), Auto / Manual and Shown / Hidden as the coral pairs, the two photos and a strip of each family's photos in play; picks in `curation.json` `fish` `mont` `{pair: {v, a, b, hide}}` (`fishMontSet`). Species pairs (from the data, players' confusions, reference sites) come later. |
| Photos | **Photo stats** | **Overview** `#photos/stats/overview` (default; `renderPstats`, Pierre 2026-11-03: "general stats on all photos", see below) · Difficulty `#photos/stats/difficulty` |
| Players | **List** | `#players/list`, **one page for every subject** (`SHARED`: no prefix, the switch inert there, the side on screen kept for the other pages; `#fish/players/list` becomes `#players/list`): Name, Last (any subject), Devices (distinct devices of their pages-opened summaries), Groups (their beta groups), then a group of columns per subject (`SCOLS`: Answers, Right, Last, Sessions with their median length, Progress for the replayed subject, Photos and Spread from sheet v81); Elo, the quiz shares, Duels, Days, Learned, Seen and Reports moved to the Journey or the row's bar. A player's Journey `#players/list/journey/<name>` and `#players/list/anon/<id>` sit under it, **one per subject** under its prefix (`#fish/players/list/journey/<name>`), with its own Corals \| Fish at the top (each with the player's answers in it); the list opens it on the subject they played last (`lastSubj`). The row's bar ends with each subject's **starting answer** (sheet v99, Pierre 2026-11-08, Q228; `admin.js` `startBlock`): the first-launch answer in force and its history (date, answer, how given), and **Change…**: the subject's answers, then Change / Cancel in the bar, then one result line; it posts `userStart` (Code.gs `users_`: `core.js` `startAdminSet` appends a choice event via `admin`, the history kept, the revision bumped so the player's devices merge it at their next sync, `startUnion`: the latest answer wins); the fallback window restarts from that event, as after `startSet` (Pierre, 2026-11-08: the level card is still offered), so a card counts only the answers after the change |
| Players | **Activity** | Recent sessions `#players/activity` (one view: Recent sessions, Activity over time), **per subject** (`#fish/players/activity`: the subject on screen's answers, `rowsOf`) |
| Players | **Visits** | `#players/visits` (Pierre, 2026-11-08, Q301 layout A of the mockup; one view), **one page for every subject** (Pierre, 2026-11-09: as the list, `SHARED`: no prefix, the switch inert there; `#fish/players/visits` becomes `#players/visits`; a Subject filter, All by default): who opens the app and what for, games or not, signed in or not, from data already logged (no app change, no sheet bump); `admin-visits.js` `renderVisits`, its help `HELP.visits`. Below |
| Analyses | **Overview** | `#analyses/overview` (2026-11-04: what the data says clearly, every result in words, the milestones) |
| Analyses | **Speed** | Answer time `#analyses/speed/answer-time` · Enlarging `#analyses/speed/enlarging` |
| Analyses | **Difficulty** | Photos `#analyses/difficulty/photos` · Formats `#analyses/difficulty/formats` · Mix-ups `#analyses/difficulty/mix-ups` · Time `#analyses/difficulty/time` (Time and difficulty: what the time to answer and enlarging say about a photo, from the report) · Pairs `#analyses/difficulty/pairs` (live from the answer log, `statsConf`; was Players › Activity › Mix-ups) |
| Analyses | **Learning** | Practice `#analyses/learning/practice` · After a break `#analyses/learning/after-a-break` · Sessions `#analyses/learning/sessions` · Profiles `#analyses/learning/profiles` (player profiles, from the report) · Answer log `#analyses/learning/answer-log` (live: learning curve, calibration, conditions, genera, mix-ups; were Activity's cards) · Stops `#analyses/learning/stops` (live: where players stop, genus sheets opened; were on Health) |

Pages by hash (with no address the admin opens on `#players/activity`, Players › Activity, the recent sessions: Pierre, 2026-11-05): genus pages, `#photos/candidates` Candidates swiper, `#inbox/todo/all` and `#inbox/todo/reports` (players' reports with who sent them, flags, suggestions, duplicates; `renderTriage()` draws both, All adding Health's line and the errors), `#photos/review/growth-form` growth forms, `#photos/review/quality`, `#photos/genera/excluded`, `#photos/genera/hidden`, `#photos/look-alikes` Look-alikes, **Players** `#players/list` (`renderUsers`; Pierre, 2026-11-01: one page to see, sort and manage players; script v11, names joined to the anonymous stats by each player's anonymous id from v13: every signed-in player by name with **Last** (second column since 2026-11-03, Pierre: who played most recently, seen on a phone without scrolling: the exact time of their last answer in the answer log loaded, else the player list's, written "now" under `RECENT.NOW` = 5 minutes as a green pill, "12 min ago", "3 h ago" today, "yesterday 18:40" ("yest. 18:40" on phones), then the date, full date and time on hover; `ago()` in `admin.js`; every admin time is 24 h whatever the browser's language (`dateText()`); sorted by that time, the default sort, most recent first, and one tap on Last brings it back), overall level (photo Elo `ras`, one Elo column on the players' scale, 1000 + 200 × level ± its margin: the sheet's chain, not the app's `~level` players see since v63, Q134; the separate Level column, the same number, was dropped on 2026-11-03, Pierre), answers, right %, last 50, days, progress %, learned, seen, **Photos** and **Spread** (sheet v81, Pierre 2026-11-06, Q191, the photo exposure study of 2026-11-06 §5: in the subject of the admin's switch, `ex` of `Code.gs` `users_`, computed by `exposure_` with `core.js` `exposureOf` over that subject's rows (`subjRows`) against its game photos per unit grouped by colony (`pools_`, `core.js` `unitPool` over the site's files `SUBJECTS` `pool` names: `slots` = data.json + curation.json through `applyCuration` / `applySlots` / `gameOf`, `entries` = the fish files through `fishPhotos` / `fishGen`, units under the ids the log holds, `renamedId`; read from GitHub main, cached 6 hours per subject, nothing stored). Photos = Seen %, the distinct game photos shown out of the game photos of the units met; Spread = Spread %, in time order, of the questions on a unit asked while it still had game colonies the player had never been shown, the share that showed one (a photo out of play now counts as a colony of its own); the cells' titles give the counts; `expoOn`, `EXPO_SHEET`; the fish genus units, built in `app.js` `fishGenera`, are not in the pool yet), reports; **Progress and Learned are the full replay** (`progOf` in `admin.js`, 2026-11-03, Pierre: the admin shows the values the player will have, not the stale copy of a player who has not reopened the app): the player's answers in the log replayed with today's rules (`core.js` `replayProgress`, with the sheet's photo memory offsets and pooled confusions from `?stats=1`), the mastery exams taken from their saved progress (`replayedOf`: not one passed under the former model), read with `userData` 4 at a time (`savedOf`, `PSAVED`, also the Journey's cards) and drawn again when the last one is in; **from sheet v42** (`RP_SHEET`, `rpOn`; Pierre, 2026-11-04: 40 players took about 15 s on a phone) Players reads none: the player list carries each player's same replay computed by the sheet once (`Code.gs` `replayedAll_`, `rp` = [progress %, learned, the saved copy's %] over the slate, the replay record reused from the Replayed tab while the player's rows, reset and first-launch answer are the same), and only the Journey (`progOf(u, true)`, which shows the record genus by genus) still reads one saved copy and replays here; a player the sheet gave no `rp` is replayed here as before; computed once per data load, memoized (`PROGM`); where the saved copy differs (its % as the leaderboard computes it, the sheet's Learned column), a small muted "↻ saved 44%" beside the value (`withSaved`, `.adm-saved`, tooltip: the player has not opened the app since), in the table, the row's bar and the Journey; the anonymous ids get their replayed % too; every column title sorts (`UCOLS`, `SF.us`/`SF.ud`), "you" marks the synced name; a player hidden from the leaderboard has a greyed name (muted, italic, "hidden from the leaderboard" in its title; no tag since 2026-11-03, Pierre: the tag widened the Name column); answers without a name (devices that never signed in, deleted accounts) are listed apart by anonymous id; a row opens a **Journey →** button (games, mix-ups, genera to review and days played are on the Journey page only; Pierre, 2026-11-02: one place for a player's path) and Manage: leaderboard **Hide** / **Show** (sheet v20, an admin's hide the player's syncs never undo), **Merge into** another name (progress merged in the browser with `core.js` `mergeUsers`, then `userMerge` relabels answers and reports and deletes the old name) and **Delete…** (sheet v55: two named choices, no default, Q89: **Delete everything**, or **Delete, keep answers anonymously** as `~deleted <id>`, pooled photo statistics only; `uDel`, `DEL_SHEET`); both confirm), **Journey** `#players/list/journey/<name>` (`admin-journey.js`, Pierre, 2026-11-02, the first RAs playing: one player's path, from the answer log the Players page already loads, joined by the anonymous id; from sheet v30 also the genus sheets they opened, "Pages opened" under the sync section): numbers (answers, right, last 50, level, genera met / slate, progress and learned replayed from the log as on Players, with "↻ saved …" when the saved copy differs, days, sessions), a Progress column in the genera table (each genus's replayed bar, `core.js` `scoreOf`), the learned genera from the replay, right answers over the last 20 against the chance the game expected (the logged `e`: photo difficulty and their level on the genus), genera met over the answers (a dot at each genus's first question, a line at each new day), when they played (2026-11-03, Pierre: the day alone did not show when a player really played; one row a day from the first to the last, days without an answer included, one cell an hour in the admin's time zone, shaded in 5 steps of the level ramp against their busiest hour, answers and right share on hover; the last `JOURNEY.DAYS` days), the genera easiest and hardest for them ranked by right answers minus expected (3 answers or more: raw rates mislead, the game hands harder photos to whoever does well), the first-meeting cards from the player's saved progress (`userData`, admin token: the first cards and when, single cards, Open all genera after which answer, when and from where, or the offer declined; an orange line on the genera chart; a Card column, Pierre 2026-11-02), the genera learned (mastery exam passed, from the saved progress: a green ★ curve on the genera chart at the answer that passed its mastery exam, its last right exam question logged `|x` (Pierre: that answer is known); a green "learned" tag in the list), their mix-ups, genera to review and the slate genera not met yet, the games (photo quiz, "Again: A or B?" rematches after a mix-up, mastery exam questions, Look-alike duels), every genus asked (order met, easiest, hardest, most asked; last 10 answers as dots, their most frequent wrong answers), and answers per day. Charts are drawn at the page's width, never scaled down, so their labels keep the 12 px minimum (`--fs-xs`) on phones too; help behind "?" (`HELP.journey`). Activity `#players/activity`, Difficulty `#photos/stats/difficulty` (by genus: one lane per view, a dot per photo sized by its answers, the lane's median, a box and whiskers from 5 photos; genera and tables by median, Pierre 2026-11-02; and **Far harder than their genus**, Pierre 2026-11-06, Q192: the photos in play whose own part of the difficulty, the `?stats=1` difficulty minus the genus offset `gd` (sheet v31), is `FAR.D` = 1.5 logits or more after `FAR.N` = 5 answers or more, the study's threshold, the farthest first, each opening the photo: `admin.js` `farHarder`; at a genus recognised 4 times in 5 such a photo is about 1 time in 2; `tests/farharder.test.js`), Confusions `#analyses/difficulty/pairs`, Photo list `#photos/review/by-difficulty`, and the to-do views Where to look `#inbox/coverage/games?look=all` (the banner of Games, open), Games `#inbox/coverage/games`, Sheets & ID Key `#inbox/coverage/sheets-id-key`, Health `#inbox/todo/health` (Games has a tap-to-open Legend of its notations, Pierre 2026-11-02; its + column gives the candidates by source, one coloured line each: Dauin, PH, Own, Other (iNaturalist elsewhere)). The crop editor keeps the photo 40 px from the screen's sides on touch screens (12 px with a mouse), so a crop dragged to the edge no longer sets off the phone's back / forward swipe (Pierre, 2026-11-02). **Photo rotation** (Pierre, 2026-11-07 "not now", 2026-11-08 "là maintenant c'est le moment"; every subject): wherever the crop editor is offered (a genus page's Crop, the Candidates swiper's, the fish Candidates'), its second row turns the photo a quarter left (⟲) or right (⟳), or back upright (0°, off while upright); the photo and its frame turn together (a CSS rotate of the editor's box, sized so the turned photo fits, a drag turned back into the photo's axes: `autosort.js` `rotDelta`), and Save writes the crop and the rotation as one Undo step: `curation.json` `rot{f: 90|180|270}` (degrees clockwise; upright = no entry: `rotSet`), `fish.rot` for a fish photo, through the draft and Publish (`merge3`, rule A1). The Crop button is on for a photo cropped or turned. **One composition**: the crop stays in fractions of the original photo, cut first, and the cut is then turned (`tools/photos.py` `turn`; `autosort.js` `photoAt` maps a point of the original into the file, `turnedSize` its size, and `tests/rotate.test.js` checks the workflow's files against them on a fixture image), so turning a photo never changes its crop (the crop box turns with it) and the judging pass's proposed crops stay valid. **The previews** draw a draft rotation at once: `crSrc` (also behind `admin-fish.js` `fishSrc` and `fuP`, so a fish photo in play is handled like a coral one) shows the file as it is when it already has the draft's crop and rotation baked in (`cr`, `ro`), else the original with the crop (`object-view-box`) and `data-rot`, which `rotFit` turns by CSS once the photo has loaded, again when its box changes size (a `ResizeObserver`): a tile's photo (object-fit cover) turned a quarter gets its image box swapped before it is turned (`rotCover`), so it shows exactly the part of the photo the turned file will show in that tile, and a half-turn is turned in its own box; a whole photo (the lightbox, the swiper) is scaled to stay whole (`rotScale`). The Draft title says "rotations", Publish's summary counts "N rotation(s)" and "N fish photo rotation(s)". **The files**: the Candidate photos workflow (`approved-photos.yml`) makes them again from the original when the rotation changes, as for a crop (`tools/fetch_approved.py` for the corals, `tools/fish_game.py` for the fish, both through `photos.write` and `photos.stale`), the four sizes uploaded to R2, the record's `ro` (with `cr`) saying what its files were made with, `iv` bumped so no cache serves the old file, `ar` measured on the turned file; the app shows the files as they are (it turns a photo itself only when R2 fails and the photo comes from its online original: "Served from Cloudflare R2"). The header groups never get cut off: when they don't all fit on one line, `fitModes()` swaps them for the menu used on phones (`body.adm-narrow`). The "?" help has two tabs: this page, and **Start here** (`HELP.start`: workflow, what reaches the site, what is permanent, who to ask), shown first until a browser has opened it once. Publish commit messages end with `(by <GitHub login>)`. Publish first shows a summary (`pubSummary()`: counts per kind of change, genera touched) and asks to confirm; "Validate N" (Forms) asks too. `?readonly` keeps no draft (clicks live in the tab only). Excluded photos gone from `data.json` show an iNat thumbnail from the photo id in their name. Choices naming a missing photo can be dropped per genus with "Forget them" (one Undo step); excluded photos never count as missing.

- **Out of the statistics** (sheet v115, `EXCL_SHEET`; Pierre, 2026-11-08, ROADMAP "Security follow-ups"): Players › List, in a player's bar (**Leave out** / **Count again**, a muted **out** beside the name) and in Not signed in (a Statistics column), one click each way (`statsExclude`, `isExcl`, `exclBtn`; the ids from the player list's `excluded`). The sheet keeps the ids in the tab "Stats excluded"; their answers rate nothing in `stats_` and the Analyses report drops them (`tools/analyses.js`, `readAnswers` `excluded`); docs/SECURITY.md "Left out of the statistics".
- Every click is a local draft (`reefid-curation-draft`, with its base `reefid-curation-base`; Undo/Redo, Ctrl+Z). Two admin tabs in one browser share it: every write is tagged `_tab: {rev, seen}` (this tab's revision, and the revision it had last seen), and before each write and on each storage event a tab merges the other's draft onto its own (`takeTab()`/`mergeTabs()` in `core.js`, a `merge3` against the exact draft the other tab started from, so clicks made in both tabs at once both survive) and takes a newly published base, so neither tab erases the other's clicks. The A click's cost (performance pass, 2026-11-09): `putDraft` keeps and stores the draft from its text `snap` (`draftJson`), and `autoWhen` skips the fish part's keeps when the click left that part as it was (the same object: the admin never writes into it, `tests/adminclick.test.js`); the fish auto-sort at load (`fishAutoRun`, which reads `fish-cand.json`) runs once the first page is drawn and the page is idle (`requestIdleCallback`, 4 s at most). Undo steps (the last 60, as many as fit in `HIST_ROOM`, 4.5 million characters: with a draft over a million characters, one or two) are kept in `sessionStorage` `reefid-admin-undo` with the draft they lead to (`keepHist`, written once the clicks pause for `HIST_WAIT`, 1.5 s, and when the page is hidden or left), so they survive a reload of the tab (re-based like the draft) and are dropped if another tab changed the draft. **Publish** commits `curation.json` to `main` through the GitHub Contents API with a fine-grained token (this repo only, Contents read/write), stored encrypted in the browser with the admin password (`reefid-admin-vault`). Once unlocked, the plain token is kept only in `sessionStorage` (this tab, until the browser closes), never in `localStorage`; the token link asks for a 90-day expiry. Publish re-reads `curation.json` (never from the HTTP cache), merges with `merge3`, retries once by itself on a 409, and names in its toast any published choice the draft replaced. `admin.html?readonly` works without a token (download JSON); the lock screen links to it. `admin.html?demo` (Pierre, 2026-11-01: agents must see and test the admin) opens every page without a token: `admin-demo.js` (loaded before `admin.js`, inert without `?demo`) replaces `fetch` for the sync script and the GitHub API with in-memory replies shaped like `Code.gs` (`answers_`, `stats_` via `core.js` `eloOf`/`confusionOf`, `users_` with merge and delete, `reports_`) and GitHub's (curation.json from the site, Publish kept in memory), on 7 made-up players (~1150 answers, seeded so every run is the same), a few anonymous answers and 5 reports; any other outside call is refused. `admin.js` `DEMO` prefixes its storage keys with `demo:` so a demo never touches a real draft, vault or Undo history, and hides Preview draft; a banner says it is a demo. Collaborators who cannot scope a fine-grained token to this personal repo are pointed to a classic token (`repo` scope). `index.html?draft` previews unpublished choices.
- **Keys on Quality and Growth form** (`tileKeys`): 1 2 3, or B E T U M S F, X and P, act only on the photo that has the keyboard focus (clicked, or reached with Tab; ringed, `.adm-qt:focus-within`), never on one the mouse merely passes over, and never with Ctrl, Alt or Cmd, a held-down key or the enlarged photo open; a tile keeps the focus when its buttons are redrawn (`keepFocus`).
- **No page titles**: an admin page never repeats its place in the header (group, tab, view) as a heading; headings name the sections of a page (with their counts), a genus or a player (Journey).
- **Key-by-key merge** (PR #240): Publish first re-reads the published file; if it changed, `rebase()` applies `merge3(base, draft, published)`: a key Pierre changed since the base keeps his value, every other key takes the published one; objects merge recursively, lists and plain values are single choices (same key changed on both sides: the draft wins). The draft is also merged onto the published file on page load. Old drafts without a base go through `mergeOld()` (adds, never removes). Never go back to publishing a whole copy.
- **Growth form** (`renderForm`): one row per IMR form; each photo has the form buttons, ✿ polyps and, on colony and close-up photos the tiles can show, **📌** (`togglePin`): a pinned photo leads its form's tile on the Coral ID Key start, then the tile fills automatically. Up to 3 per form in click order (a 4th drops the oldest); giving the photo another form unpins it in the same undo step (a ✿ click never does). `proto/idkey.js` `pinLead()` applies the pins to the IMR form tile and to the Coral Finder tile whose `forms.json` `cf` starts with that letter (never Meandering). **Agreement with the automatic guess** (Pierre, 2026-11-03: he validates forms by hand to see whether the detection is good): a line at the top (`#fagree`, `formAgree()`, `agreeFill()`), "Your forms: N set · agreement with the automatic guess: X% (of N where both exist) · unsure guesses: M", over the photos the Show, View and genus filters leave (`FSCOPE`; Not checked yet and Polyps out don't change it), form letters only (✿ apart): his form (the draft's curation `gf`) against the dashed guess (`AUTOF`: `forms.json` `gf`, or the judging pass's `fm` for a candidate); unsure = `gunsure` guesses still on the guess. Nothing new is recorded; a click refreshes it in place (`agreeRefresh()` from `formTile`); ✓ Validate N counts as agreeing. A `<details>` opens a guess × yours table (diagonal = agreed, per-guess agreement), its title lists the same pairs on hover. **Guesses first** (`FF.gfirst`, a checkbox beside Not checked yet): in each row the photos still on a guess first, unsure ones first, the ones he set last (`fRank`).
- **Photo stats › Overview** `#photos/stats/overview` (`renderPstats`, `poolStats`; Pierre, 2026-11-03: "general stats on all photos: how many in play, roughly how many per genus and per zoom level, a median and a spread, not a box plot"): computed in the browser from what the admin already has, no new sheet call (the photo ratings `?stats=1` alone, `loadStats(true)`, not the answer log). Headline: photos in play (the games' pool, `gamePool` over the game genera and their views, as Coverage › Games), colonies in play (distinct observations), game genera with at least one photo, candidates waiting. **All photos**: every photo the admin knows by state, each counted once (in play, not in games with the first reason that applies, comparison only = sent to Not this genus › Look-alike comparison, curation `out` `'cmp'`, own photos and candidates alike, which `photos()`/`cands()` skip; removed, candidates waiting, rejected, excluded) and the total. **Per genus**: photos and colonies in play per game genus, median [p25–p75] (`core.js` `distOf`, `quantile`: linear between neighbours), range, mean, and a bar chart of the genera by photos in play (round bands, a genus with none counts 0); **Fewest and most** (5 each, links to the genus page). **Views**: photos in play per view, share, per-genus median [middle half] over the genera that have the view (a 2-level genus has no Close-up), genera without any. **Sources** (IMR lecture, Pierre, iNat Dauin / Philippines / elsewhere; the book is never in games): total, share, per-genus spread, genera with any. **Answers per photo**: answers that rated each photo in play (as Difficulty), in bands (none, 1-2, 3-9, 10-29, 30+), median of all and of the rated ones, and how many have 3 or more; **3+ answers per genus** (Pierre, 2026-11-06, Q192; the photo exposure study of 2026-11-06 §4, §7.6: per-photo ratings cannot converge at this traffic, so the goal is the first 3 to 5 answers per photo): per game genus its photos in play, those with `RATE.MIN` = 3 answers or more and their share, the least covered first, and how many are far harder than the genus (`farHarder`, linked to Difficulty). The page shows numbers, labels and column headers only (rule A2); what each row, column and spread means is in its ? help (`HELP.pstats`). `tests/admin.smoke.js` checks the numbers against `covOf` and a plain median at 390 and 1280 px, and that marking a photo in play and a candidate comparison only moves them to that row with the total unchanged (and Undo puts them back).
- **Look-alikes** (`renderMont`): one row per classic pair (same list and automatic pick as `app.js` `classics()` / `montage()`). Per pair: View Colony / Close-up / Corallites / **All** (photos of every zoom, the automatic pick prefers the nearest zooms), **Auto | Manual** (clicking a photo makes it Manual; Auto drops his view and photo picks but keeps a hide), **Shown | Hidden** (Hidden keeps the comparison off the public Look-alikes page until good examples exist). Stored in `curation.json` `mont`.
- **Candidates** `#photos/candidates` (default view on phones, `renderCand`): one pending candidate per screen (iNat candidates from `data.json` `cand`), source filter Pierre / Dauin / PH / All (default Dauin; a source with nothing left gives way to the first one, in that order, that still has candidates). **Game genera first** (Pierre, 2026-11-03, `isGame`, `GAME` = `core.js` `gameOf`: the slate, Millepora, the bonus genera): every candidate of a game genus of the source comes before any candidate of a Key-only genus, which come after in their own weighted draw (`candQueue` sorts on `game` first; the no-two-in-a-row step never pulls one forward). Key-only genera get **no Corallites candidates** (`capViews`: Colony and Close-up only; a 2-level genus's macro view is its Close-up, so it stays): a candidate guessed macro (`vg`) is not proposed and the swiper has no + Corallites button for them; their macro photos already accepted stay (sheet, Coral ID Key). On the real data (PH tab, fixed seed): Key-only candidates in the first 50 went from 24 to 0, the first one now comes after the 1110 game candidates. Order inside each block = weighted random favouring genus × view cells with few game photos (weight 1/(1+count)³ from `covOf`, cubed since 2026-11-01 so thin cells come first: in the first 50 of All, cells with 0-2 colonies went from 12 to 30 and cells with 8+ from 11 to 2; x3 when the photo completes a game colony), never long runs of one genus; shuffle seed in `reefid-admin-cseed`. Buttons only (a swipe gesture misfired when zooming, declined): ✕ Reject (`seen['cand:f']`, a reviewed mark; the photo stays a candidate on its genus page, tagged "rejected"), + Colony / Close-up / Corallites (adds it), Skip, Not this, × exclude, Crop (`crop`, applied by `fetch_approved.py` on download). Claude's view guess (`vg`) is a dashed outline, never a green tick (it looked like Pierre's choice). Auto-refilled candidates arrive without growth-form/quality guesses.
- **Auto-sort** (Pierre, 2026-11-03: "take the obvious off my hands"; `autosort.js`, pure and tested in `tests/autosort.test.js`, loaded by `admin.html` only; admin `autoRun`, `autoBlock`, `autoUndo`, `renderRetest`). **Where decisions are written**: in the curation draft, at each load of the admin by someone who can publish (not read-only), exactly as Pierre's clicks would write them (`genera.add`, `quality`, `gf`, `crop`; `seen['cand:f']` for a reject; `exclude[f] = 'auto'`), logged in `curation.json` `auto`, and sent by his Publish (merged key by key, `merge3`). Why the draft and not a tool committing `curation.json`: the same path and safeguards as his own clicks (the merge onto the latest file, the Publish summary that counts "auto-sort decisions", Undo), one implementation that both decides and shows the agreement, and no PR that changes `curation.json` (rule A1); nothing reaches the site before his Publish.
