One overlay at a time (new-player walkthrough 2026-11-02, `pages.smoke.js` checks each): an achievement pop-up waits while a card or the tour is open, and a card that opens over one sends it back to wait (`achBlocked()`, `achHold()` in `progress.js`); leaving the page a card opened on (Back, a tab, a link; not a redraw of the same page) closes it and its keys (`route()` calls each card's `ulClose`), and first-meeting cards not shown yet come next time. **Every card comes from one builder**, `app.js` `cardShow` (2026-11-03, docs/DESIGN.md "Cards"): the `.unlock` overlay with role dialog, `aria-modal`, labelled by its kicker and title, the focus on its main button, kept inside on Tab and given back on close, Escape / Enter / a tap outside as each card says (`esc`, `enter`, `tap`: as its main or secondary button, or a function), the quiz's answer keys never reaching the question behind, the tour's keys passing while it waits under the tour, `ulClose` for `route()`, and on close `qclockAgain` (the time to answer counts from the card's close). The callers keep their own rules (when a card is due, waiting while `#tour, .unlock` is up, what a button does): `quiz.js` `introShow` / `introCard`, `introOfferShow`, `backShow`, `bonusOfferShow`; `progress.js` `recalcNotice`, `sorryNotice` (the apology card); `sync.js` `signinNudge`. `pages.smoke.js` opens every kind and checks the dialog, its title, the focus trap, Escape and the focus return.
- **The subject to start with** (Pierre, 2026-11-06: new players choose Corals or Fish, no more defaulting to Corals; the tour is no longer a coral tour for them). `tour.js` `plan()` works out the steps (`DEF`, keyed `k`) at each step. A player with one subject (`app.js` `subjMine`: everyone but the admin and the fish group while fish has `open: 'group'`) gets the six steps as before, with no word of the other subject. A player with two subjects or more gets **pick** first, a forced choice like the first-launch question (no Skip, no Next until answered; Escape closes the tour, the subject stays as it was): "What do you want to start with?", a line saying the app has that many subjects, each with its own progress, switchable any time, and one `.startopt.tpick` button per subject (its icon, name, *beta* tag, `UNITS[id].pick` line). The pick is kept (`SUBJECT_KEY`, synced) and `setSubject` loads the subject; then **ask** (that subject's first-launch question), **swap** (spotlights the Home subject row `.hsubj`, else the header chip: "Corals or Fish, any time"), then doors, sign-in, games, progress, help in that subject's words (`UNITS[id].tour[k]` `{t, d}`; the steps' own words are the corals'). A subject that opens by signing in during the tour (a group member on a new device: the sheet's reply names the group, the page reloads on the sign-in step) adds pick, the new subject's question when it differs from the one asked, and swap right after the sign-in step, the earlier steps unchanged; what the tour started with is kept for that reload (`sessionStorage` `reefid-tour-open` `{two, s}`). With `open: 'all'` every new player gets the two-subject tour with no code change. `tests/pages.smoke.js` part `subjtour`.
- **The apology card** (`progress.js` `sorryNotice`, `core.js` `SORRY`, `sorryOf`, `sorryFelt`, `sorryText`; Pierre, 2026-11-04): one card, at app open, to the players who felt the opening of #408 run unchecked (merged 2026-11-03 11:34 UTC; shown until `SORRY.UNTIL`, 2026-11-17). What the device holds when this version first runs on it is read once and kept (`reefid-sorry-first` {t, n, h, c}): n, the answers between `SORRY.FROM` and then (the answers still in `LOG0` / `SYNC.log`, else the answers since the last card shown before the window); h, the opening hold's sum (`reefid-hold`); c, the most genera carded within 15 answers in the window. Due with n ≥ `SORRY.N` 20 and h ≥ `SORRY.H` 3, or h ≥ 1.5 with c ≥ `SORRY.CARDS` 4. "Hi Ana! Sorry about your last run." (`core.js` `firstName`: the first word of the sign-in name, capitalised, else "Hi!"; rule U10) then "Yesterday evening we adjusted how the app picks your questions, and some of it wasn't right yet: too many new genera came at once, and the questions didn't get easier after misses. It's fixed now. Sorry for the rough ride!" (the day said in the player's clock: This / Yesterday evening, else the date). Button "Thanks" or Escape: `reefid-sorry` (this device only, like `reefid-recalc`). One card at a time like `recalcNotice`, after it.
- **Progress recalculated for everyone, and new game modes announced** (`RECALC`/`recalcNotice` in `progress.js`, `reefid-recalc`; Pierre, 2026-11-02; the typed questions announced the same way on 2026-11-03, "Think you know your genera? Think again.", text validated by Pierre, with its own kicker and button: a note's `k`, `b`; each note has a `kind`, `update` for a recalculation (the card's medallion is then the player's progress ring now) or `feature` for a new game mode (✨), and the latest due gives the card its kind): one pop-up per device, at the first moment no tour or card is open, to a player who already has answers: the notes of `RECALC.L` not seen yet ("We've improved how progress is measured", 2026-11-02; "*Millepora* now counts in your progress", 2026-11-02, when Millepora joined the slate and the % went over 40 genera), latest first, in plain words with no algorithm details. `reefid-recalc` holds the id of the last note seen, so a player who saw the first sees only the second, once. Marked seen by its button or Escape; leaving the page closes it unseen. A later recalculation adds a note (new `id`, title, text) at the end of `RECALC.L`.
- **Out-of-game prompts spaced out** (`promptOk`/`promptMark` in `quiz.js`, `reefid-prompt-at`; "fewer, better pop-ups", Pierre 2026-11-02): the sign-in card (from 20 answers), the one-tap question (`PULSE.FIRST` 35), the fallback card of the first-launch question (at 20, 40 or 60 answers, `startDue`) and the card offering to go back to one at a time after Open all (`backDue`) come at least `PROMPT.GAP` (10) answers apart, never with a card, the tour or an achievement toast on screen or waiting (`overlayBusy()`); one that falls too close waits. A right answer gets a ~400 ms celebration on the choice itself and on "✓ Yes" (`.cheer` in `app.css`; none with reduced motion); Next is there at once.
- **One-tap questions** (`quiz.js` `PULSE`, `pulseDue`, `pulseAsk`; the bank in `core.js` `PULSEQ`, `pulsePick`, `pulseScore`, shared with the admin; `reefid-pulse` = {at: answers when the last one showed, q: its item, left: the round's items still to ask, x: 1 after "Not now", v: the current session {t0: its first answer's time, t: its last, n: its answers}}; Pierre 2026-11-02, varied 2026-11-03: "ask slightly different questions about roughly the same concept"): after an answer in the photo quiz (the custom quiz too, `photoQuiz`) and duels, **a card at Next, before the next question is drawn** (Pierre, 2026-11-03, after a long run: "in a flow state, used to clicking Next, I don't see them"; 3 of his first 4 prompts, then a block in the feedback, ended with Next and no tap; before that, a row inside the feedback was missed too). The answer arms Next (`pulseArm`) when the prompt is due; at the tap it is checked again (a card or a toast that came meanwhile: no prompt, nothing marked, it stays due), then marked (`promptMark`) and shown (`pulseAsk`) with `cardShow` kind `feedback` (💬): kicker "Quick question", the question as the title, its three answers on one row of equal widths (`app.css` `.pulseb`), a quiet "Not now". Anti-reflex: every button is disabled (faded) for `PULSE.WAIT` (600 ms), the focus is on the question (Enter, Next's key, answers nothing), a tap outside does nothing, so the double tap or the reflex tap on Next cannot answer or dismiss it. Escape = Not now. When it closes (an answer, Not now, Escape) the next question is drawn (`route`); its clock starts with its photo as always, so the card's time is not counted. First at `PULSE.FIRST` (35) answers, then every `PULSE.EVERY` (40 since 2026-11-03, was 60: a target that depends on the level needs a few answers per level band from each player), `PULSE.AFTERX` (2) times that after a "Not now", always within the prompt spacing above. **Never early in a session** (Pierre, 2026-11-05, on his phone: "Getting boring?" came when he had barely started; until then the floor was 8 answers of the page, `session.n`, which a page left open on a phone carried over to the next day's session): only once the current session has `PULSE.SESS_N` (15) answers and has lasted `PULSE.SESS_MS` (5 min) (`pulseSess`; still going: its last answer less than 30 min ago). A session = answers less than `model.js` `SESSION.GAP` (30 min) apart, the admin's sessions (`admin.js` `HEALTH.GAP`) and the tips' visits; every answer in a game counts (`answer()` calls `pulseNote`, which keeps it in `reefid-pulse` `v` on the device: a reload is no new session). A prompt due sooner (the answers counter met in a previous session) waits for that, so no session opens on one, and the spacing in answers is unchanged: in a long session one every 40 answers as before. The same rule for every item of the bank. Six items, asked in rounds (each once, shuffled; a new round never starts with the item just asked, so the same wording never comes twice in a row; the very first is the plain "These questions are"): "These questions are" (Too easy · About right · Too hard), "Right now I feel" (Bored · In the zone · Struggling; replaced "For me, the challenge is: Too low · Just right · Too high" before release, ambiguous to non-native speakers), "Want harder photos?" (Yes · Keep it · Easier), "Having fun?" (Not really · It's OK · Yes!), "Frustrating?" (Not at all · A bit · Very), "Getting boring?" (Not at all · A bit · Yes). Each answer sits on one or two common scales (`pulseScore`): felt difficulty (−1 too easy, 0 right, +1 too hard) and feel (−1 bad, +1 good). Logged by `ev('pulse')` with `r` = the answer's code, `src` = the item id, and as before `m`, `n`, `rr`, `lv`, `met` (what the game knew when the prompt showed, the same on every event of one prompt). The response rate: each prompt also logs `r` = `shown` when the card shows, `skip` for Not now or Escape, `none` when another page is opened with the card up (Back, a link: `cardShow` closes it with `route`); a prompt left open when the app closes is counted from its showing (shown − answered − Not now). Codes unchanged since the inline block (`none` was then mostly Next). At most two events a prompt, one prompt every 40 answers: far within the sheet's caps (200 a device, 3000 in all per 6 hours). **No new sheet version**: since v19 `events_` keeps `r` as text in the Message column and `src` in the Where column (Detail keeps `r` only for easy / ok / hard, the codes the difficulty items still use with their v19 meaning); rows from before have an empty Where and count as the first item (`PULSE0`). `tests/sync.test.js` checks that v27 reads every item and code (and `shown`, `skip`, `none`) back as sent; `tests/pulse.test.js` the bank, the rotation, the spacing and the session gate (a fake clock: a new session with the counter due, slow and quick answers, a 30-minute break, a long session's cadence). Admin To do › Errors (`#inbox/todo/errors`, `pulseView`): the common scales by level band (overall level when answered: below −0.5, −0.5 to +0.5, above +0.5) and by mode with the prompts shown and the % answered, then every item's answers counted by band and mode (% of the column's answers) and under them its Not now, no answer and shown with the % answered (`pulseResp`; rows from before 2026-11-03 logged no showing and stay out of the rates). Research and how the answers will be used: docs/LEARNING.md "What the players say about the difficulty".
- **Enlarging a photo made visible** (Pierre, 2026-11-03: in the logged answers (Enlarged column, below) every player but one had enlarged a quiz photo for 0-2% of their answers, so most did not know they could; "make it clear, without nagging every time"). Every photo that opens the enlarged view in the quiz, the duels and the genus sheet carries the enlarge mark (`app.js` `ZOOMI`, docs/DESIGN.md), its button labelled "Enlarge photo". And once per device a coach mark, "Tap a photo to see it bigger" ("Click" with a mouse), points at the mark on the first photo of a photo-quiz question (`zoomHint()` in `quiz.js`, called by `quizPhoto`; `ZHINT`: from `AT` = 1 answer, so the 2nd question at the earliest, `DELAY` 600 ms after the question shows, gone at the first tap, click or key or after `MS` = 6 s). Key **`reefid-zoomhint`** (`app.js` `ZHINT_KEY`, per device, outside the `reefid-sync-*` progress keys, kept on sign-out): `"seen"` once the hint has shown, `"zoomed"` once any photo has been enlarged on the device (`zoom()` sets it); either value means it never shows (again). It never shows with the tour, a card or an achievement toast on screen or waiting (`overlayBusy()`, the gating of `promptOk` without its spacing: it asks nothing and goes by itself), nor once the question is answered or a photo enlarged: while one is up it looks again every `DELAY` and shows once it has gone, on the same question if still unanswered, else on a later one (CI on PR #410: a duel won as the first question left its "first duel won" toast up over the 2nd, and a single try skipped the hint there). Nothing in the scoring changes: the question, its clock (`QCLOCK`) and its answer are untouched. `pages.smoke.js` checks the mark and its label, the hint on a new player's 2nd question only (held under an achievement toast, then shown on the same question), not after a reload, and not after a photo was enlarged.
- **Signing in** (`signIn()` in `sync.js`, 2026-11-01): the welcome tour's step 2 holds the sign-in form (`tour.js`, a `.tsync` box filled by `bindSync()`), and a one-time card (`signinNudge()`) offers it again after `NUDGE_AT` (20; 10 before 2026-11-02) answers played without an account (`reefid-nudge`; never in the admin-token browser). The account is the app's, not a subject's (Pierre, 2026-11-04: "it's the app we want people to sign in to, not the game mode"): the 20 answers count in every subject (`sync.js` `answersAll`: each subject's `~level` ok + ko, which sign-out clears), so a fish-only player meets the card too (until 2026-11-04 it counted `reefid-log0`, which holds no fish answers). Skipping the tour before its sign-in step (Skip or Escape) shows the same card at once, without using up the 20-answer one (skipped on the sign-in step itself, the form was seen and dismissed: no card then); once signed in there, its button reads "Start playing" and opens the photo quiz. Answers played before signing in wait in `reefid-log0` (outside the `reefid-sync-*` keys, so never in the progress data) and join `SYNC.log` at sign-in, so the `Answers` tab gets them too. A new name keeps the device's progress and sends it. **A known name asks first** (Pierre, 2026-11-03: players type only their first name, so two of them can pick the same name, even the same simple code, and the second one would enter the first one's account and merge their answers into it): when the sheet answers `found`, on every device (a fresh one too: everyone confirms the account once per device), the sign-in box (the card, the tour's step, the header button: all `bindSync()`) shows "Is it you? An account named X already exists: N answers, last played D.", and how many answers of this device would join it. `core.js` `accountOf` reads the numbers from the reply (answers: `~level` ok + ko, else the genera answered; last play: `~level.at`, else the latest genus `at`, else the sheet's `updated`, said "last saved"; a missing field is left out of the sentence). No sheet change. *Yes, it's me* signs in as below; *No, it's someone else* merges, saves and sends nothing and returns to the form, the name focused, with a hint to add the surname's initial. The name field asks for "First name + initial" from the start, and a name taken with another code (`error: 'pin'`) says "Someone already uses the name X. If it isn't you, add your surname's initial (X B.). If it is you, check your code." Simple codes stay allowed (Pierre: people must remember them). **Reserved names** (Pierre, 2026-11-09): a new account whose name holds, as one of its words in any capitalisation, a member name of `Object.prototype` (`constructor`, `__proto__`, `toString`, `valueOf`, `hasOwnProperty`…) or `prototype` (`core.js` `RESERVED_NAMES`, `reservedName`) is not made: when the sheet answers `found: false` the form says "This name is reserved, please choose another." and sends nothing more; the sheet refuses it as well (from v119: a save that would create it answers `error: 'reserved'` before anything is logged, and the app then stops and says "Not saved: this name is reserved, please choose another"), and refuses an admin archive name holding one (`users_` `userArchive`). The same refusal covers a name holding anywhere the seedling or one of its look-alikes (v123, `core.js` `SEED_LOOKALIKES`: 🌱 🌿 🪴 ☘ 🍀 🍃): the seedling is the admin's own mark, which the app shows after the admin's account name (the leaderboard, below), so no player can show it. Otherwise a name is any text: trimmed, at most 40 UTF-16 code units (the form's `maxlength`, `Code.gs` `clean_`), matched case-blind (`toLowerCase`) with no Unicode normalisation, so other emoji, accents and scripts are names like any other. An account that already has such a name signs in, loads and saves as before; the sheet's maps by player have no prototype (`Object.create(null)`), so such a player counts like any other. A known name, once confirmed: the sheet's settings and quiz state win, and when `reefid-log0` held answers, what was played since the first anonymous answer is added to the sheet's (`mergeProgress(reefid-anon-base, local, sheet)`, `reefid-anon-base` = the progress just before that answer, so leftovers of an older sign-out are never counted twice or credited to another account), else the sheet's replaces it; then the page reloads and the start-up sync sends the result. **Sign out clears the device's progress** (`reefid-sync-coral-progress`, `-quiz`, `-reset`, `reefid-log0`, `reefid-nudge`, `reefid-anon-base`; a second click confirms when answers are unsent), so the next player starts empty and leftovers never get merged into someone else's account.
- Sheet "IMR Coral ID – RA progress" (Pierre's Drive). Tabs: `RAs` (one row per RA; hidden code hash + data JSON; **column layout is positional, never change it, add tabs instead**), `By genus`, `Answers` (one row per answer), `Activity` (QUERY per RA), `Reports` (v7/v8: one row per report or feedback: time, reporter, verified, reason, expected, answered, suggested genus, note, mode (game, photo, sheet or page), photos, where (v8), id), `Progress data` (v6: name, revision, progress JSON in 45,000-character chunks, because a cell holds at most 50,000; `RAs` column H keeps the JSON when it fits, else `@Progress data`).
- **`Timings` tab** (sheet v130; the storage study of 2026-11-14, step 1; Pierre, 2026-11-14, Q352: the answer log moves to Cloudflare D1 only when a measured threshold is crossed, so the threshold is measured here). **No player data**: one row per measured operation, `Time`, `Op` (a fixed name in the code, never built from a request), `Ms`, `Rows` and `Cols` (what a whole-tab read read; for a request, the `Answers` rows it saw), `Version` (the script's) and `Weight` (how many calls the row stands for); never a name, code, device, answer id or answer content (rules D2, D7: an account's delete, merge or archive has nothing to clean there). `Code.gs` `TIM`, `timStart_`, `tim_`, `timEnd_`, `timTrim_`. Measured: the whole-tab reads of `Answers` (`read Answers`, `logRows_`: `answerRows_` and the admin's readAnswers) and of its Id column (`read Answer ids`, `freshAnswers_`, `logAnswers_`), the photo stats' build (`stats build`, a cache miss of `stats_`), every player's saved data (`read Progress data`, `datas_`), the leaderboard's replays (`board warm`), and as a whole a completed `save`, an `anon save` that logged, a `load`, a public `stats` request, the admin's readAnswers (`admin answers`) and each run of the 15-minute `trigger` (`autoUpdate`). **Sampled per request**: 1 request in `TIM.EVERY` (50; a trigger run 1 in `TIM.EVERY_TRIGGER`, 16) keeps every row it measures at that weight; any other request keeps only an operation slower than `TIM.SLOW` (5 s; a trigger run `TIM.SLOW_TRIGGER`, 60 s) at weight 1; at most `TIM.ROWS` (8) rows a request. The rows wait in memory and are written at the very end of the request (`doGet` / `doPost` / `autoUpdate`'s `finally`), after every script lock is released, one `appendRow` each (atomic, so two requests ending together never write over each other), inside try/catch: a failure loses the rows, never a reply. **Bounded by the trigger** (`timTrim_`, its one writer of deletions): past `TIM.MAX` (4,000) rows the oldest rows are deleted in one `deleteRows`, back to `TIM.KEEP` (3/4) of it: the tab keeps the recent months (about 50 days at the expected 60 rows a day) at a fixed size, every kept row as it was logged, so the weighted figures stay unbiased; past twice that (a trigger that stopped) nothing more is written. Read it with `tools/timings.js` (Tools): per operation the weighted count, median, p90 and max ms, the most rows read, the trend by day (by week past 45 days), and the quota lines (the slowest call against the 6-minute limit, the trigger's minutes a day against the 90 of a consumer account; the 20,000 URL fetches a day are not measured). `tests/timings.test.js`; the tab is off in `tests/gas.js` unless a test asks for it.
- Devices on one account: every change triggers pull, three-way merge (against `SYNC.base`), then send. The app also pulls and merges (no write) on start-up, when it comes back to the screen (30 s throttle) and when back online, so progress made on another device shows up without answering a question first. Since v6 each send carries `at`, the sheet revision it merged against; if another device wrote in between, the sheet answers `stale` (answers still logged) and the app pulls, merges and sends again (at most twice).
- **Lighter saves on slow lines** (sheet v116, `sync.js` `LIGHT_SHEET`, rule D4: before it, everything as above; Pierre, 2026-11-08, ROADMAP "Lighter saves on slow lines": field use at IMR on weak connections). The device keeps the saved copy at the revision it merged against (`SYNC.sd` `{rev, d}`, as a load serves it: the new key names, no `_acct`; `sdSeen` after a load, `sdSaved` after a save, checked against the reply's `sum`), and then: (1) **no load before a save** (`skipPull`: the copy held at `SYNC.srv`, the account generation known, no answers logged from a refused save `lg`, no anonymous answers to move, no send whose reply never came, and not the page's first sync, which still loads: `repsPulled`); the held copy is merged as the load would have brought it (unchanged: `mine`); if another device saved since, the save's `at` gets `stale` **with the load itself** (`Code.gs` `load_`, read once the lock is released), before anything is counted or logged, and the app merges it and saves again at once, its answers still queued: the same three-way merge on the same copy, two requests as before; (2) **only what changed**: `dz`, `core.js` `dataDelta` against the held copy (per key; per unit inside an object such as a subject's progress), with `sum`, the checksum of the whole data (`dataSum`: every object's keys sorted, so the order a copy is spelled in does not matter); the sheet rebuilds the copy (`lightData_`, `dataPatch`) and keeps it only if its checksum is the app's, else answers `delta` before counting or logging anything (the answer caps included) and the app loads it again and sends its data whole, until the script version changes (`SYNC.nd`: never a loop of refused deltas); (3) **gzipped**: `{z: base64(gzip)}` (`CompressionStream`) when longer than 1 KB, else `{lt: …}`; wrapped either way, so a script before v116 finds no name and writes nothing; a body it cannot read answers `zip`, and the app sends it unpacked until the script version changes (`SYNC.nz`); (4) the **old key names added by the sheet** (`core.js` `withLegacy`, for an app from before the rename), the loads it serves under the new names only, and the hold's ids from `pt` only (`hold_` `light`: not the last 500); (5) a load of the copy the device holds (`have` = its revision) comes back `same: 1` without it (`syncGet` gives it the copy held when it asked); (6) on a slow line (the last sync took more than `SAVE_WAIT.SLOW` 8 s), the save waits twice that, at most `SAVE_WAIT.MAX` 20 s, counted from the first change not sent (`syncSoon`; leaving the app sends it at once). Measured with `node tools/syncperf.js` (the app's sync.js on the in-memory script, 1,500 answers of history, 35 KB of progress): one device, per answer, 2 requests (4 HTTP round trips) and about 81 KB up and 92 KB down (25 KB gzipped) before, 1 request (2 round trips), 2.6 KB up and 0.9 KB down after; at the field link's 86 kbit/s and 400 ms, about 11.5 s on the line per answer before, 1.1 s after. Answers logged from a refused save (`lg`) keep the replay asked for (rules version 0) and adopted again at every load and save until a save carries them (`lgFor`); until v116 a subject's adoption stopped it (`lgOk`), which let another device's save in between drop them. A save whose fate is unknown sets the same flag (`fateUnknown`, 2026-11-09): its reply never came (the line, a request cut by the background, no answer in `SAVE.TIMEOUT`, a reply that is not the sheet's JSON) or the script failed in it (`server`); the sheet may have logged and written it, and another device saved over it since, so this device no longer finds its own copy at its next load, and the plain merge counted that save's answers twice (8 to 11 runs in 40 of the property tests with lost replies, classic and light); the replay of the log counts each once by its id, at the cost of a replay in the next load's and save's replies. A subject whose replay that load cannot adopt (no replay in the reply, an answer queued here older than the log's last one, the fish units not in yet) waits instead of merging, its rules version back to 0 (`mergeSubject` `waitRp`, the corals as the fish since 2026-11-09): a double count, once in the progress, stays for good, since a replay keeps a unit from the device when it holds more answers than the log (`core.js` `replayedOf`). Tested in `tests/lightsave.test.js` (with a property test of light devices, a classic one and an older app on one account), the harness in `tests/syncpage.js`.
- **A save that fails** (Pierre, 2026-11-05: the red "Not saved" came up "a bit too often"; `sync.js` `SAVE`, `sheetCall`, `saveFailed`, `saveOk`, `saveRetry`; `tests/savefail.test.js`). The Events tab held 26 "save failed: Failed to fetch" (Chrome) / "Load failed" (Safari) from 2026-11-02 to 11-05, 15 from one installed phone, on every page, each shown in red at once: a failed sync set `SYNC.err` at its first failure and was tried again only at the next answer, the next return to the app or back online. Two causes the code shows: (1) **the sheet's own failures looked like network failures**: an exception thrown out of `doGet` / `doPost` (above all `lock.waitLock(10000)` timing out, the script lock held by other players' saves, anonymous sends and event batches, each save holding it through its hold and full replay) made Apps Script answer with its HTML error page, which has no CORS header, so the browser hid it and the app saw "Failed to fetch"; fixed by sheet v50 (every reply is JSON: `lock` or `server`, the save's hold, groups and replay computed after the lock is released); (2) **a request cut by the app going to the background** (iOS and Android stop a hidden page's network; a sync runs 3 s after each answer, a GET and a POST each carrying the whole progress, several seconds at 86 kbit/s) counted as a failure. Now: a failed try of a passing kind (no reply, no answer within `SAVE.TIMEOUT` 90 s, a reply that is not JSON, or the sheet's `busy`, `stale` again, `lock`, `server`, `save`) is tried again by itself after `SAVE.RETRY` (5, 10, 20, 40, 60, 120, then every 300 s) while the app is in view and online; a try during which the app went to the background (`visibilitychange` hidden, `pagehide`, `freeze`) is not counted and is tried again when the app comes back (at once, without the 30 s pull throttle); leaving the app sends a save still waiting for its 3 s timer. **"Not saved" shows only once saving has failed `SAVE.RED_N` (3) counted tries in a row over `SAVE.RED_MS` (60 s) at least** (with the retries above: at the 5th try, 75 s after the first failure); `pin`, `wait` and `gone` stay red at once. The answers stay queued meanwhile and the whole queue goes at the next try. The run of failures is kept in the account record (`SYNC.fail`: `n` counted, `hid` cut by the background, `at` first failure, `why`, `d`), so a reload keeps counting. Events (kind `sync`): `save failed: <error>` when the red shows, `save recovered: <error>` when a run with a counted failure ends with a save (the player saw nothing); Where says what the last failed try did, e.g. `load · no reply · 0.4 s · try 5 · 76 s · 0 hidden` (step: `load` the pull before the send, `save` the send; the HTTP status when a reply came; its seconds; the tries; seconds since the first failure; tries cut by the background). The sheet's errors read as `sheet said <error>`. The admin's Errors page counts only `save failed` as failed saves and shows the last try; To do leaves out `save recovered`.
- Endpoints: `POST` progress + answer log; `GET ?name=&pin=` load (reply carries `v`, the script version, and from v23 `hold`, the opening hold; from v28, when the request's `rv` is below the script's rules version, `replay`: see "Full replay" below); `GET ?stats=1` photo Elo (`photos`, `conf`, and from v26 the photo memory offsets `mem` with their answers `memn`; v27 rates typed answers `|t` with no guess rate; from v31 the genus offsets `gd` of the hierarchical photo Elo, `{genus: [offset, weighed answers]}`, `photos` carrying each photo's total; from v68 the per-format offsets `fmt`, `{format: [offset, answers]}`, `core.js` `fmtOffsetsOf`; stats cache `stats15`, `stats17` from v68); `GET ?answers=1` (admin-only from v14: `POST {readAnswers, token, aname, apin}`; and `POST {readStats, token}` for `stats_` with the per-player `ras`, the public `?stats=1` keeping only `photos` and `conf`, so a leaderboard name can't be matched to an answer history) anonymous answers for admin Stats (v6: with `&name=&pin=` the reply's `me` is the caller's own anonymous id; the salt is secret, in Script Properties `ANON_SALT`). `POST {reports, rname, rpin}` (v7) players' reports, logged once per id; the reporter goes in `rname`/`rpin`, never `name`/`pin`, so an older script refuses it (`input`) instead of taking it for an empty progress write; `Verified` = 1 when the code matches the sync account. `POST {readReports: 1, token}` (v8) the reports with names, newest first, for admins only: the script checks the admin page's GitHub token with GitHub (`UrlFetchApp`, write access to the repo), remembers a valid one 10 minutes by its hash, never stores it. That permission (`script.external_request`) is NOT asked by a redeploy nor by running `doGet`: grant it by running, once in the editor, a function that calls `UrlFetchApp` (e.g. `function autoriser(){ UrlFetchApp.fetch('https://api.github.com'); }`) and accepting the prompt (done 2026-11-01). Hardening (v6): user strings never become formulas (`safe_`), answer fields are pattern-checked, 5 wrong codes for a name block it for 10 minutes (`wait`), answers are logged before the progress write.
- **Versioning and deploy**: every change gets the next `VERSION` (header comment + constant), assigned by the integrator at merge, never by the PR (rule D3; Pierre, 2026-11-08, Q305): the PR writes its history entry in `tools/sync/changes/<branch>.md`, and `tools/sheet_version.py --assign` folds it into the header line and the version history below and bumps `VERSION`. From v11 the script updates itself (`autoUpdate`, a 15-minute time trigger made once by `installAutoUpdate`): when `tools/sync/Code.gs` on `main` has a higher `VERSION` (and still contains `autoUpdate`), it replaces its own code and manifest (`tools/sync/appsscript.json`) through the Apps Script API with its own OAuth token, saves a version and points the web app deployment at it (same `/exec` URL); the `Deploy log` tab records each install or error. `installAutoUpdate` checks the API at once (a standard Cloud project with the Apps Script API may be needed, HANDOVER). If the code was replaced but the deployment failed, Script Property `AUTO_PENDING` makes the next run finish it. One-time setup by Pierre in HANDOVER. Before that, or if it fails: Pierre copies <https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ppericard/coral-id-imr/main/tools/sync/Code.gs> into Extensions > Apps Script, then Deploy > Manage deployments > Edit > New version.
- `Answers` columns: Name, Time, Genus shown, Answered, Right, Mode, Photos (space-separated file stems), Id, Expected, Ability, New photos, Choices (v10: the genera offered, space-separated), Seconds, Enlarged (v23, see below), UTC offset (v44, Pierre 2026-11-04: the device's offset when the answer was given, minutes east of UTC, `tz`; logged only, `readAnswers` index 14, for the time of day at home; the rows logged before got +480 once, by the one-shot `ONCE_` tz, as every player so far was on Philippine time; the app sends it without a `*_SHEET` gate because an older script ignores fields it does not know), Typed and Retyped right (v59, a player's idea approved by Pierre 2026-11-05, the name study's question g: a typed answer's raw text as first typed, `ty`, up to 40 characters, control characters out, never a formula; and its spelling step, `tr`: 1 a close typo of the right name retyped exactly, 0 retyped wrong or skipped, blank when none was asked; logged only, `readAnswers` indexes 15-16; no gate, as for the UTC offset), Scoring (v61, Pierre 2026-11-06, Q132: what the app scored the answer with, `core.js` `scoreRec` as JSON: photo difficulty, memory, mix-ups term, look-alike test, recalls, the corallites hint revealed after the answer, from v68 the format's offset `o`; read by its name, `SC_COL`; column W, after v60's five columns (next item); `readAnswers` index 22, an object; the replay rows' index 22 too, `rowOf_` and `logRow`, `core.js` `LOG_SC`; blank when the app sent none; no gate either; docs/LEARNING.md "One scorer"), Rejected tries (v100, Q237: the texts a typed answer refused before it, a JSON list; logged only), Device (v109, Pierre 2026-11-08, Q312: the anonymous id of the device that sent the answer, `sync.js` `devId`, the Device of its Events and Pages rows; one `dev` per save or anonymous send, written in each of its rows by `logAnswers_` (`dev_`: 8 to 16 lower-case letters and digits, else blank), signed in or not; sent only once the script's replies say v ≥ `sync.js` `DEV_SHEET` (`devField`, rule D4), blank in the rows of an older app and those logged before; served by no reader yet (`readAnswers` stops at index 22); kept through a claim at sign-in, a rename and a merge, blanked when a delete keeps the answers as `~deleted <id>` (`devBlank_`, rule D7), gone with them on a delete of everything; `tests/devanswers.test.js`, `tests/adopt.test.js`). Every reader takes them by position (`answers_`, `stats_`, `users_`, `hold_`, the Activity QUERY on A:E): new columns only ever go at the end. Mode suffixes: `|d` a duel slipped into the photo quiz, `|w` (v23, 2026-11-02 only) a question of the gentle restart (left out of the hold), `|c` a comeback question after a break (its `e` recall-adjusted; counted in the hold), `|kN` the number of plausible choices (always last but `|t` and `|x`), `|t` (v27) a typed question (the player typed the genus: rated with no guess rate, `guess_` 0; Answered is the genus read, empty for "I don't know", the raw text in Typed since v59; Choices empty), `|x` a mastery exam question.
- **Surprise cards' answers** (sheet v98, Pierre, 2026-11-08: "il faut l'enregistrer bien sûr"): the answer to a fish wink (`quiz.js` `winkShow`, `winkAnswer`) goes the normal answer path (`logAnswer`: the account's queue, or `LOG0` and the anonymous queue), one `Answers` row: Genus shown the wink unit (`f:WhaleSharks`), Answered the choice (its old entry id renamed, `core.js` `renamedId`: `t01` is `f:Damselfishes`), Right, **Mode `wink`** (`core.js` `WINK_MODE`, the marker), Photos the wink's photo, Id, Choices the four ids, Seconds and Enlarged (`QCLOCK`, `qclockArm`), UTC offset, Subject `fish`, Rank, Taxon shown/answered, Species shown; Expected, Ability, New photos and Scoring blank (nothing scored). No reader counts it: `core.js` `subjRows`/`subjEntries` leave it out (`winkRow`, `winkEntry`: every admin page, analysis and evaluation tool, the app's hold, replay, last answer and sorry card), `Code.gs` `replayed_` and `answers_`, the Analyses head's count of each subject's answers (`analyses_`, `CORE_.subjCounts`, v105: the refit trigger), the Activity tab, the admin's `ANS.all`, and the app's counts of its queue (the "answers waiting" line, the sign-in note, the tips' seed); `tests/winklog.test.js`. Sent only from `WINK_SHEET` (98), queued until then (rule D4).
- **Every subject's answers, at any rank** (sheet v60, Pierre 2026-11-06: "fish answers are logged from the very first second"; "the sheet must be able to record an answer at any taxonomic level"). Five more `Answers` columns at the end: **Subject** (`core.js` `SUBJECTS` id: `coral`, `fish`; blank in the rows logged before, all coral; an app that sends no `s` is coral), **Rank** (the rank asked: `SUBJECTS` `rank`, coral `genus`, fish `family`, an IMR entry, which two entries may share as a scientific family; a unit's own rank wins, `app.js` `rankOf`: `genus` for a fish scientific genus; later `species`), **Taxon shown** and **Taxon answered** (the names at that rank when a unit's id is not its name, `app.js` `unitName`: `Damselfish` for `t01`, `Chromis` for `g:Chromis`; blank for a coral genus, its own name, and for "I don't know"), **Species shown** (`app.js` `speciesShown` through `UNITS` `species`: the PDF species of a fish photo, `fish-photos.json` `sp` read in `fish.json` species, several joined by `, `; blank for the corals' genus photos). Genus shown, Answered, Choices and a duel's Mode keep the units as the app asks them, the machine's ids (`t01`, `g:Chromis`: `logAnswers_` accepts digits and `:` after a first letter). The app's log entry carries `s`, `rk`, `tn`, `ta`, `sp` (`quiz.js` `answer`). `readAnswers` rows carry the five at indexes 17-21 (`core.js` `LOG_SUBJ` 17, the same index as the tab's column R; the subject filled with `coral` and the rank with the subject's in the rows logged before). **Only the replayed subject feeds what is computed** (`SUBJECTS` `replay`: the corals): `Code.gs` `answerRows_` keeps its rows only, so `stats_` (photo Elo, genus offsets `gd`, photo memory `mem`, confusions, trusted players), `hold_`, `replay_`, `replayedAll_` and the admin player list (`users_`) never see a fish row; every reader of one subject's answers keeps its rows with `core.js` `subjRows` (`admin.js` `ANS.rows`, with every subject's in `ANS.all`; `tools/analyses.js`, `abilityeval.js` and the tools that read its `normalise`, `memreplay.js`, `realcheck.js`, `rtprep.js`, `fallbackeval.js`); the app's own readers of its queue use `subjEntries` (`holdAdopt`, `replayAdopt`, the reset check in `mergeSaved`, `signIn`'s anonymous base, `quiz.js` `ANONB`, `model.js` `lastAt`, `progress.js` `sorryNotice`). The Activity QUERY counts every subject's answers (activity, not a statistic). A tab with fewer columns reads them blank (`logRows_`) until the next answer adds them (`logAnswers_` inserts the columns before its header check). **Rule D4**: an older script would file a fish answer as a coral one, so the answers of a subject the sheet does not replay are sent only once the script reports v ≥ `LOG_SHEET` (60; `core.js` `logOk`, `sync.js` `syncOnce`, `sendAnon`, `anonSoon`, `syncPull`) and wait in the queue until then; the corals' are always sent.
- **"Did you know?" tips** (the owner, 2026-11-04: "for players we detect who never, or very very rarely, use photo enlargement, show a 'Did you know?' pop-up [...] target players without them knowing: those who never used a feature, or once or twice maybe by accident, to remind them it exists. Once a player knows and has used it several times, how often they use it is their preference"). A small generic system, one tip built so far; the candidates are in ROADMAP "Did you know? tips". **Registry and rules** in `tips.js` (pure, no DOM, tier 2 before `quiz.js`, `tests/tips.test.js`): `TIPS` holds each tip's `id`, `ctx` (the games whose break may show it), `target(u)` (who it is for, on the usage counters), `from` (the questions answered from which it can first show), `now(b)` (its preferred moment at a break: b = {ctx, miss, zm}), `wait` (questions it waits for that moment before any break will do), `max` showings ever and `every` (questions between two), its card (`kick`, `title(o)`, `body(o)`, o = {mouse, subj}, subj the `core.js` `SUBJECTS` record on screen: the text follows the subject, whose fields hold the words, never a test on its id (rule S1); the zoom tip reads `zoom`: corallites for coral players, markings and fins for fish players) and `act` (its "Show me", `quiz.js` `TIP_ACT`). `tipDue(state, b)` picks the tip of a break; `TIPR`: at most one tip a visit, never in its first `FLOOR` (10) answers; a visit = answers less than `GAP` (30 min, as `model.js` `SESSION.GAP`) apart, a reload is no new visit. **State, per device** (`reefid-tips`, outside the `reefid-sync-*` progress keys, so not synced and kept on sign-out, like `reefid-zoomhint`): `u` the usage counters (`q` game questions answered here: photo quiz, custom quiz, duels; `z` quiz photos the player enlarged here, on a question or its mix-up montage, `quiz.js` `tipZoomed`; a tip's own Show me is not counted), seeded once from the answers the device still holds (`LOG0` / `SYNC.log`: their count and `zm` flags; a signed-in device holds only its unsent answers); `s` per tip the `u.q` of each showing; `v` the visit {t, n, tip}. Per device on purpose: a tip is about how the person uses this device, the progress format and `core.js` stay untouched, and a tip shown on two devices is no harm. **The break at Next** (`quiz.js` `breakArm`): between an answer and the next question, at most one card. A card that wants the break arms Next unless another already did (first come, first served; `answer()` arms the one-tap question first, `pulseArm`, then the tips, `tipAfter`: when the question is due, the tip waits for a later break); at the tap the card checks again and either shows (its card then decides whether the next question follows) or lets Next go on. A tip arms it only when due and within the prompt spacing (`promptOk`: never with a card, the tour or an achievement toast up, `PROMPT.GAP` answers from the sign-in card, the one-tap question and the offers; showing one calls `promptMark`, so the next prompt waits its turn too), never with the mastery toast due; at the tap it checks again that it is still due, that nothing is on screen and that the next question planned (`NEXTQ`) brings no genus card (never two cards back to back). **The zoom tip** (`id` `zoom`): for a player with 20 questions or more here and at most 2 photos enlarged ever (3 = never again); its moment, the Next right after a miss on a photo they did not enlarge before answering (`zm` 0), else any break from 20 questions later (40 for the first); twice at most, the second 100 questions after the first. Card (`tipShow`, `cardShow` kind `tip`, 💡): "Did you know?", "Tap a photo to see it bigger" ("Click" with a mouse), the photo just answered small with its enlarge mark ringed, one line, **Show me** (closes the card and opens the enlarged view of the photo just answered through the stage's own enlarge button, so with the answer shown, after a mix-up the photo shown then the one of the genus answered; the player is back on the feedback when it closes, Next goes on) and **Got it** (the next question). Anti-reflex as the one-tap question: its buttons take no tap for `TIP.WAIT` (600 ms), the focus is on the title (Enter presses nothing), a tap outside does nothing, Escape = Got it. **Logged** with the one-tap questions: `ev('pulse', {r, src: 'tip:<id>', m, n})`, r = `shown`, then `show` (Show me), `ok` (Got it, Escape) or `none` (another page opened with it up). The sheet's `events_` keeps only the kinds it knows (`EVK`: err, photo, sync, pulse, load; a `tip` kind would be dropped), and a pulse row keeps `r` in Message and `src` in Where as sent: no sheet or script change. The admin (To do › Errors, `#inbox/todo/errors`) keeps the `tip:` rows out of the one-tap tables and counts them apart (`tipsView`: per tip, devices, Shown, Show me, Got it, Left). At most 2 events a showing, 2 showings ever per tip and device. `tests/pages.smoke.js` `tipsCheck` (390 and 1280 px).
- **The Duel tab's relief card** (Pierre, 2026-11-04; `quiz.js` `DUELRUN`, `reliefNote`, `reliefDue`, `reliefArm`, `reliefShow`; docs/LEARNING.md "The Duel tab when the hold sets in"): the first time in a duel run (`#practice/duels` or `#practice/duels/A/B`, from the moment the page is opened, `app.js` `route` passing `quizDuel` whether it was just opened, until it is left; Next and Keep drilling stay in it) an answer takes the opening hold's sum (`HOLDS`, `holdStep`) from below `INTRO.HOLD` to it or more, the next break at Next (`breakArm`, armed before the one-tap question, the sign-in card and the tips) shows a card (`cardShow` kind `tip`, `.reliefcard`): "Tough pair today", "Take a step back?", one line, **Mix in other genera** (the main button, `#practice/quiz`), **Compare them** (`#learn/compare/A/B`, the duel's two genera), a quiet **Keep drilling** (`.linkish.reliefx`, the next duel; Escape too). **The way back** (Pierre, 2026-11-04: Compare them must not throw the player out of the duel for good): Compare them keeps `DUELBACK` {h: the duel's hash, cmp: the comparison's, run, d: the duel page's history depth}; the comparison (`learn.js` `viewCompare`, `duelBackFor`) then shows **Back to the duel**, in view however far the page scrolls and in place of Drill this pair: on phones and tablets a floating main button at the bottom centre (`.cmpfab`, the page ending with room for it, `.cmpfabpad`), from 1024 px a light band sticky under the page bar, "Duel paused: A or B" and the button (`.duelband`, its offset from the bar's height in `--ctx-h`; DESIGN.md); Pierre, 2026-11-04: inline at the top and the end, it was not seen; `duelBack`: `history.back()` when the entry before is the duel's, else the duel's address), and the duel opened from it (that button or the browser's Back: `route` passes `quizDuel` the page left) continues the same run (its card already shown), logged `back`. Any other page drops `DUELBACK` (`route`, `duelBackKeep`), so a comparison opened any other way is unchanged. At most once a run; gated like the tips (`promptOk` at arming and at the tap, `promptMark` when shown, never right before a genus card, `nextCards`); a break it cannot take passes it to the next one of the run while the hold is on. Anti-reflex as the tips (`TIP.WAIT`, focus on the title, no tap outside). Logged with the tips: `ev('pulse', {r, src: 'tip:duelhold', m, n})`, r `shown`, then `mix`, `cmp`, `keep` or `none`, and `back` on the return from the comparison; no sheet change; admin `tipsView` adds Compare, Mix in, Keep drilling and Back to the duel columns when a row has them. In memory only (a reload starts a new run). `tests/pages.smoke.js` `duelReliefCheck`, `tests/pace.test.js`.
- **The custom quiz's reminder of the units waiting in the Quiz** (Pierre, 2026-11-12: a player drilled 417 answers over two days in the custom quiz, on purpose, with 13 genera still not open: a reminder, "qu'on fait qu'une fois, et quand quelqu'un passe beaucoup de temps dans le custom quiz"; `quiz.js` `LEFTN`, `leftStep`, `leftNote`, `leftDue`, `leftCount`, `leftWords`, `leftArm`, `leftShow`; docs/LEARNING.md "Custom quiz"). The custom quiz opens no unit (rule L4). Each answer counts (`leftNote`): a custom-quiz answer adds one, a photo-quiz answer starts the count over, a Duel-tab answer does neither, on this device, per subject (`reefid-<dev>custom-left` {n, shown}: `reefid-custom-left`, `reefid-fish-custom-left`; the player's, removed at sign-out and account deletion with the other per-subject records, `sync.js` `signOutClear`, rule D7). Once the count reaches `LEFTN.AT` (100) while units of the path may still open (`leftCount`: the units in play, `pool()`, not met, `model.js` `introduced`, that the gates allow, `mayOpen`, as the photo quiz's opening reads them; bonus genera only when the player turned them on) and the opening hold is off (`model.js` `introHold`: while it is on, the Quiz opens nothing new for a while, so the card waits for it to end; Pierre, 2026-11-12, on #850), the next break at Next (`breakArm`, armed after the relief card and before the sign-in card, the one-tap question and the tips) shows a card (`cardShow` kind `tip`, `.leftcard`): "New genera", "13 new genera are waiting in the Quiz" (the subject's `one` / `many`: "1 new family is waiting…"), one line, **Go to the Quiz** (the main button, `#practice/quiz`) and **Keep drilling** (the next question; Escape too). Once ever per subject: marked when shown on this device (`shown`) and, signed in, for the account (`core.js` `TOLD` token `left:<subject>`, `sync.js` `toldAdd` / `toldHas`, no new sync code); signed in, it waits for the visit's first sync to have merged the account's notices (`noticesReady`, Codex review #850). Nothing to open: no card, the count goes on. Gated like the relief card (`promptOk` at arming and at the tap, `promptMark` when shown, never right before a genus card, `nextCards`, nothing on screen); anti-reflex as the tips (`TIP.WAIT`, focus on the title, no tap outside). Logged with the tips: `ev('pulse', {r, src: 'tip:customleft', m, n})`, r `shown`, then `quiz`, `keep` or `none`; admin `tipsView` adds a To the Quiz column when a row has one. `tests/custom.test.js`, `tests/pages.smoke.js` `customLeftCheck` (`SMOKE_ONLY=tips`).
- **Answer time and enlargement** (sheet v23, Pierre 2026-11-02: "a right answer that needed a long look or an enlarged photo says the photo was harder"; measure first, no model change): each answer of the photo quiz and the duels carries `rt`, the seconds from the moment the question's first photo is in (its load event, or at once when already loaded) to the click on a choice, to 0.1 s, at most 600, the clock started again when a first-meeting card or the open-all offer over the question closes and stopped while the app is hidden (`QCLOCK` in `quiz.js`); none when the photo never loaded; and `zm`, 1 when a photo of the question was enlarged before answering. Sent with every answer, signed in or not; v23 logs them in Seconds (clamped 0-600, rounded to 0.1) and Enlarged (0/1; blank when not sent: older app, or rows before v23); `readAnswers` rows carry them at indexes 12 and 13 (null when blank), after the anonymous flag (11). The admin player journey shows the player's median time and share of answers with a photo enlarged. Since rules version 4 (2026-11-04, Pierre; sheet v32) the seconds weigh a right answer's ability steps (genus and global level) by clamp(1 − 1.5 z, 0.5, 1.5), z its log seconds against the player's own median over their earlier timed answers (`core.js` `RTIME`, `rtWeight`, `rtAdd`; typed answers, answers without seconds and the first 10 timed answers: weight 1; misses unchanged), on in the app only once the script reports v ≥ `RT_SHEET` (32; `sync.js` `sheetV`, the last reply's `v` kept in `reefid-sheet-v`; `model.js` `rtOn`; the device's own answers replayed on top of an adopted replay follow that reply's `v`, `replayAdopt`), and replayed from the Seconds column by v32 (`rowOf_` index 12; docs/LEARNING.md "Response time"). The player's times are kept in the global level's record, `P['~level'].rt` = {tenths of a second: answers} (exact median, a few hundred keys at most), recorded whatever the script version, added up by `mergeProgress` and `mergeUsers`, rebuilt by the replay (`replayedOf`, `replayBase` take it with the level's answer count). The enlarged mark is still logged only. An older script ignores the two fields. Enlarged rates before and after the enlarge mark and its one-time hint (2026-11-03, "Enlarging a photo made visible" above) are not comparable as they stand.
- **Opening hold synced** (sheet v23, Pierre 2026-11-02: it follows a signed-in player from one device to another and is rebuilt from the answers given before it existed): the `GET ?name=&pin=` reply carries `hold` (and `holdIds`, the ids of the player's last 500 answers counted, and from v28 of every answer from the request's `pt` on, as the replay's `ids`, so the app never adds again the answers of a send whose reply was lost, up to the 2,000 a queue holds: Codex reviews #370, #399; the reply to each successful send carries both too, computed over every answer then logged in time order, so a device that sent answers older than another device's takes the right value at once), the opening hold's CUSUM (`model.js` `holdStep`, docs/LEARNING.md "Held while the player falls behind") computed by `Code.gs` `hold_` over the player's logged answers in time order since their last progress reset (`reefid-sync-coral-reset` in the saved data), leaving out the gentle restart's answers (mode `|w`, logged on 2026-11-02 only; the comeback's, `|c`, count with their own logged `e`); `HOLD.SLACK` in `Code.gs` is `INTRO.SLACK` in `model.js` (`tests/sync.test.js` replays the app's own `holdStep` on the same answers). Cached per player (`hold:<name>`) until answers are logged or moved for that name. The app (`sync.js` `mergeSaved` → `model.js` `holdSync`) adds its answers the sheet has not confirmed yet (`SYNC.log`, `|w` ones and those before the reset left out) and takes that as its `reefid-hold`: the same sum over the same answers on every device, so right answers on one device pay back a hold started on another; a reset made on this device and not sent yet ignores the reply's `hold`. Players not signed in, and any app against an older script (no `hold`), keep the device's hold only.
- Version history: v2 Answers/Activity tabs; v131 (2026-11-14) The embedded core.js lists Analyses › Difficulty › Pairs and Analyses › Learning › Answer log among the fish admin's pages (SUBJECTS fish admin pages), with its word for the observations of a question's photos (admin obs): fish admin at parity with the corals (Pierre, 2026-11-08: "compléter la partie admin des poissons pour qu'il y ait toutes les fonctionnalités existantes de la partie coraux"). Nothing the script computes changes; v130 (2026-11-14) A small `Timings` tab measures how the sheet copes as the answer log grows (the storage study of 2026-11-14, step 1: "log ms and rows for each full read and for each save, sampled, in a small tab with no player data"; Pierre, 2026-11-14, Q352: the answer log moves to Cloudflare D1 only when a measured threshold is crossed): one row per measured operation (Time, Op, Ms, Rows, Cols, Version, Weight), never a name, code, device, answer id or answer content (rules D2, D7). Measured: the whole-tab reads of `Answers` (`logRows_`) and of its Id column (`freshAnswers_`, `logAnswers_`), the photo stats' build (`stats_`), every player's saved data (`datas_`), the leaderboard's replays (`boardWarm_`), and as a whole a completed save, an anonymous send that logged, a load, a public stats request, the admin's readAnswers and each run of the 15-minute trigger. Sampled per request (`TIM.EVERY` 50; trigger runs `TIM.EVERY_TRIGGER` 16) at that weight, plus every operation slower than `TIM.SLOW` 5 s (a trigger run 60 s) at weight 1; kept in memory and written at the end of the request (`timEnd_`, from `doGet` / `doPost` / `autoUpdate`'s finally, after every lock is released), one `appendRow` a row inside try/catch, so a failure never touches a reply. The trigger keeps the tab bounded (`timTrim_`): past `TIM.MAX` 4,000 rows the oldest rows are deleted, back to 3,000 (`TIM.KEEP`), the kept rows as logged; past twice that nothing more is written. Read with `tools/timings.js`. Nothing a player sees changes; v129 (2026-11-14) The embedded `core.js` names the fish admin's Photos › Families page `photos/tree` (`SUBJECTS` fish `admin` `pages`): the tab became Tree, the key's classification as a tree of any depth (Pierre, 2026-11-13, Q340); nothing the script computes changes; v128 (2026-11-09) Other species in view, split in two (Pierre, 2026-11-09, Q323): the embedded core.js gains `FISH_COUNT`, `fishCount` and `fishOther` (a fish photo's count tag `o`, other species with no risk of confusion, may be in play; `k`, other species one could take for the subject, and the legacy `m` not sorted yet never are, rule P3) and its `fishIn` and `fishPhotos` read them; the script itself uses none of them (the replay and the leaderboards are unchanged), so nothing a player sees from the sheet moves; v127 (2026-11-09) The log is the only reference for progress (rule D9; Pierre, 2026-11-09, Q322, Q330): the full replay's record of a unit and its answer count win over a player's saved copy also when the copy holds more answers there (counted twice: a save whose reply was lost, then another device's save, #824), and a unit the log has no answer on loses the answers the copy counts on it, through `core.js` `replayedOf` and `replayBase`, so the leaderboard too (`board_`, `boardWarm_`, rule D8); the fish replay is no longer floored against the saved bars (`replayFloor`, `floorRef_` gone; a record kept as `{P, F}` before is still read, `rplRec_`): a fish bar may go down. Rules version 10 (corals) and fish rules version 2, with no card (Q330); v126 (2026-11-09) The shared core reads a typed fish name more plainly and through the name index, with no change to the sheet's own work (Pierre, 2026-11-09, the names study's plain fixes, Q319 and Q320): a family's name and its other number are one name (`core.js` `typeForms`), a name typed exactly is never read as a slip of another, a name several families share is all of them (`typeMatchIn` `shared`), and `fishNameIndex` lifts the names below a family (its species' listed and scientific names, its genera's names, the kind words of `fish.json` `vn`) to that family, each right only on a photo it designates (Q333: `typeFits`), with Pierre's exceptions (`curation.json` `fish` `names`); the sheet never reads typed text, so nothing it computes changes; v125 (2026-11-09) The admin's 🌱 added on its own, the account's name unchanged (replaces the v123 rename, userSeed, now removed): the account the admin page is signed in to when it reads the answer log with a valid admin token (answers_, its name and code sent as aname / apin, the code checked) is the admin's account, one at most, kept as its code hash in the script's properties (ADMIN_ACCT; another account signed in there replaces it); its rows on every leaderboard carry a 4th field 1 (board_) and its load reply admin: 1 (load_), which the app shows as a 🌱 after the name, outside the name's text; a delete, a merge (its from) and an archive of that account clear it (rule D7); new names holding the seedling or a look-alike stay refused (Pierre, 2026-11-09, Q328 corrected: "the emoji isn't part of my username", added automatically with no step for him); v122 (2026-11-09) the shared core reads fish-game.json in its short form (Pierre, 2026-11-09: an iNaturalist observation by its id and the original photo by its extension instead of full addresses, tools/fish_short.py): `fishGameRead` gives every record its addresses back before the fish photo pools are built (`POOLS` entries); the pools are the same as from the file before; v121 (2026-11-09) the shared core leaves the coral skeleton photos out of every photo list: applyCuration drops a photo marked `skel` (data.json, written by `tools/apply_skeletons.py` from `judging/skeletons.json`) wherever it is, as it drops an excluded one (rule P6, Pierre, 2026-11-09: coral skeletons are never shown, the features cannot be seen properly); nothing the script computes changes; v120 (2026-11-09) the shared core reads a typed answer faster, with the same answers: `typeDist` keeps three rows of its matrix instead of the whole one, `typeMatchIn` skips a name whose length is further from the text's than its spelling tolerance (the distance is at least that difference), and `typeElse` reads its lists' names once per norm (performance pass asked by Pierre, 2026-11-09: "what can we speed up in the app"); an account that already has such a name loads and saves as before; and the maps by player that still had a prototype have none (the player list's numbers, the beta groups and their changes, the ids of `readAnswers` and the live view, the achievements' shares, the replayed progress of the list; `core.js` `trustOf`, `eloReplay`, `playSplit`, `exposureOf`), so such a player is a player like any other there and nothing is added to `Object.prototype` (Pierre, 2026-11-09: these names broke the maps by player; the front door refuses them, nobody locked out); v118 (2026-11-09) Every leaderboard follows the rules: a player whose saved copy applied an older rules version of a subject (corals `RVS`, fish `FISH_RVS`, any subject's `rvsOf`) is ranked on that subject's board by the progress and level their app will hold once it adopts the replay of their log (core.js `replayAdopted`, the record `sync.js` `replayAdoptS` adopts: `replayedOf` over the saved copy, with the floor of a subject replayed with one), from the record its Replayed tab keeps for today's rules, row count and reset (`boardRpl_`); a player up to date, or one with no record yet, by the saved copy as before; the board only reads (nothing fetched, no lock) and stays cached 5 minutes. The 15-minute trigger, once up to date, makes those records ahead for every listed player behind the rules (`boardWarm_`: the replay of `replay_`, `rplMake_`, outside the script lock, all written under one short lock, each only if the account (same name and code, listed), its saved data, its answers of that subject since its reset and its Replayed row are, read again under the lock, what it was made from (Codex review #810, rule D7: an account deleted meanwhile never gets its row back), at most `BOARD_WARM_MS` of new replays a run), the same record the player's next sync reuses, drops the boards that changed from the cache and counts them in the Deploy log; one player's failure never stops the others' or the board, and every map keyed by a player name, `core.js` `replayProgress`'s too, has no prototype (Codex review #810: a player named `constructor`) (Pierre, 2026-11-09, rule D8: "whenever progress is recalculated, the leaderboard must be recalculated automatically, even if people do not reconnect"); v117 (2026-11-09) The fish replay stored and sent at last: a tab that exists but is empty (made by hand, or by anything but `sheet_`) gets its header as a new tab does, and `find_` reads no name in an empty tab instead of throwing; the sheet's "Replayed Fish" tab stood empty without its header, so every fish replay failed in `replay_` before it was stored or sent (`replays_` leaves a failed subject out) and no fish player was replayed while their apps asked at every sync (Pierre, 2026-11-08, Q298, Q310: every fish player's progress replayed from the answer log, today's bar as a floor; production bug, 2026-11-09). And `core.js` `replayProgress` keys its players without a prototype, so a player named like an Object property (`constructor`, `__proto__`) is replayed like any other (Codex review #810); v116 (2026-11-09) Lighter saves on slow lines: a light save (the app's `light`, wrapped as `{z: base64 gzip}` or `{lt}` so an older script finds no name and writes nothing; a body it cannot unpack answers `zip`) sends only what changed since the copy it holds at its revision (`dz`, `core.js` `dataDelta`) with the checksum of its whole data (`sum`, `dataSum`), rebuilt here on the saved copy and kept only if the checksums agree (`lightData_`, `dataPatch`; else `delta`, before anything is logged), or its whole data under the new key names, the old ones added here (`withLegacy`); its reply says the checksum of the copy saved (`sum`); a light save against another revision is answered `stale` before anything is counted or logged, with the load of the copy there (`load_`, read once the lock is released), so the app merges and saves again without a load of its own; a load says the revision of the copy the device holds (`have`) and an unchanged copy comes back `same: 1` without it; a light request's hold names the answers from `pt` only (`hold_`: not the last 500) and its loads serve the copy under the new key names only; their own progress, level and leaderboard place stay, and a delete, merge or archive moves or drops the row (sxMove_, rule D7); and an answer whose photos are game photos of another unit or subject and none of its own (photoCheck_ over pools_, a coral photo named after its genus counting as its own, photos in no pool unsure) rates no photo in stats_ (skip counts in readStats), is logged as every answer, never refused, and the first such send of each 6-hour window per sender is written to the Alerts tab under its admin id (photoAlert_, against the cached pools only), a fish answer at the genus or species level checked against its family's photos and an old entry id as the family it became (unitUp_, core.js renamedId); only an answer that rates gives a photo its genus offset, so a left-out or flagged one moves no photo difficulty; the game pools are cached in parts (pool2<subject>, cacheBigPut_: the fish pool is over the cache's 100 KB a value) and stats_ reads every subject's; (Pierre's wording: no explanation); sign-out (`signOutClear`) drops the reports queued under an account the sheet removed (not merged) or archived. archiving moves the record's name mentions in Events and Alerts to the archive name (`redact_`), and a report carries the generation it was queued under (`racct`, `sync.js` `ra`): another generation, or a name with no account left, logs it with no reporter, Verified 0 (Codex review #603); a save carries the device's generation (`acct`), checked under the lock before anything is written (no account left or another generation: error `archived`), and page summaries too (`eacct`: another generation files them under no player); a save's reply carries the generation (`acct`, kept by the creating device at once), and a report's generation is checked whatever its code; v57 (2026-11-06) a player's load reply carries `reps`, the ids of their own verified reports (`repsOf_`, the last 50; `analysis.js` per-player results and counts of players, AV 5; `admin-analyses.js` `anLive` hides the per-player lines of an older stored report). `analysesGet` head says `stale` when an account was removed after the stored report (`rosterAt_`), and `tools/analyses.js` refits at once (`plan` why `removed`). A report sent with the name and code of a removed account goes under the name it joined (merged, Verified 1) or under no name (deleted, Verified 0).
